




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

dīghanikāyai


sīlakkhandhavaggaṭṭhakathā


ganthārambhakathā

karuṇāsītalahadayaṃ, pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatamaṃ|

sanarāmaralaukagaruṃ, vandai sugataṃ gativimuttaṃ||

buddhaupi buddhabhāvaṃ, bhāvaitvā caiva sacchikatvā ca|

yaṃ upagatau gatamalaṃ, vandai tamanuttaraṃ dhammaṃ||

sugatassa aurasānaṃ, puttānaṃ mārasainamathanānaṃ|

aṭṭhan‍nampi samūhaṃ, sirasā vandai ariyasaṅghaṃ||

iti  mai pasan‍namatinau, ratanattayavandanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ|

yaṃ suvihatantarāyau, hutvā tassānubhāvaina||

dīghassa dīghasuttaṅkitassa, nipuṇassa āgamavarassa|

buddhānubuddhasaṃvaṇṇitassa, saddhāvahaguṇassa||

atthappakāsanatthaṃ, aṭṭhakathā āditau vasisataihi|

pañ‍cahi yā saṅgītā, anusaṅgītā ca pacchāpi||

sīhaḷadīpaṃ pana ābhatātha, vasinā mahāmahindaina|

ṭhapitā sīhaḷabhāsāya, dīpavāsīnamatthāya||

apanaitvāna  tatauhaṃ, sīhaḷabhāsaṃ manauramaṃ bhāsaṃ|

tantinayānucchavikaṃ, āraupaintau vigatadausaṃ||

samayaṃ avilaumaintau, thairānaṃ thairavaṃsapadīpānaṃ|

sunipuṇavinicchayānaṃ, mahāvihārai nivāsīnaṃ||

hitvā punappunāgatamatthaṃ, atthaṃ pakāsayissāmi|

sujanassa ca tuṭṭhatthaṃ, ciraṭṭhitatthañ‍ca dhammassa||

sīlakathā dhutadhammā, kammaṭṭhānāni caiva sabbāni|

cariyāvidhānasahitau, jhānasamāpattivitthārau||

sabbā ca abhiñ‍ñāyau, pañ‍ñāsaṅkalananicchayau caiva|

khandhadhātāyatanindriyāni, ariyāni caiva cattāri||

sac‍cāni  pac‍cayākāradaisanā, suparisuddhanipuṇanayā|

avimuttatantimaggā, vipassanā bhāvanā caiva||

iti  pana sabbaṃ yasmā, visuddhimaggai mayā suparisuddhaṃ|

vuttaṃ tasmā bhiyyau, na taṃ idha vicārayissāmi||

‘‘majjhai visuddhimaggau, aisa catun‍nampi āgamānañhi|

ṭhatvā pakāsayissati, tattha yathā bhāsitaṃ atthaṃ’’||

ic‍caiva katau tasmā, tampi gahaitvāna saddhimaitāya|

aṭṭhakathāya vijānatha, dīghāgamanissitaṃ atthanti||

nidānakathā

tattha dīghāgamau nāma sīlakkhandhavaggau, mahāvaggau, pāthikavaggauti vaggatau tivaggau hauti; suttatau catuttiṃsasuttasaṅgahau| tassa vaggaisu sīlakkhandhavaggau ādi, suttaisu brahmajālaṃ| brahmajālassāpi ‘‘aivaṃ  mai suta’’ntiādikaṃ āyasmatā ānandaina paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālai vuttaṃ nidānamādi|

paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikathā

paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti nāma caisā kiñ‍cāpi vinayapiṭakai tantimārūḷhā, nidānakausal‍latthaṃ pana idhāpi aivaṃ vaiditabbā| dhammacak‍kappavattanañhi ādiṃ katvā yāva subhaddaparibbājakavinayanā katabuddhakic‍cai, kusinārāyaṃ upavattanai mal‍lānaṃ sālavanai yamakasālānamantarai visākhapuṇṇamadivasai pac‍cūsasamayai anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbutai bhagavati laukanāthai, bhagavatau dhātubhājanadivasai san‍nipatitānaṃ sattan‍naṃ bhikkhusatasahassānaṃ saṅghatthairau āyasmā mahākassapau sattāhaparinibbutai bhagavati subhaddaina vuḍḍhapabbajitaina – ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, mā saucittha, mā paridaivittha, sumuttā mayaṃ taina mahāsamaṇaina, upaddutā ca hauma – ‘idaṃ vau kappati, idaṃ vau na kappatī’ti, idāni pana mayaṃ yaṃ icchissāma, taṃ karissāma, yaṃ na icchissāma na taṃ karissāmā’’ti (cūḷava॰ 437) vuttavacanamanussarantau, īdisassa ca saṅghasan‍nipātassa puna dul‍labhabhāvaṃ mañ‍ñamānau, ‘‘ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ pāpabhikkhū ‘atītasatthukaṃ pāvacana’nti mañ‍ñamānā  pakkhaṃ labhitvā nacirassaiva saddhammaṃ antaradhāpaiyyuṃ, yāva ca dhammavinayau tiṭṭhati, tāva anatītasatthukamaiva pāvacanaṃ hauti| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘yau vau, ānanda, mayā dhammau ca vinayau ca daisitau pañ‍ñattau, sau vau mamac‍cayaina satthā’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.216)|

‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyaiyyaṃ, yathayidaṃ sāsanaṃ addhaniyaṃ assa ciraṭṭhitikaṃ’|

yañ‍cāhaṃ bhagavatā –

‘dhāraissasi pana mai tvaṃ, kassapa, sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni nibbasanānī’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.154) vatvā cīvarai sādhāraṇaparibhaugaina|

‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi; kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva, ākaṅkhati vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.152)|

aivamādinā nayaina navānupubbavihārachaḷabhiñ‍ñāppabhaidai uttarimanussadhammai attanā samasamaṭṭhapanaina ca anuggahitau, tathā ākāsai pāṇiṃ cālaitvā alaggacittatāya caiva candaupamapaṭipadāya ca pasaṃsitau, tassa kimañ‍ñaṃ āṇaṇyaṃ bhavissati| nanu maṃ bhagavā rājā viya sakakavacaissariyānuppadānaina attanau kulavaṃsappatiṭṭhāpakaṃ puttaṃ ‘saddhammavaṃsappatiṭṭhāpakau mai ayaṃ bhavissatī’ti, mantvā iminā asādhāraṇaina anuggahaina anuggahaisi, imāya ca uḷārāya pasaṃsāya pasaṃsīti cintayantau dhammavinayasaṅgāyanatthaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhaṃ janaisi| yathāha –

‘‘atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘aikamidāhaṃ, āvusau, samayaṃ  pāvāya kusināraṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihī’’ti (cūḷava॰ 437) sabbaṃ subhaddakaṇḍaṃ vitthāratau vaiditabbaṃ| atthaṃ panassa mahāparinibbānāvasānai āgataṭṭhānaiyaiva kathayissāma|

tatau paraṃ āha –

‘‘handa mayaṃ, āvusau, dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca  saṅgāyāma, purai adhammau dippati, dhammau paṭibāhiyyati; purai avinayau dippati, vinayau paṭibāhiyyati; purai adhammavādinau balavantau haunti, dhammavādinau dubbalā haunti, purai avinayavādinau balavantau haunti, vinayavādinau dubbalā hauntī’’ti (cūḷava॰ 437)|

bhikkhū āhaṃsu – ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, thairau bhikkhū uc‍cinatū’’ti| thairau pana sakalanavaṅgasatthusāsanapariyattidharai puthuj‍janasautāpan‍nasakadāgāmianāgāmi sukkhavipassaka khīṇāsavabhikkhū anaikasatai, anaikasahassai ca  vaj‍jaitvā tipiṭakasabbapariyattippabhaidadharai paṭisambhidāppattai mahānubhāvai yaibhuyyaina bhagavatau aitadaggaṃ āraupitai taivij‍jādibhaidai khīṇāsavabhikkhūyaiva aikūnapañ‍casatai pariggahaisi| yai sandhāya idaṃ vuttaṃ – ‘‘atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau aikainūnāni pañ‍ca arahantasatāni uc‍cinī’’ti (cūḷava॰ 437)|

kissa pana thairau aikainūnamakāsīti? āyasmatau ānandatthairassa aukāsakaraṇatthaṃ| tainahāyasmatā sahāpi, vināpi, na sak‍kā dhammasaṅgītiṃ kātuṃ| sau hāyasmā saikkhau sakaraṇīyau, tasmā sahāpi na sak‍kā| yasmā panassa kiñ‍ci dasabaladaisitaṃ suttagaiyyādikaṃ appac‍cakkhaṃ nāma natthi| yathāha –

‘‘dvāsīti buddhatau gaṇhiṃ, dvai sahassāni bhikkhutau|

caturāsīti sahassāni, yai mai dhammā pavattinau’’ti|| (thairagā॰ 1027)|

tasmā vināpi na sak‍kā|

yadi aivaṃ saikkhaupi samānau dhammasaṅgītiyā bahukārattā thairaina uc‍cinitabbau assa, atha kasmā na uc‍cinitauti? parūpavādavivaj‍janatau| thairau hi āyasmantai ānandai ativiya  vissatthau ahausi, tathā hi naṃ sirasmiṃ palitaisu jātaisupi ‘na vāyaṃ kumārakau mattamañ‍ñāsī’ti, (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.154) kumārakavādaina auvadati| sakyakulappasutau cāyasmā tathāgatassa bhātā cūḷapituputtau| tattha kaici bhikkhū chandāgamanaṃ viya mañ‍ñamānā – ‘‘bahū asaikkhapaṭisambhidāppattai bhikkhū ṭhapaitvā  ānandaṃ saikkhapaṭisambhidāppattaṃ thairau uc‍cinī’’ti upavadaiyyuṃ| taṃ parūpavādaṃ parivaj‍jaintau, ‘ānandaṃ vinā dhammasaṅgītiṃ na sak‍kā kātuṃ, bhikkhūnaṃyaiva naṃ anumatiyā gahaissāmī’ti na uc‍cini|

atha sayamaiva bhikkhū ānandassatthāya thairaṃ yāciṃsu| yathāha –

‘‘bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘ayaṃ, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau kiñ‍cāpi saikkhau abhabbau chandā dausā mauhā bhayā agatiṃ gantuṃ, bahu cānaina bhagavatau santikai dhammau ca vinayau ca pariyattau, taina hi, bhantai, thairau āyasmantampi  ānandaṃ uc‍cinatū’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantampi ānandaṃ uc‍cinī’’ti (cūḷava॰ 437)|

aivaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anumatiyā uc‍cinitaina taināyasmatā saddhiṃ pañ‍cathairasatāni ahaisuṃ|

atha khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kattha nu khau mayaṃ dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyaiyyāmā’’ti? atha khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘rājagahaṃ khau mahāgaucaraṃ pahūtasaināsanaṃ, yaṃnūna mayaṃ rājagahai vassaṃ vasantā dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyaiyyāma, na añ‍ñai bhikkhū rājagahai vassaṃ upagacchaiyyu’’nti (cūḷava॰ 437)|

kasmā pana naisaṃ aitadahausi? ‘‘idaṃ pana amhākaṃ thāvarakammaṃ, kauci visabhāgapuggalau saṅghamajjhaṃ pavisitvā uk‍kauṭaiyyā’’ti| athāyasmā mahākassapau ñattidutiyaina kammaina sāvaisi –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, āvusau saṅghau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau imāni pañ‍ca bhikkhusatāni samman‍naiyya rājagahai vassaṃ vasantāni dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyituṃ, na añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi rājagahai vassaṃ vasitabba’’nti| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau saṅghau, saṅghau imāni pañ‍cabhikkhusatāni samman‍na’’ti ‘rājagahai vassaṃ vasantāni dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyituṃ, na añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi rājagahai vassaṃ vasitabbanti| yassāyasmatau khamati imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ  bhikkhusatānaṃ sammuti’ rājagahai vassaṃ vasantānaṃ dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyituṃ, na añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi rājagahai vassaṃ vasitabbanti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatāni saṅghaina imāni pañ‍cabhikkhusatāni rājagahai vassaṃ vasantāni dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyituṃ, na añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi rājagahai vassaṃ vasitabbanti, khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti (cūḷava॰ 438)|

ayaṃ  pana kammavācā tathāgatassa parinibbānatau aikavīsatimai divasai katā| bhagavā hi visākhapuṇṇamāyaṃ pac‍cūsasamayai parinibbutau, athassa sattāhaṃ suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sarīraṃ gandhamālādīhi pūjayiṃsu| aivaṃ sattāhaṃ sādhukīḷanadivasā nāma ahaisuṃ| tatau sattāhaṃ citakāya agginā jhāyi, sattāhaṃ sattipañ‍jaraṃ katvā sandhāgārasālāyaṃ dhātupūjaṃ kariṃsūti, aikavīsati divasā gatā| jaiṭṭhamūlasuk‍kapakkhapañ‍camiyaṃyaiva dhātuyau bhājayiṃsu| aitasmiṃ dhātubhājanadivasai san‍nipatitassa mahābhikkhusaṅghassa subhaddaina vuḍḍhapabbajitaina kataṃ anācāraṃ āraucaitvā vuttanayainaiva ca bhikkhū uc‍cinitvā ayaṃ kammavācā katā|

imañ‍ca pana kammavācaṃ katvā thairau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau, idāni tumhākaṃ cattālīsa divasā aukāsau katau, tatau paraṃ ‘ayaṃ nāma nau palibaudhau atthī’ti, vattuṃ na labbhā, tasmā aitthantarai yassa raugapalibaudhau vā ācariyupajjhāyapalibaudhau vā mātāpitupalibaudhau vā atthi, pattaṃ vā pana pacitabbaṃ, cīvaraṃ vā kātabbaṃ, sau taṃ palibaudhaṃ chinditvā taṃ karaṇīyaṃ karautū’’ti|

aivañ‍ca pana vatvā thairau attanau pañ‍casatāya parisāya parivutau rājagahaṃ gatau| añ‍ñaipi mahāthairā attanau attanau parivārai gahaitvā saukasal‍lasamappitaṃ mahājanaṃ assāsaitukāmā taṃ taṃ disaṃ pak‍kantā| puṇṇatthairau pana sattasatabhikkhuparivārau ‘tathāgatassa parinibbānaṭṭhānaṃ āgatāgataṃ mahājanaṃ assāsaissāmī’ti kusinārāyaṃyaiva aṭṭhāsi|

āyasmā ānandau yathā pubbai aparinibbutassa, aivaṃ parinibbutassāpi bhagavatau sayamaiva  pattacīvaramādāya pañ‍cahi bhikkhusataihi saddhiṃ yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| gacchatau gacchatau panassa parivārā bhikkhū gaṇanapathaṃ vītivattā| taināyasmatā gatagataṭṭhānai mahāparidaivau ahausi| anupubbaina pana sāvatthimanuppattai thairai sāvatthivāsinau manussā ‘‘thairau kira āgatau’’ti sutvā gandhamālādihatthā pac‍cuggantvā – ‘‘bhantai, ānanda, pubbai bhagavatā saddhiṃ āgacchatha, aj‍ja kuhiṃ bhagavantaṃ  ṭhapaitvā āgatatthā’’tiādīni vadamānā paraudiṃsu| buddhassa bhagavatau parinibbānadivasai viya mahāparidaivau ahausi|

tatra sudaṃ āyasmā ānandau anic‍catādipaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyākathāya taṃ mahājanaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitvā jaitavanaṃ pavisitvā dasabalaina vasitagandhakuṭiṃ vanditvā dvāraṃ vivaritvā mañ‍capīṭhaṃ nīharitvā papphauṭaitvā gandhakuṭiṃ sammaj‍jitvā milātamālākacavaraṃ chaḍḍaitvā mañ‍capīṭhaṃ atiharitvā puna yathāṭhānai ṭhapaitvā bhagavatau ṭhitakālai karaṇīyaṃ vattaṃ sabbamakāsi| kurumānau ca nhānakauṭṭhakasammaj‍janaudakupaṭṭhāpanādikālaisu gandhakuṭiṃ vanditvā – ‘‘nanu bhagavā, ayaṃ tumhākaṃ nhānakālau, ayaṃ dhammadaisanākālau, ayaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ auvādadānakālau, ayaṃ sīhasaiyyakappanakālau, ayaṃ mukhadhauvanakālau’’tiādinā nayaina paridaivamānauva akāsi, yathā taṃ bhagavatau guṇagaṇāmatarasañ‍ñutāya patiṭṭhitapaimau caiva akhīṇāsavau ca anaikaisu ca jātisatasahassaisu añ‍ñamañ‍ñassūpakārasañ‍janitacittamaddavau| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā – ‘‘bhantai, ānanda, tumhai aivaṃ paridaivamānā kathaṃ añ‍ñai assāsaissathā’’ti saṃvaijaisi| sau tassā vacanaina saṃviggahadayau santhambhitvā tathāgatassa parinibbānatau pabhuti ṭhānanisaj‍jabahulattā ussan‍nadhātukaṃ kāyaṃ samassāsaituṃ dutiyadivasai khīraviraicanaṃ pivitvā vihāraiyaiva nisīdi| yaṃ sandhāya subhaina māṇavaina pahitaṃ māṇavakaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘akālau, khau māṇavaka, atthi mai aj‍ja bhaisaj‍jamattā pītā, appaiva nāma svaipi upasaṅkamaiyyāmā’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.447)|

dutiyadivasai caitakatthairaina pacchāsamaṇaina gantvā subhaina māṇavaina puṭṭhau imasmiṃ dīghanikāyai subhasuttaṃ nāma dasamaṃ suttaṃ abhāsi|

atha ānandatthairau jaitavanamahāvihārai khaṇḍaphul‍lappaṭisaṅkharaṇaṃ  kārāpaitvā upakaṭṭhāya vassūpanāyikāya bhikkhusaṅghaṃ auhāya rājagahaṃ gatau  tathā añ‍ñaipi dhammasaṅgāhakā bhikkhūti| aivañhi gatai, tai sandhāya ca idaṃ vuttaṃ – ‘‘atha khau thairā bhikkhū rājagahaṃ agamaṃsu, dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca  saṅgāyitu’’nti (cūḷava॰ 438)| tai āsaḷhīpuṇṇamāyaṃ upausathaṃ katvā pāṭipadadivasai san‍nipatitvā vassaṃ upagacchiṃsu|

taina khau pana samayaina rājagahaṃ parivāraitvā aṭṭhārasa mahāvihārā haunti, tai sabbaipi chaḍḍitapatitauk‍lāpā ahaisuṃ| bhagavatau hi parinibbānai sabbaipi bhikkhū attanau attanau pattacīvaramādāya vihārai ca parivaiṇai ca chaḍḍaitvā agamaṃsu| tattha katikavattaṃ kurumānā thairā bhagavatau vacanapūjanatthaṃ titthiyavādaparimaucanatthañ‍ca – ‘paṭhamaṃ māsaṃ khaṇḍaphul‍lappaṭisaṅkharaṇaṃ karaumā’ti cintaisuṃ| titthiyā hi aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘‘samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakā satthari ṭhitaiyaiva vihārai paṭijaggiṃsu, parinibbutai chaḍḍaisuṃ, kulānaṃ mahādhanaparic‍cāgau vinassatī’’ti| taisañ‍ca vādaparimaucanatthaṃ cintaisunti vuttaṃ hauti| aivaṃ cintayitvā ca pana katikavattaṃ kariṃsu| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘atha khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – bhagavatā, khau āvusau, khaṇḍaphul‍lappaṭisaṅkharaṇaṃ vaṇṇitaṃ, handa mayaṃ, āvusau, paṭhamaṃ māsaṃ khaṇḍaphul‍lappaṭisaṅkharaṇaṃ karauma, majjhimaṃ māsaṃ san‍nipatitvā dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyissāmā’’ti (cūḷava॰ 438)|

tai dutiyadivasai gantvā rājadvārai aṭṭhaṃsu| rājā āgantvā vanditvā – ‘‘kiṃ bhantai, āgatatthā’’ti attanā kattabbakic‍caṃ pucchi| thairā aṭṭhārasa mahāvihārapaṭisaṅkharaṇatthāya hatthakammaṃ paṭivaidaisuṃ| rājā hatthakammakārakai manussai adāsi| thairā paṭhamaṃ māsaṃ sabbavihārai paṭisaṅkharāpaitvā rañ‍ñau āraucaisuṃ – ‘‘niṭṭhitaṃ, mahārāja, vihārapaṭisaṅkharaṇaṃ, idāni dhammavinayasaṅgahaṃ karaumā’’ti| ‘‘sādhu bhantai visaṭṭhā karautha, mayhaṃ āṇācak‍kaṃ, tumhākañ‍ca dhammacak‍kaṃ hautu, āṇāpaitha, bhantai, kiṃ karaumī’’ti| ‘‘saṅgahaṃ karauntānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ san‍nisaj‍jaṭṭhānaṃ mahārājā’’ti| ‘‘kattha karaumi, bhantai’’ti? ‘‘vaibhārapabbatapassai sattapaṇṇi guhādvārai kātuṃ yuttaṃ mahārājā’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau rājā ajātasattu vissakammunā  nimmitasadisaṃ suvibhattabhittithambhasaupānaṃ, nānāvidhamālākammalatākammavicittaṃ, abhibhavantamiva rājabhavanavibhūtiṃ, avahasantamiva daivavimānasiriṃ, siriyā nikaitanamiva aikanipātatitthamiva ca daivamanussanayanavihaṃgānaṃ, laukarāmaṇaiyyakamiva sampiṇḍitaṃ daṭṭhabbasāramaṇḍaṃ maṇḍapaṃ kārāpaitvā vividhakusumadāmaulambakaviniggalantacāruvitānaṃ nānāratanavicittamaṇikauṭṭimatalamiva ca, naṃ nānāpupphūpahāravicittasupariniṭṭhitabhūmikammaṃ brahmavimānasadisaṃ alaṅkaritvā, tasmiṃ mahāmaṇḍapai  pañ‍casatānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anagghāni pañ‍ca kappiyapac‍cattharaṇasatāni pañ‍ñapaitvā, dakkhiṇabhāgaṃ nissāya uttarābhimukhaṃ thairāsanaṃ, maṇḍapamajjhai puratthābhimukhaṃ buddhassa bhagavatau āsanārahaṃ dhammāsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā, dantakhacitaṃ bījaniñ‍caittha ṭhapaitvā, bhikkhusaṅghassa āraucāpaisi – ‘‘niṭṭhitaṃ, bhantai, mama kic‍ca’’nti|

tasmiñ‍ca pana divasai aikac‍cai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ sandhāya aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai aikau bhikkhu vissagandhaṃ vāyantau vicaratī’’ti| thairau taṃ sutvā imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai añ‍ñau vissagandhaṃ vāyantau vicaraṇakabhikkhu nāma natthi| addhā aitai maṃ sandhāya vadantīti saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍ji| aikac‍cai naṃ āhaṃsuyaiva – ‘‘svai āvusau, ānanda, san‍nipātau, tvañ‍ca saikkhau sakaraṇīyau, taina tai na yuttaṃ san‍nipātaṃ gantuṃ, appamattau hauhī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau – ‘svai san‍nipātau, na khau maitaṃ patirūpaṃ yvāhaṃ saikkhau samānau san‍nipātaṃ gacchaiyya’nti, bahudaiva rattiṃ kāyagatāya satiyā vītināmaitvā  rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayai caṅkamā aurauhitvā vihāraṃ pavisitvā ‘‘nipaj‍jissāmī’’ti kāyaṃ āvaj‍jaisi, dvai pādā bhūmitau muttā, apattañ‍ca sīsaṃ bimbauhanaṃ, aitasmiṃ antarai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍ci| ayañhi āyasmā caṅkamaina bahi vītināmaitvā visaisaṃ nibbattaituṃ asak‍kauntau cintaisi – ‘‘nanu maṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘katapuñ‍ñausi tvaṃ, ānanda, padhānamanuyuñ‍ja, khippaṃ hauhisi anāsavau’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.207)| buddhānañ‍ca kathādausau nāma natthi, mama pana ac‍cāraddhaṃ vīriyaṃ, taina mai cittaṃ uddhac‍cāya saṃvattati| handāhaṃ vīriyasamataṃ yaujaimī’’ti, caṅkamā aurauhitvā pādadhauvanaṭṭhānai ṭhatvā pādai dhauvitvā  vihāraṃ pavisitvā mañ‍cakai nisīditvā, ‘‘thaukaṃ vissamissāmī’’ti kāyaṃ mañ‍cakai apanāmaisi| dvai pādā bhūmitau muttā, sīsaṃ bimbauhanamappattaṃ, aitasmiṃ antarai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ, catuiriyāpathavirahitaṃ thairassa arahattaṃ| taina ‘‘imasmiṃ sāsanai anipan‍nau anisin‍nau aṭṭhitau acaṅkamantau kau bhikkhu arahattaṃ pattau’’ti vuttai ‘‘ānandatthairau’’ti vattuṃ vaṭṭati|

atha thairā bhikkhū dutiyadivasai pañ‍camiyaṃ kāḷapakkhassa katabhattakic‍cā pattacīvaraṃ paṭisāmaitvā dhammasabhāyaṃ san‍nipatiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā ānandau arahā samānau san‍nipātaṃ agamāsi| kathaṃ agamāsi? ‘‘idānimhi san‍nipātamajjhaṃ pavisanārahau’’ti haṭṭhatuṭṭhacittau aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā bandhanā muttatālapak‍kaṃ viya, paṇḍukambalai nikkhittajātimaṇi viya, vigatavalāhakai nabhai samuggatapuṇṇacandau viya, bālātapasamphassavikasitaraiṇupiñ‍jaragabbhaṃ padumaṃ viya  ca, parisuddhaina pariyaudātaina sappabhaina sassirīkaina ca mukhavaraina attanau arahattappattiṃ āraucayamānau viya agamāsi| atha naṃ disvā āyasmatau mahākassapassa aitadahausi – ‘‘saubhati vata bhau arahattappattau ānandau, sacai satthā dharaiyya, addhā aj‍jānandassa sādhukāraṃ dadaiyya, handa, dānissāhaṃ satthārā dātabbaṃ sādhukāraṃ dadāmī’’ti, tikkhattuṃ sādhukāramadāsi|

majjhimabhāṇakā pana vadanti – ‘‘ānandatthairau attanau arahattappattiṃ ñāpaitukāmau bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ nāgatau, bhikkhū yathāvuḍḍhaṃ attanau attanau pattāsanai nisīdantā ānandatthairassa āsanaṃ ṭhapaitvā nisin‍nā| tattha kaici aivamāhaṃsu – ‘aitaṃ āsanaṃ kassā’ti? ‘ānandassā’ti| ‘ānandau pana kuhiṃ gatau’ti? tasmiṃ samayai thairau cintaisi – ‘idāni mayhaṃ gamanakālau’ti| tatau attanau ānubhāvaṃ dassaintau pathaviyaṃ nimuj‍jitvā attanau āsanaiyaiva attānaṃ dassaisī’’ti, ākāsaina gantvā nisīdītipi aikai| yathā vā tathā vā hautu| sabbathāpi taṃ disvā āyasmatau mahākassapassa sādhukāradānaṃ yuttamaiva|

aivaṃ āgatai pana tasmiṃ āyasmantai mahākassapatthairau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau, kiṃ paṭhamaṃ saṅgāyāma, dhammaṃ vā vinayaṃ vā’’ti? bhikkhū  āhaṃsu – ‘‘bhantai, mahākassapa, vinayau nāma buddhasāsanassa āyu| vinayai ṭhitai sāsanaṃ ṭhitaṃ nāma hauti| tasmā paṭhamaṃ vinayaṃ saṅgāyāmā’’ti| ‘‘kaṃ dhuraṃ katvā’’ti? ‘‘āyasmantaṃ upāli’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ ānandau nappahautī’’ti? ‘‘nau nappahauti’’| api ca khau pana sammāsambuddhau dharamānauyaiva vinayapariyattiṃ nissāya āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ aitadaggai ṭhapaisi – ‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, mama sāvakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vinayadharānaṃ yadidaṃ upālī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 1.228)| ‘tasmā upālitthairaṃ pucchitvā vinayaṃ saṅgāyāmā’ti|

tatau thairau vinayaṃ pucchanatthāya attanāva attānaṃ samman‍ni| upālitthairaupi vissaj‍janatthāya samman‍ni| tatrāyaṃ pāḷi – atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ,

ahaṃ upāliṃ vinayaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti||

āyasmāpi upāli saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ,

ahaṃ āyasmatā mahākassapaina vinayaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiyya’’nti|| (cūḷava॰ 439)|

aivaṃ attānaṃ samman‍nitvā āyasmā upāli uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ  katvā thairai bhikkhū vanditvā dhammāsanai nisīdi dantakhacitaṃ bījaniṃ gahaitvā, tatau mahākassapatthairau thairāsanai nisīditvā āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ vinayaṃ pucchi| ‘‘paṭhamaṃ āvusau, upāli, pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñatta’’nti? ‘‘vaisāliyaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘kaṃ ārabbhā’’ti? ‘‘sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ ārabbhā’’ti| ‘‘kismiṃ vatthusmi’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhammai’’ti|

‘‘atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ paṭhamassa pārājikassa vatthumpi pucchi, nidānampi pucchi, puggalampi pucchi, pañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, anupañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, āpattimpi pucchi, anāpattimpi pucchi’’ (cūḷava॰ 439)| puṭṭhau puṭṭhau āyasmā upāli vissaj‍jaisi|

kiṃ panaittha paṭhamapārājikai kiñ‍ci apanaitabbaṃ vā pakkhipitabbaṃ vā atthi natthīti? apanaitabbaṃ natthi| buddhassa hi bhagavatau bhāsitai apanaitabbaṃ nāma natthi| na hi tathāgatā aikabyañ‍janampi niratthakaṃ vadanti| sāvakānaṃ pana daivatānaṃ  vā bhāsitai apanaitabbampi hauti, taṃ dhammasaṅgāhakatthairā apanayiṃsu| pakkhipitabbaṃ pana sabbatthāpi atthi, tasmā yaṃ yattha pakkhipituṃ yuttaṃ, taṃ pakkhipiṃsuyaiva| kiṃ pana tanti? ‘taina samayainā’ti vā, ‘taina khau pana samayainā’ti vā, ‘atha khauti vā’, ‘aivaṃ vuttaiti’ vā, ‘aitadavaucā’ti vā, aivamādikaṃ sambandhavacanamattaṃ| aivaṃ pakkhipitabbayuttaṃ pakkhipitvā pana – ‘‘idaṃ paṭhamapārājika’’nti ṭhapaisuṃ| paṭhamapārājikai saṅgahamārūḷhai pañ‍ca arahantasatāni saṅgahaṃ āraupitanayainaiva gaṇasajjhāyamakaṃsu – ‘‘taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā vairañ‍jāyaṃ viharatī’’ti| taisaṃ sajjhāyāraddhakālaiyaiva sādhukāraṃ dadamānā viya mahāpathavī udakapariyantaṃ katvā akampittha|

aitainaiva nayaina saisāni tīṇi pārājikāni saṅgahaṃ āraupaitvā ‘‘idaṃ pārājikakaṇḍa’’nti ṭhapaisuṃ| tairasa saṅghādisaisāni ‘‘tairasaka’’nti ṭhapaisuṃ| dvai sikkhāpadāni ‘‘aniyatānī’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| tiṃsa sikkhāpadāni ‘‘nissaggiyāni pācittiyānī’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| dvainavuti sikkhāpadāni ‘‘pācittiyānī’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| cattāri sikkhāpadāni ‘‘pāṭidaisanīyānī’’ti  ṭhapaisuṃ| pañ‍casattati sikkhāpadāni ‘‘saikhiyānī’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| satta dhammai ‘‘adhikaraṇasamathā’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| aivaṃ sattavīsādhikāni dvai sikkhāpadasatāni ‘‘mahāvibhaṅgau’’ti kittaitvā ṭhapaisuṃ| mahāvibhaṅgāvasānaipi purimanayainaiva mahāpathavī akampittha|

tatau bhikkhunīvibhaṅgai aṭṭha sikkhāpadāni ‘‘pārājikakaṇḍaṃ nāma ida’’nti ṭhapaisuṃ| sattarasa sikkhāpadāni ‘‘sattarasaka’’nti ṭhapaisuṃ| tiṃsa sikkhāpadāni ‘‘nissaggiyāni pācittiyānī’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| chasaṭṭhisatasikkhāpadāni ‘‘pācittiyānī’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| aṭṭha sikkhāpadāni ‘‘pāṭidaisanīyānī’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| pañ‍casattati sikkhāpadāni ‘‘saikhiyānī’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| satta dhammai ‘‘adhikaraṇasamathā’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| aivaṃ tīṇi sikkhāpadasatāni cattāri ca sikkhāpadāni ‘‘bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau’’ti kittaitvā – ‘‘ayaṃ ubhatau vibhaṅgau nāma catusaṭṭhibhāṇavārau’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| ubhatauvibhaṅgāvasānaipi vuttanayainaiva mahāpathavikampau ahausi|

aitainaivupāyaina asītibhāṇavāraparimāṇaṃ khandhakaṃ, pañ‍cavīsatibhāṇavāraparimāṇaṃ parivārañ‍ca saṅgahaṃ āraupaitvā ‘‘idaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ nāmā’’ti ṭhapaisuṃ| vinayapiṭakāvasānaipi vuttanayainaiva mahāpathavikampau ahausi| taṃ āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ paṭicchāpaisuṃ – ‘‘āvusau, imaṃ tuyhaṃ nissitakai vācaihī’’ti| vinayapiṭakasaṅgahāvasānai upālitthairau dantakhacitaṃ bījaniṃ nikkhipitvā dhammāsanā aurauhitvā thairai bhikkhū vanditvā attanau pattāsanai nisīdi|

vinayaṃ saṅgāyitvā dhammaṃ saṅgāyitukāmau āyasmā mahākassapau bhikkhū pucchi – ‘‘dhammaṃ saṅgāyantai hi kaṃ puggalaṃ dhuraṃ katvā dhammau saṅgāyitabbau’’ti? bhikkhū – ‘‘ānandatthairaṃ dhuraṃ katvā’’ti āhaṃsu|

atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau, yadi saṅghassa  pattakal‍laṃ,

ahaṃ ānandaṃ dhammaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ,

ahaṃ āyasmatā mahākassapaina dhammaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā thairai bhikkhū vanditvā dhammāsanai nisīdi dantakhacitaṃ bījaniṃ gahaitvā| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau bhikkhū pucchi – ‘‘kataraṃ, āvusau, piṭakaṃ paṭhamaṃ saṅgāyāmā’’ti? ‘‘suttantapiṭakaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘suttantapiṭakai catassau saṅgītiyau, tāsu paṭhamaṃ kataraṃ saṅgīti’’nti? ‘‘dīghasaṅgītiṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘dīghasaṅgītiyaṃ catutiṃsa suttāni, tayau vaggā, taisu paṭhamaṃ kataraṃ vagga’’nti? ‘‘sīlakkhandhavaggaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘sīlakkhandhavaggai tairasa suttantā, taisu paṭhamaṃ kataraṃ sutta’’nti? ‘‘brahmajālasuttaṃ nāma bhantai, tividhasīlālaṅkataṃ, nānāvidhamicchājīvakuha lapanādividdhaṃsanaṃ, dvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhijālavinivaiṭhanaṃ, dasasahassilaukadhātukampanaṃ, taṃ paṭhamaṃ saṅgāyāmā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca, ‘‘brahmajālaṃ, āvusau ānanda, kattha bhāsita’’nti? ‘‘antarā ca, bhantai, rājagahaṃ antarā ca nāḷandaṃ rājāgārakai ambalaṭṭhikāya’’nti| ‘‘kaṃ ārabbhā’’ti? ‘‘suppiyañ‍ca paribbājakaṃ, brahmadattañ‍ca māṇava’’nti| ‘‘kismiṃ vatthusmi’’nti? ‘‘vaṇṇāvaṇṇai’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ brahmajālassa nidānampi pucchi, puggalampi pucchi, vatthumpi pucchi (cūḷava॰ 440)| āyasmā ānandau vissaj‍jaisi| vissaj‍janāvasānai pañ‍ca arahantasatāni gaṇasajjhāyamakaṃsu| vuttanayainaiva ca pathavikampau ahausi|

aivaṃ brahmajālaṃ saṅgāyitvā tatau paraṃ ‘‘sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ, panāvusau ānanda, kattha bhāsita’’ntiādinā nayaina pucchāvissaj‍janānuk‍kamaina saddhiṃ brahmajālaina sabbaipi tairasa suttantai saṅgāyitvā – ‘‘ayaṃ sīlakkhandhavaggau nāmā’’ti kittaitvā ṭhapaisuṃ|

tadanantaraṃ mahāvaggaṃ, tadanantaraṃ pāthikavagganti, aivaṃ tivaggasaṅgahaṃ catutiṃsasuttapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ  catusaṭṭhibhāṇavāraparimāṇaṃ tantiṃ saṅgāyitvā ‘‘ayaṃ dīghanikāyau nāmā’’ti vatvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ paṭicchāpaisuṃ – ‘‘āvusau, imaṃ tuyhaṃ nissitakai vācaihī’’ti|

tatau  anantaraṃ asītibhāṇavāraparimāṇaṃ majjhimanikāyaṃ saṅgāyitvā dhammasaināpatisāriputtatthairassa nissitakai paṭicchāpaisuṃ – ‘‘imaṃ tumhai pariharathā’’ti|

tatau anantaraṃ satabhāṇavāraparimāṇaṃ saṃyuttanikāyaṃ saṅgāyitvā mahākassapatthairaṃ paṭicchāpaisuṃ – ‘‘bhantai, imaṃ tumhākaṃ nissitakai vācaithā’’ti|

tatau anantaraṃ vīsatibhāṇavārasataparimāṇaṃ aṅguttaranikāyaṃ saṅgāyitvā anuruddhatthairaṃ paṭicchāpaisuṃ – ‘‘imaṃ tumhākaṃ nissitakai vācaithā’’ti|

tatau anantaraṃ dhammasaṅgahavibhaṅgadhātukathāpuggalapañ‍ñattikathāvatthuyamakapaṭṭhānaṃ abhidhammauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ saṃvaṇṇitaṃ sukhumañāṇagaucaraṃ tantiṃ saṅgāyitvā – ‘‘idaṃ abhidhammapiṭakaṃ nāmā’’ti vatvā pañ‍ca arahantasatāni sajjhāyamakaṃsu| vuttanayainaiva pathavikampau ahausīti|

tatau paraṃ jātakaṃ, niddaisau, paṭisambhidāmaggau, apadānaṃ, suttanipātau, khuddakapāṭhau, dhammapadaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, vimānavatthu, paitavatthu, thairagāthā, thairīgāthāti imaṃ tantiṃ saṅgāyitvā ‘‘khuddakaganthau nāmāya’’nti ca vatvā ‘‘abhidhammapiṭakasmiṃyaiva saṅgahaṃ āraupayiṃsū’’ti dīghabhāṇakā vadanti| majjhimabhāṇakā pana ‘‘cariyāpiṭakabuddhavaṃsaihi saddhiṃ sabbampaitaṃ khuddakaganthaṃ nāma suttantapiṭakai pariyāpan‍na’’nti vadanti|

aivamaitaṃ sabbampi buddhavacanaṃ rasavasaina aikavidhaṃ, dhammavinayavasaina duvidhaṃ, paṭhamamajjhimapacchimavasaina tividhaṃ| tathā piṭakavasaina| nikāyavasaina pañ‍cavidhaṃ, aṅgavasaina navavidhaṃ, dhammakkhandhavasaina caturāsītisahassavidhanti vaiditabbaṃ|

kathaṃ rasavasaina aikavidhaṃ? yañhi bhagavatā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhitvā yāva anupādisaisāya  nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati, aitthantarai pañ‍cacattālīsavassāni daivamanussanāgayakkhādayau anusāsantaina vā pac‍cavaikkhantaina vā vuttaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ aikarasaṃ vimuttirasamaiva hauti| aivaṃ rasavasaina aikavidhaṃ|

kathaṃ dhammavinayavasaina duvidhaṃ? sabbamaiva caitaṃ dhammau caiva vinayau cāti saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| tattha vinayapiṭakaṃ vinayau, avasaisaṃ buddhavacanaṃ dhammau| tainaivāha ‘‘yan‍nūna mayaṃ dhammañ‍ca  vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyaiyyāmā’’ti (cūḷava॰ 437)| ‘‘ahaṃ upāliṃ vinayaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ, ānandaṃ dhammaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti ca| aivaṃ dhammavinayavasaina duvidhaṃ|

kathaṃ paṭhamamajjhimapacchimavasaina tividhaṃ? sabbamaiva hidaṃ paṭhamabuddhavacanaṃ, majjhimabuddhavacanaṃ, pacchimabuddhavacananti tippabhaidaṃ hauti| tattha –

‘‘anaikajātisaṃsāraṃ, sandhāvissaṃ anibbisaṃ|

gahakāraṃ gavaisantau, dukkhā jāti punappunaṃ||

gahakāraka diṭṭhausi, puna gaihaṃ na kāhasi|

sabbā tai phāsukā bhaggā, gahakūṭaṃ visaṅkhataṃ|

visaṅkhāragataṃ cittaṃ, taṇhānaṃ khayamajjhagā’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 153-54)|

idaṃ paṭhamabuddhavacanaṃ| kaici ‘‘yadā havai pātubhavanti dhammā’’ti (mahāva॰ 1) khandhakai udānagāthaṃ vadanti| aisā pana pāṭipadadivasai sabbañ‍ñubhāvappattassa saumanassamayañāṇaina pac‍cayākāraṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa uppan‍nā udānagāthāti vaiditabbā|

yaṃ  pana parinibbānakālai abhāsi – ‘‘handa dāni, bhikkhavai, āmantayāmi vau, vayadhammā saṅkhārā, appamādaina sampādaithā’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.218) idaṃ pacchimabuddhavacanaṃ| ubhin‍namantarai yaṃ vuttaṃ, aitaṃ majjhimabuddhavacanaṃ nāma| aivaṃ paṭhamamajjhimapacchimabuddhavacanavasaina tividhaṃ|

kathaṃ piṭakavasaina tividhaṃ? sabbampi caitaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ suttantapiṭakaṃ abhidhammapiṭakanti tippabhaidamaiva  hauti| tattha paṭhamasaṅgītiyaṃ saṅgītañ‍ca asaṅgītañ‍ca sabbampi samaudhānaitvā ubhayāni pātimaukkhāni, dvai vibhaṅgā, dvāvīsati khandhakā, sauḷasaparivārāti – idaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ nāma| brahmajālādicatuttiṃsasuttasaṅgahau dīghanikāyau, mūlapariyāyasuttādidiyaḍḍhasatadvaisuttasaṅgahau majjhimanikāyau, aughataraṇasuttādisattasuttasahassasattasatadvāsaṭṭhisuttasaṅgahau saṃyuttanikāyau, cittapariyādānasuttādinavasuttasahassapañ‍casatasattapañ‍ñāsasuttasaṅgahau aṅguttaranikāyau, khuddakapāṭha-dhammapada-udāna-itivuttaka-suttanipāta-vimānavatthu-paitavatthu-thairagāthā-thairīgāthā-jātaka-niddaisa-paṭisambhidāmagga-apadāna-buddhavaṃsa-cariyāpiṭakavasaina pan‍narasappabhaidau khuddakanikāyauti  idaṃ suttantapiṭakaṃ nāma| dhammasaṅgahau, vibhaṅgau, dhātukathā, puggalapañ‍ñatti, kathāvatthu, yamakaṃ, paṭṭhānanti – idaṃ abhidhammapiṭakaṃ nāma| tattha –

‘‘vividhavisaisanayattā, vinayanatau caiva kāyavācānaṃ|

vinayatthavidūhi ayaṃ, vinayau vinayauti akkhātau’’||

vividhā hi aittha pañ‍cavidhapātimaukkhuddaisapārājikādi satta āpattikkhandhamātikā vibhaṅgādippabhaidā nayā| visaisabhūtā ca daḷhīkammasithilakaraṇappayaujanā anupañ‍ñattinayā| kāyikavācasikaajjhācāranisaidhanatau caisa kāyaṃ vācañ‍ca vinaiti, tasmā vividhanayattā visaisanayattā kāyavācānaṃ vinayanatau caiva vinayauti akkhātau| tainaitamaitassa vacanatthakausal‍latthaṃ vuttaṃ –

‘‘vividhavisaisanayattā, vinayanatau caiva kāyavācānaṃ|

vinayatthavidūhi ayaṃ, vinayau vinayauti akkhātau’’ti||

itaraṃ  pana –

‘‘atthānaṃ sūcanatau suvuttatau, savanatautha sūdanatau|

suttāṇā suttasabhāgatau ca, suttanti akkhātaṃ||

tañhi attatthaparatthādibhaidai atthai sūcaiti| suvuttā caittha atthā, vainaiyyajjhāsayānulaumaina vuttattā| savati caitaṃ atthai sassamiva phalaṃ, pasavatīti vuttaṃ hauti| sūdati caitaṃ dhainu viya khīraṃ, paggharāpaitīti vuttaṃ hauti| suṭṭhu  ca nai tāyati, rakkhatīti vuttaṃ hauti| suttasabhāgañ‍caitaṃ, yathā hi tacchakānaṃ suttaṃ pamāṇaṃ hauti, aivamaitampi viñ‍ñūnaṃ| yathā ca suttaina saṅgahitāni pupphāni na vikirīyanti, na viddhaṃsīyanti, aivamaiva taina saṅgahitā atthā| tainaitamaitassa vacanatthakausal‍latthaṃ vuttaṃ –

‘‘atthānaṃ sūcanatau, suvuttatau savanatautha sūdanatau|

suttāṇā suttasabhāgatau ca, suttanti akkhāta’’nti||

itarau  pana –

‘‘yaṃ aittha vuḍḍhimantau, salakkhaṇā pūjitā paricchin‍nā|

vuttādhikā ca dhammā, abhidhammau taina akkhātau’’||

ayañhi abhisaddau vuḍḍhilakkhaṇapūjitaparicchin‍nādhikaisu dissati| tathā haisa ‘‘bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamantī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 3.389) vuḍḍhiyaṃ āgatau| ‘‘yā tā rattiyau abhiñ‍ñātā abhilakkhitā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.49) salakkhaṇai| ‘‘rājābhirājā manujindau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.399) pūjitai| ‘‘paṭibalau vinaituṃ abhidhammai abhivinayai’’tiādīsu (mahāva॰ 85) paricchin‍nai| añ‍ñamañ‍ñasaṅkaravirahitai dhammai ca vinayai cāti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘abhik‍kantaina vaṇṇainā’’tiādīsu (vi॰ va॰ 819) adhikai|

aittha ca ‘‘rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti’’ (dha॰ sa॰ 251), ‘‘maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatī’’tiādinā (vibha॰ 642) nayaina vuḍḍhimantaupi dhammā vuttā| ‘‘rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā’’tiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 1) nayaina ārammaṇādīhi lakkhaṇīyattā  salakkhaṇāpi| ‘‘saikkhā dhammā, asaikkhā dhammā, laukuttarā dhammā’’tiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ tikamātikā 11, dukamātikā 12) nayaina pūjitāpi, pūjārahāti adhippāyau| ‘‘phassau hauti, vaidanā hautī’’tiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 1) nayaina sabhāvaparicchin‍nattā paricchin‍nāpi| ‘‘mahaggatā dhammā, appamāṇā dhammā (dha॰ sa॰ tikamātikā 11), anuttarā dhammā’’tiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ dukamātikā 11) nayaina adhikāpi dhammā vuttā| tainaitamaitassa vacanatthakausal‍latthaṃ vuttaṃ –

‘‘yaṃ aittha vuḍḍhimantau, salakkhaṇā pūjitā paricchin‍nā|

vuttādhikā ca dhammā, abhidhammau taina akkhātau’’ti||

yaṃ panaittha avisiṭṭhaṃ, taṃ –

‘‘piṭakaṃ piṭakatthavidū, pariyattibbhājanatthatau āhu|

taina samaudhānaitvā, tayaupi vinayādayau ñaiyyā’’||

pariyattipi  hi ‘‘mā piṭakasampadānainā’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 3.66) piṭakanti vuc‍cati| ‘‘atha purisau āgacchaiyya kudālapiṭakamādāyā’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 3.70) yaṃ kiñ‍ci bhājanampi| tasmā ‘piṭakaṃ piṭakatthavidū pariyattibhājanatthatau āhu|

idāni ‘taina samaudhānaitvā  tayaupi vinayādayau ñaiyyā’ti, taina aivaṃ duvidhatthaina piṭakasaddaina saha samāsaṃ katvā vinayau ca sau piṭakañ‍ca pariyattibhāvatau, tassa tassa atthassa bhājanatau cāti vinayapiṭakaṃ, yathāvuttainaiva nayaina suttantañ‍ca taṃ piṭakañ‍cāti suttantapiṭakaṃ, abhidhammau ca sau piṭakañ‍cāti abhidhammapiṭakanti| aivamaitai tayaupi vinayādayau ñaiyyā|

aivaṃ ñatvā ca punapi taisuyaiva piṭakaisu nānappakārakausal‍latthaṃ –

‘‘daisanāsāsanakathābhaidaṃ taisu yathārahaṃ|

sikkhāppahānagambhīrabhāvañ‍ca paridīpayai||

pariyattibhaidaṃ sampattiṃ, vipattiñ‍cāpi yaṃ yahiṃ|

pāpuṇāti yathā bhikkhu, tampi sabbaṃ vibhāvayai’’||

tatrāyaṃ paridīpanā vibhāvanā ca| aitāni hi tīṇi piṭakāni yathāk‍kamaṃ āṇāvauhāraparamatthadaisanā, yathāparādhayathānulaumayathādhammasāsanāni, saṃvarāsaṃvaradiṭṭhivinivaiṭhananāmarūpaparicchaidakathāti ca vuc‍canti| aittha  hi vinayapiṭakaṃ āṇārahaina bhagavatā āṇābāhul‍latau daisitattā āṇādaisanā, suttantapiṭakaṃ vauhārakusalaina bhagavatā vauhārabāhul‍latau daisitattā vauhāradaisanā, abhidhammapiṭakaṃ paramatthakusalaina bhagavatā paramatthabāhul‍latau daisitattā paramatthadaisanāti vuc‍cati|

tathā paṭhamaṃ – ‘yai tai pacurāparādhā sattā, tai yathāparādhaṃ aittha sāsitā’ti yathāparādhasāsanaṃ, dutiyaṃ – ‘anaikajjhāsayānusayacariyādhimuttikā sattā yathānulaumaṃ aittha sāsitā’ti yathānulaumasāsanaṃ, tatiyaṃ – ‘dhammapuñ‍jamattai ‘‘ahaṃ mamā’’ti sañ‍ñinau sattā yathādhammaṃ aittha sāsitā’ti yathādhammasāsananti vuc‍cati|

tathā paṭhamaṃ – ajjhācārapaṭipakkhabhūtau saṃvarāsaṃvarau aittha kathitauti saṃvarāsaṃvarakathā| saṃvarāsaṃvarauti  khuddakau caiva mahantau ca saṃvarau, kammākammaṃ viya, phalāphalaṃ viya ca, dutiyaṃ – ‘‘dvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhipaṭipakkhabhūtā diṭṭhivinivaiṭhanā aittha kathitā’’ti diṭṭhivinivaiṭhanakathā, tatiyaṃ – ‘‘rāgādipaṭipakkhabhūtau nāmarūpaparicchaidau aittha kathitau’’ti nāmarūpaparicchaidakathāti vuc‍cati|

tīsupi caitaisu tissau sikkhā, tīṇi pahānāni, catubbidhau ca gambhīrabhāvau vaiditabbau| tathā hi vinayapiṭakai visaisaina adhisīlasikkhā vuttā, suttantapiṭakai adhicittasikkhā, abhidhammapiṭakai adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā|

vinayapiṭakai ca vītik‍kamappahānaṃ, kilaisānaṃ vītik‍kamapaṭipakkhattā sīlassa| suttantapiṭakai pariyuṭṭhānappahānaṃ, pariyuṭṭhānapaṭipakkhattā samādhissa| abhidhammapiṭakai anusayappahānaṃ, anusayapaṭipakkhattā pañ‍ñāya| paṭhamai ca tadaṅgappahānaṃ, itaraisu vikkhambhanasamucchaidappahānāni| paṭhamai ca duc‍caritasaṃkilaisappahānaṃ, itaraisu taṇhādiṭṭhisaṃkilaisappahānaṃ|

aikamaikasmiñ‍caittha catubbidhaupi dhammatthadaisanā paṭivaidhagambhīrabhāvau vaiditabbau| tattha dhammauti tanti| atthauti tassāyaiva atthau| daisanāti tassā manasā vavatthāpitāya tantiyā daisanā| paṭivaidhauti tantiyā tantiatthassa ca yathābhūtāvabaudhau| tīsupi caitaisu aitai dhammatthadaisanāpaṭivaidhā| yasmā sasādīhi viya mahāsamuddau  mandabuddhīhi dukkhaugāḷhā alabbhanaiyyapatiṭṭhā ca, tasmā gambhīrā| aivaṃ aikamaikasmiṃ aittha catubbidhaupi gambhīrabhāvau vaiditabbau|

aparau nayau, dhammauti haitu| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘haitumhi ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā’’ti| atthauti haituphalaṃ, vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘haituphalai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā’’ti (vibha॰ 720)| daisanāti pañ‍ñatti, yathā dhammaṃ dhammābhilāpauti adhippāyau| anulaumapaṭilaumasaṅkhaipavitthārādivasaina vā kathanaṃ| paṭivaidhauti abhisamayau, sau ca laukiyalaukuttarau visayatau asammauhatau ca, atthānurūpaṃ dhammaisu, dhammānurūpaṃ atthaisu, pañ‍ñattipathānurūpaṃ pañ‍ñattīsu avabaudhau| taisaṃ taisaṃ vā tattha tattha vuttadhammānaṃ paṭivijjhitabbau salakkhaṇasaṅkhātau aviparītasabhāvau|

idāni  yasmā aitaisu piṭakaisu yaṃ yaṃ dhammajātaṃ vā atthajātaṃ vā, yā cāyaṃ yathā yathā ñāpaitabbau atthau sautūnaṃ ñāṇassa abhimukhau hauti, tathā tathā tadatthajautikā daisanā, yau caittha aviparītāvabaudhasaṅkhātau paṭivaidhau, taisaṃ taisaṃ vā dhammānaṃ paṭivijjhitabbau salakkhaṇasaṅkhātau aviparītasabhāvau| sabbampaitaṃ anupacitakusalasambhāraihi duppañ‍ñaihi sasādīhi viya mahāsamuddau dukkhaugāḷhaṃ alabbhanaiyyapatiṭṭhañ‍ca, tasmā gambhīraṃ| aivampi aikamaikasmiṃ aittha catubbidhaupi gambhīrabhāvau vaiditabbau|

aittāvatā ca –

‘‘daisanāsāsanakathā, bhaidaṃ taisu yathārahaṃ|

sikkhāppahānagambhīra, bhāvañ‍ca paridīpayai’’ti  –

ayaṃ gāthā vuttatthāva hauti|

‘‘pariyattibhaidaṃ sampattiṃ, vipattiñ‍cāpi yaṃ yahiṃ|

pāpuṇāti yathā bhikkhu, tampi sabbaṃ vibhāvayai’’ti –

aittha pana tīsu piṭakaisu tividhau pariyattibhaidau daṭṭhabbau| tissau hi pariyattiyau – alagaddūpamā, nissaraṇatthā, bhaṇḍāgārikapariyattīti|

tattha yā duggahitā, upārambhādihaitu pariyāpuṭā, ayaṃ alagaddūpamā| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau alagaddatthikau alagaddagavaisī  alagaddapariyaisanaṃ caramānau, sau passaiyya mahantaṃ alagaddaṃ, tamainaṃ bhaugai vā naṅguṭṭhai vā gaṇhaiyya, tassa sau alagaddau paṭiparivattitvā hatthai vā bāhāyaṃ vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā aṅgapac‍caṅgai ḍaṃsaiyya, sau tatau nidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya, maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? duggahitattā, bhikkhavai, alagaddassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cai maughapurisā dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti, suttaṃ…pai॰… vaidal‍laṃ, tai taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ na upaparikkhanti, taisaṃ tai dhammā pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ anupaparikkhataṃ na nijjhānaṃ khamanti, tai upārambhānisaṃsā caiva dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti, itivādappamaukkhānisaṃsā ca, yassa catthāya dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti, tañ‍cassa atthaṃ  nānubhaunti, taisaṃ tai dhammā duggahitā dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? duggahitattā, bhikkhavai, dhammāna’’nti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.238)|

yā pana suggahitā sīlakkhandhādipāripūriṃyaiva ākaṅkhamānaina pariyāpuṭā, na upārambhādihaitu, ayaṃ nissaraṇatthā| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ – ‘‘taisaṃ tai dhammā suggahitā dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? suggahitattā, bhikkhavai, dhammāna’’nti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.239)|

yaṃ pana pariñ‍ñātakkhandhau pahīnakilaisau bhāvitamaggau paṭividdhākuppau sacchikataniraudhau khīṇāsavau kaivalaṃ pavaiṇīpālanatthāya vaṃsānurakkhaṇatthāya pariyāpuṇāti, ayaṃ bhaṇḍāgārikapariyattīti|

vinayai pana suppaṭipan‍nau bhikkhu sīlasampadaṃ nissāya tissau vij‍jā pāpuṇāti, tāsaṃyaiva ca tattha pabhaidavacanatau| suttai suppaṭipan‍nau samādhisampadaṃ nissāya cha abhiñ‍ñā pāpuṇāti, tāsaṃyaiva ca tattha pabhaidavacanatau| abhidhammai suppaṭipan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampadaṃ  nissāya catassau paṭisambhidā pāpuṇāti, tāsañ‍ca tatthaiva pabhaidavacanatau, aivamaitaisu suppaṭipan‍nau yathāk‍kamaina imaṃ vij‍jāttayachaḷabhiñ‍ñācatuppaṭisambhidābhaidaṃ sampattiṃ pāpuṇāti|

vinayai pana duppaṭipan‍nau anuñ‍ñātasukhasamphassaattharaṇapāvuraṇādiphassasāmañ‍ñatau paṭikkhittaisu upādin‍nakaphassādīsu anavaj‍jasañ‍ñī hauti| vuttampi  haitaṃ – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yai mai antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.234)| tatau dussīlabhāvaṃ pāpuṇāti| suttai duppaṭipan‍nau – ‘‘cattārau mai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 4.5) adhippāyaṃ ajānantau duggahitaṃ gaṇhāti, yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ – ‘‘attanā duggahitaina amhai caiva abbhācikkhati, attānañ‍ca khaṇati, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.236)| tatau micchādiṭṭhitaṃ pāpuṇāti| abhidhammai duppaṭipan‍nau dhammacintaṃ atidhāvantau acintaiyyānipi cintaiti| tatau cittakkhaipaṃ pāpuṇāti, vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, acintaiyyāni, na cintaitabbāni, yāni cintaintau ummādassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.77)| aivamaitaisu duppaṭipan‍nau yathāk‍kamaina imaṃ dussīlabhāva micchādiṭṭhitā cittakkhaipabhaidaṃ vipattiṃ pāpuṇātī’’ti|

aittāvatā  ca –

‘‘pariyattibhaidaṃ sampattiṃ, vipattiñ‍cāpi yaṃ yahiṃ|

pāpuṇāti yathā bhikkhu, tampi sabbaṃ vibhāvayai’’ti –

ayampi gāthā vuttatthāva hauti| aivaṃ nānappakāratau piṭakāni ñatvā taisaṃ vasainaitaṃ buddhavacanaṃ tividhanti ñātabbaṃ|

kathaṃ nikāyavasaina pañ‍cavidhaṃ? sabbamaiva caitaṃ dīghanikāyau, majjhimanikāyau, saṃyuttanikāyau, aṅguttaranikāyau, khuddakanikāyauti pañ‍cappabhaidaṃ hauti| tattha katamau dīghanikāyau? tivaggasaṅgahāni brahmajālādīni catuttiṃsa suttāni|

‘‘catuttiṃsaiva suttantā, tivaggau yassa saṅgahau|

aisa dīghanikāyauti, paṭhamau anulaumikau’’ti||

kasmā  panaisa dīghanikāyauti vuc‍cati? dīghappamāṇānaṃ suttānaṃ samūhatau nivāsatau ca| samūhanivāsā hi nikāyauti vuc‍canti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikanikāyampi samanupassāmi aivaṃ cittaṃ, yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā’’ (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.100)| pauṇikanikāyau cikkhal‍likanikāyauti aivamādīni caittha sādhakāni sāsanatau laukatau ca| aivaṃ saisānampi nikāyabhāvai vacanatthau vaiditabbau|

katamau majjhimanikāyau? majjhimappamāṇāni pañ‍cadasavaggasaṅgahāni mūlapariyāyasuttādīni diyaḍḍhasataṃ dvai ca suttāni|

‘‘diyaḍḍhasatasuttantā, dvai ca suttāni yattha sau|

nikāyau majjhimau pañ‍ca, dasavaggapariggahau’’ti||

katamau saṃyuttanikāyau? daivatāsaṃyuttādivasaina kathitāni aughataraṇādīni satta suttasahassāni satta ca suttasatāni dvāsaṭṭhi ca suttāni|

‘‘sattasuttasahassāni, sattasuttasatāni ca|

dvāsaṭṭhi caiva suttantā, aisau saṃyuttasaṅgahau’’ti||

katamau aṅguttaranikāyau? aikaikaaṅgātiraikavasaina kathitāni cittapariyādānādīni nava suttasahassāni pañ‍ca suttasatāni sattapañ‍ñāsañ‍ca suttāni|

‘‘nava suttasahassāni, pañ‍ca suttasatāni ca|

sattapañ‍ñāsa suttāni, saṅkhyā aṅguttarai aya’’nti||

katamau khuddakanikāyau? sakalaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ, abhidhammapiṭakaṃ, khuddakapāṭhādayau ca pubbai dassitā pañ‍cadasappabhaidā, ṭhapaitvā cattārau nikāyai avasaisaṃ buddhavacanaṃ|

‘‘ṭhapaitvā caturaupaitai, nikāyai dīghaādikai|

tadañ‍ñaṃ buddhavacanaṃ, nikāyau khuddakau matau’’ti||

aivaṃ nikāyavasaina pañ‍cavidhaṃ|

kathaṃ aṅgavasaina navavidhaṃ? sabbamaiva hidaṃ suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthā, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍lanti navappabhaidaṃ hauti| tattha ubhatauvibhaṅganiddaisakhandhakaparivārā, suttanipātai maṅgalasuttaratanasuttanālakasuttatuvaṭṭakasuttāni ca añ‍ñampi ca suttanāmakaṃ tathāgatavacanaṃ  suttanti vaiditabbaṃ| sabbampi sagāthakaṃ suttaṃ gaiyyanti  vaiditabbaṃ| visaisaina saṃyuttakai sakalaupi sagāthavaggau, sakalampi abhidhammapiṭakaṃ, niggāthakaṃ suttaṃ, yañ‍ca añ‍ñampi aṭṭhahi aṅgaihi asaṅgahitaṃ buddhavacanaṃ, taṃ vaiyyākaraṇanti vaiditabbaṃ| dhammapadaṃ, thairagāthā, thairīgāthā, suttanipātai nausuttanāmikā suddhikagāthā ca gāthāti vaiditabbā| saumanassañ‍ñāṇamayikagāthā paṭisaṃyuttā dvaiasīti suttantā udānanti vaiditabbaṃ| ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā’’tiādinayappavattā dasuttarasatasuttantā itivuttakanti vaiditabbaṃ| apaṇṇakajātakādīni pañ‍ñāsādhikāni pañ‍cajātakasatāni ‘jātaka’nti vaiditabbaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, acchariyā abbhutā dhammā ānandai’’tiādinayappavattā (dī॰ ni॰ 2.209) sabbaipi acchariyabbhutadhammapaṭisaṃyuttasuttantā abbhutadhammanti vaiditabbaṃ| cūḷavaidal‍la-mahāvaidal‍la-sammādiṭṭhi-sak‍kapañha-saṅkhārabhājaniya-mahāpuṇṇamasuttādayau  sabbaipi vaidañ‍ca tuṭṭhiñ‍ca laddhā laddhā pucchitasuttantā vaidal‍lanti vaiditabbaṃ| aivaṃ aṅgavasaina navavidhaṃ|

kathaṃ dhammakkhandhavasaina caturāsītisahassavidhaṃ? sabbamaiva caitaṃ buddhavacanaṃ –

‘‘dvāsīti buddhatau gaṇhiṃ, dvai sahassāni bhikkhutau|

caturāsīti sahassāni, yai mai dhammā pavattinau’’ti||

aivaṃ paridīpitadhammakkhandhavasaina caturāsītisahassappabhaidaṃ hauti| tattha aikānusandhikaṃ suttaṃ aikau dhammakkhandhau| yaṃ anaikānusandhikaṃ, tattha anusandhivasaina dhammakkhandhagaṇanā| gāthābandhaisu pañhāpucchanaṃ aikau dhammakkhandhau, vissaj‍janaṃ aikau| abhidhammai aikamaikaṃ tikadukabhājanaṃ, aikamaikañ‍ca cittavārabhājanaṃ, aikamaikau dhammakkhandhau| vinayai atthi vatthu, atthi mātikā, atthi padabhājanīyaṃ, atthi antarāpatti, atthi āpatti, atthi anāpatti, atthi tikacchaidau| tattha aikamaikau kauṭṭhāsau aikamaikau dhammakkhandhauti vaiditabbau| aivaṃ dhammakkhandhavasaina caturāsītisahassavidhaṃ|

aivamaitaṃ abhaidatau rasavasaina aikavidhaṃ, bhaidatau dhammavinayādivasaina  duvidhādibhaidaṃ buddhavacanaṃ saṅgāyantaina mahākassapappamukhaina vasīgaṇaina ‘‘ayaṃ  dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ paṭhamabuddhavacanaṃ, idaṃ majjhimabuddhavacanaṃ, idaṃ pacchimabuddhavacanaṃ, idaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ, idaṃ suttantapiṭakaṃ, idaṃ abhidhammapiṭakaṃ, ayaṃ dīghanikāyau…pai॰… ayaṃ khuddakanikāyau, imāni suttādīni navaṅgāni, imāni caturāsīti dhammakkhandhasahassānī’’ti, imaṃ pabhaidaṃ vavatthapaitvāva saṅgītaṃ| na kaivalañ‍ca imamaiva, añ‍ñampi uddānasaṅgaha-vaggasaṅgaha-paiyyālasaṅgaha-aikakanipāta-dukanipātādinipātasaṅgaha-saṃyuttasaṅgaha-paṇṇāsasaṅgahādi-anaikavidhaṃ tīsu piṭakaisu sandissamānaṃ saṅgahappabhaidaṃ vavatthapaitvā aiva sattahi māsaihi saṅgītaṃ|

saṅgītipariyausānai cassa – ‘‘idaṃ mahākassapatthairaina dasabalassa sāsanaṃ pañ‍cavassasahassaparimāṇakālaṃ pavattanasamatthaṃ kata’’nti sañ‍jātappamaudā sādhukāraṃ viya dadamānā ayaṃ mahāpathavī udakapariyantaṃ katvā anaikappakāraṃ kampi saṅkampi sampakampi sampavaidhi, anaikāni ca acchariyāni pāturahaisunti, ayaṃ paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti nāma| yā laukai –

‘‘sataihi  pañ‍cahi katā, taina pañ‍casatāti ca|

thairaihaiva katattā ca, thairikāti pavuc‍catī’’ti||



1. brahmajālasuttavaṇṇanā

paribbājakakathāvaṇṇanā

imissā  paṭhamamahāsaṅgītiyā vattamānāya vinayasaṅgahāvasānai suttantapiṭakai ādinikāyassa ādisuttaṃ brahmajālaṃ pucchantaina āyasmatā mahākassapaina – ‘‘brahmajālaṃ, āvusau ānanda, kattha bhāsita’’nti, aivamādivuttavacanapariyausānai yattha ca bhāsitaṃ, yañ‍cārabbha bhāsitaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ pakāsaintau āyasmā ānandau aivaṃ mai sutantiādimāha| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘brahmajālassāpi aivaṃ mai sutantiādikaṃ āyasmatā ānandaina paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālai vuttaṃ nidānamādī’’ti|

1. tattha aivanti nipātapadaṃ| maitiādīni nāmapadāni| paṭipan‍nau hautīti aittha paṭīti upasaggapadaṃ, hautīti ākhyātapadanti| iminā tāva nayaina padavibhāgau vaiditabbau|

atthatau pana aivaṃ-saddau tāva upamūpadaisasampahaṃsanagarahaṇavacanasampaṭiggahākāranidassanāvadhāraṇādianaikatthappabhaidau| tathāhaisa – ‘‘aivaṃ jātaina mac‍caina, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ bahu’’nti (dha॰ pa॰ 53) aivamādīsu upamāyaṃ āgatau| ‘‘aivaṃ tai abhik‍kamitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai paṭik‍kamitabba’’ntiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 4.122) upadaisai| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ sugatā’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 3.66) sampahaṃsanai| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ panāyaṃ vasalī yasmiṃ vā tasmiṃ vā tassa muṇḍakassa  samaṇakassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsatī’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.187) garahaṇai| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantaiti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausu’’ntiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.1) vacanasampaṭiggahai| ‘‘aivaṃ byā khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.398) ākārai| ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, māṇavaka, yaina samaṇau ānandau tainupasaṅkama, upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina samaṇaṃ ānandaṃ appābādhaṃ  appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha| ‘‘subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau bhavantaṃ ānandaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’’ti| ‘‘aivañ‍ca vadaihi, sādhu kira bhavaṃ ānandau yaina subhassa  māṇavassa taudaiyyaputtassa nivaisanaṃ, tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.445) nidassanai| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, kālāmā, imai dhammā kusalā vā akusalā vāti? akusalā, bhantai| sāvaj‍jā vā anavaj‍jā vāti? sāvaj‍jā, bhantai| viñ‍ñugarahitā vā viñ‍ñuppasatthā vāti? viñ‍ñugarahitā, bhantai| samattā samādin‍nā ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti nau vā, kathaṃ vau aittha hautīti? samattā, bhantai, samādin‍nā ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti, aivaṃ nau aittha hautī’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 3.66) avadhāraṇai| svāyamidha ākāranidassanāvadhāraṇaisu daṭṭhabbau|

tattha ākāratthaina aivaṃ-saddaina aitamatthaṃ dīpaiti, nānānayanipuṇamanaikajjhāsayasamuṭṭhānaṃ, atthabyañ‍janasampan‍naṃ, vividhapāṭihāriyaṃ, dhammatthadaisanāpaṭivaidhagambhīraṃ, sabbasattānaṃ sakasakabhāsānurūpatau sautapathamāgacchantaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ sabbappakāraina kau samatthau viñ‍ñātuṃ, sabbathāmaina pana sautukāmataṃ janaitvāpi ‘aivaṃ mai sutaṃ’ mayāpi aikainākāraina sutanti|

nidassanatthaina – ‘‘nāhaṃ sayambhū, na mayā idaṃ sacchikata’’nti attānaṃ parimaucaintau – ‘aivaṃ mai sutaṃ’, ‘mayāpi aivaṃ suta’nti idāni vattabbaṃ sakalaṃ suttaṃ nidassaiti|

avadhāraṇatthaina – ‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, mama sāvakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahussutānaṃ yadidaṃ ānandau, gatimantānaṃ, satimantānaṃ, dhitimantānaṃ, upaṭṭhākānaṃ yadidaṃ  ānandau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 1.223)| aivaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘āyasmā ānandau atthakusalau, dhammakusalau, byañ‍janakusalau, niruttikusalau, pubbāparakusalau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 5.169)| aivaṃ dhammasaināpatinā ca pasatthabhāvānurūpaṃ attanau dhāraṇabalaṃ dassaintau sattānaṃ sautukāmataṃ janaiti – ‘aivaṃ mai sutaṃ’, tañ‍ca khau atthatau vā byañ‍janatau vā anūnamanadhikaṃ, aivamaiva na añ‍ñathā daṭṭhabba’’nti|

mai-saddau tīsu atthaisu dissati| tathā hissa – ‘‘gāthābhigītaṃ mai abhaujanaiyya’’ntiādīsu (su॰ ni॰ 81) mayāti atthau| ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṅkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitū’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.88) mayhanti atthau| ‘‘dhammadāyādā mai, bhikkhavai, bhavathā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.29) mamāti atthau| idha pana mayā sutanti ca, mama sutanti ca atthadvayai yuj‍jati|

sutanti ayaṃ suta-saddau saupasaggau ca anupasaggau ca – gamanavissutakilin‍na-upacitānuyauga-sautaviñ‍ñaiyya-sautadvārānusāra-viñ‍ñātādianaikatthappabhaidau, tathā hissa ‘‘saināya pasutau’’tiādīsu gacchantauti atthau| ‘‘sutadhammassa passatau’’tiādīsu (udā॰ 11) vissutadhammassāti atthau| ‘‘avassutā avassutassā’’tiādīsu (pāci॰ 657) kilin‍nākilin‍nassāti atthau| ‘‘tumhaihi puñ‍ñaṃ pasutaṃ anappaka’’ntiādīsu (khu॰ pā॰ 7.12) upacitanti atthau| ‘‘yai jhānapasutā dhīrā’’tiādīsu (dha॰ pa॰ 181) jhānānuyuttāti atthau| ‘diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ muta’ntiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.241) sautaviñ‍ñaiyyanti atthau| ‘‘sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.339) sautadvārānusāraviñ‍ñātadharauti atthau| idha panassa sautadvārānusāraina upadhāritanti vā upadhāraṇanti vāti atthau| ‘mai’ saddassa hi ‘mayā’ti atthai sati ‘aivaṃ mayā sutaṃ’ sautadvārānusāraina upadhāritanti yuj‍jati| ‘mamā’ti atthai sati aivaṃ mama sutaṃ sautadvārānusāraina upadhāraṇanti yuj‍jati|

aivamaitaisu tīsu padaisu aivanti sautaviñ‍ñāṇādiviñ‍ñāṇakic‍canidassanaṃ| maiti vuttaviñ‍ñāṇasamaṅgipuggalanidassanaṃ| sutanti assavanabhāvapaṭikkhaipatau anūnādhikāviparītaggahaṇanidassanaṃ| tathā aivanti tassā sautadvārānusāraina pavattāya viñ‍ñāṇavīthiyā nānappakāraina ārammaṇai pavattibhāvappakāsanaṃ| maiti attappakāsanaṃ| sutanti dhammappakāsanaṃ| ayañhaittha saṅkhaipau  – ‘‘nānappakāraina ārammaṇai pavattāya viñ‍ñāṇavīthiyā mayā na añ‍ñaṃ kataṃ, idaṃ pana kataṃ, ayaṃ dhammau sutau’’ti|

tathā aivanti niddisitabbadhammappakāsanaṃ| maiti puggalappakāsanaṃ| sutanti puggalakic‍cappakāsanaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘yaṃ suttaṃ niddisissāmi, taṃ mayā aivaṃ suta’’nti|

tathā aivanti yassa cittasantānassa nānākārappavattiyā nānatthabyañ‍janaggahaṇaṃ hauti, tassa nānākāraniddaisau| aivanti hi ayamākārapañ‍ñatti| maiti kattuniddaisau| sutanti visayaniddaisau| aittāvatā nānākārappavattaina  cittasantānaina taṃ samaṅginau kattu visayaggahaṇasan‍niṭṭhānaṃ kataṃ hauti|

athavā  aivanti puggalakic‍caniddaisau| sutanti viñ‍ñāṇakic‍caniddaisau| maiti ubhayakic‍cayuttapuggalaniddaisau| ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipau, ‘‘mayā savanakic‍caviñ‍ñāṇasamaṅginā puggalaina viñ‍ñāṇavasaina laddhasavanakic‍cavauhāraina suta’’nti|

tattha aivanti ca maiti ca sac‍cikaṭṭhaparamatthavasaina avij‍jamānapañ‍ñatti| kiñhaittha taṃ paramatthatau atthi, yaṃ aivanti vā maiti vā niddaisaṃ labhaitha? sutanti vij‍jamānapañ‍ñatti| yañhi taṃ aittha sautaina upaladdhaṃ, taṃ paramatthatau vij‍jamānanti| tathā ‘aiva’nti ca, maiti ca, taṃ taṃ upādāya vattabbatau upādāpañ‍ñatti| ‘suta’nti diṭṭhādīni upanidhāya vattabbatau upanidhāpañ‍ñatti| aittha ca aivanti vacanaina asammauhaṃ dīpaiti| na hi sammūḷhau nānappakārapaṭivaidhasamatthau hauti| ‘suta’nti vacanaina sutassa asammausaṃ dīpaiti| yassa hi sutaṃ sammuṭṭhaṃ hauti, na sau kālantaraina mayā sutanti paṭijānāti| ic‍cassa asammauhaina pañ‍ñāsiddhi, asammausaina pana satisiddhi| tattha pañ‍ñāpubbaṅgamāya satiyā byañ‍janāvadhāraṇasamatthatā, satipubbaṅgamāya pañ‍ñāya atthapaṭivaidhasamatthatā| tadubhayasamatthatāyaugaina atthabyañ‍janasampan‍nassa dhammakausassa anupālanasamatthatau dhammabhaṇḍāgārikattasiddhi|

aparau nayau, aivanti vacanaina yaunisau manasikāraṃ dīpaiti| ayaunisau manasikarautau hi nānappakārapaṭivaidhābhāvatau| sutanti vacanaina avikkhaipaṃ dīpaiti, vikkhittacittassa savanābhāvatau| tathā hi vikkhittacittau puggalau sabbasampattiyā vuc‍camānaupi  ‘‘na mayā sutaṃ, puna bhaṇathā’’ti bhaṇati| yaunisau manasikāraina caittha attasammāpaṇidhiṃ pubbai ca katapuñ‍ñataṃ sādhaiti, sammā appaṇihitattassa pubbai akatapuñ‍ñassa vā tadabhāvatau| avikkhaipaina saddhammassavanaṃ sappurisūpanissayañ‍ca sādhaiti| na hi vikkhittacittau sautuṃ sak‍kauti, na ca sappurisai anupassayamānassa savanaṃ atthīti|

aparau  nayau, yasmā aivanti yassa cittasantānassa nānākārappavattiyā nānatthabyañ‍janaggahaṇaṃ hauti, tassa nānākāraniddaisauti vuttaṃ, sau ca aivaṃ bhaddakau ākārau na sammāappaṇihitattanau pubbai akatapuñ‍ñassa vā hauti, tasmā aivanti iminā bhaddakainākāraina pacchimacak‍kadvayasampattimattanau dīpaiti| sutanti savanayaugaina purimacak‍kadvayasampattiṃ| na hi appatirūpadaisai vasatau sappurisūpanissayavirahitassa vā savanaṃ atthi| ic‍cassa pacchimacak‍kadvayasiddhiyā āsayasuddhisiddhā hauti, purimacak‍kadvayasiddhiyā payaugasuddhi, tāya ca āsayasuddhiyā adhigamabyattisiddhi, payaugasuddhiyā āgamabyattisiddhi| iti  payaugāsayasuddhassa āgamādhigamasampan‍nassa vacanaṃ aruṇuggaṃ viya sūriyassa udayatau yaunisau manasikārau viya ca kusalakammassa arahati bhagavatau vacanassa pubbaṅgamaṃ bhavitunti ṭhānai nidānaṃ ṭhapaintau – ‘‘aivaṃ mai suta’’ntiādimāha|

aparau nayau, ‘aiva’nti iminā nānappakārapaṭivaidhadīpakaina vacanaina attanau atthapaṭibhānapaṭisambhidāsampattisabbhāvaṃ dīpaiti| ‘suta’nti iminā sautabbappabhaidapaṭivaidhadīpakaina dhammaniruttipaṭisambhidāsampattisabbhāvaṃ| ‘aiva’nti ca idaṃ yaunisau manasikāradīpakaṃ vacanaṃ bhāsamānau – ‘‘aitai mayā dhammā manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā’’ti dīpaiti| ‘suta’nti idaṃ savanayaugadīpakaṃ vacanaṃ bhāsamānau – ‘‘bahū mayā dhammā sutā dhātā vacasā paricitā’’ti dīpaiti| tadubhayaināpi atthabyañ‍janapāripūriṃ dīpaintau savanai ādaraṃ janaiti| atthabyañ‍janaparipuṇṇañhi dhammaṃ ādaraina assuṇantau mahatā hitā paribāhirau hautīti, tasmā ādaraṃ janaitvā sak‍kac‍caṃ ayaṃ dhammau sautabbauti|

‘‘aivaṃ  mai suta’’nti iminā pana sakalaina vacanaina āyasmā ānandau tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammaṃ attanau adahantau asappurisabhūmiṃ atik‍kamati| sāvakattaṃ paṭijānantau sappurisabhūmiṃ auk‍kamati| tathā asaddhammā cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, saddhammai cittaṃ patiṭṭhāpaiti| ‘‘kaivalaṃ sutamaivaitaṃ mayā, tassaiva bhagavatau vacana’’nti dīpaintau attānaṃ parimaucaiti, satthāraṃ apadisati, jinavacanaṃ appaiti, dhammanaittiṃ patiṭṭhāpaiti|

apica  ‘‘aivaṃ mai suta’’nti attanā uppāditabhāvaṃ appaṭijānantau purimavacanaṃ vivarantau – ‘‘sammukhā paṭiggahitamidaṃ mayā tassa bhagavatau catuvaisāraj‍javisāradassa dasabaladharassa āsabhaṭṭhānaṭṭhāyinau sīhanādanādinau sabbasattuttamassa dhammissarassa dhammarājassa dhammādhipatinau dhammadīpassa dhammasaraṇassa saddhammavaracak‍kavattinau sammāsambuddhassa vacanaṃ, na aittha atthai vā dhammai vā padai vā byañ‍janai vā kaṅkhā vā vimati vā kātabbā’’ti sabbaisaṃ daivamanussānaṃ imasmiṃ dhammai assaddhiyaṃ vināsaiti, saddhāsampadaṃ uppādaiti| tainaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘vināsayati assaddhaṃ, saddhaṃ vaḍḍhaiti sāsanai|

aivaṃ mai sutamic‍caivaṃ, vadaṃ gautamasāvakau’’ti||

aikanti  gaṇanaparicchaidaniddaisau| samayanti paricchin‍naniddaisau| aikaṃ samayanti aniyamitaparidīpanaṃ| tattha samayasaddau –

‘‘samavāyai khaṇai kālai, samūhai haitudiṭṭhisu|

paṭilābhai pahānai ca, paṭivaidhai ca dissati’’||

tathā hissa – ‘‘appaivanāma svaipi upasaṅkamaiyyāma kālañ‍ca samayañ‍ca upādāyā’’ti aivamādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.447) samavāyau atthau| ‘‘aikauva khau bhikkhavai, khaṇau ca samayau ca brahmacariyavāsāyā’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 8.29) khaṇau| ‘‘uṇhasamayau pariḷāhasamayau’’tiādīsu (pāci॰ 358) kālau| ‘‘mahāsamayau pavanasmi’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.332) samūhau| ‘‘samayaupi khau tai, bhaddāli, appaṭividdhau ahausi, bhagavā khau  sāvatthiyaṃ viharati, bhagavāpi maṃ jānissati, bhaddāli nāma bhikkhu satthusāsanai sikkhāya aparipūrakārī’ti| ayampi khau, tai bhaddāli, samayau appaṭividdhau ahausī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.135) haitu| ‘‘taina khau pana samayaina uggahamānau paribbājakau samaṇamuṇḍikāputtau samayappavādakai tindukācīrai aikasālakai mal‍likāya ārāmai paṭivasatī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.260) diṭṭhi|

‘‘diṭṭhai dhammai ca yau atthau, yau catthau samparāyikau|

atthābhisamayā dhīrau, paṇḍitauti pavuc‍catī’’ti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.128) –

ādīsu  paṭilābhau| ‘‘sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 7.9) pahānaṃ| ‘‘dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau saṅkhataṭṭhau santāpaṭṭhau vipariṇāmaṭṭhau abhisamayaṭṭhau’’tiādīsu (paṭi॰ 108) paṭivaidhau| idha panassa kālau atthau| taina saṃvaccharautumāsaḍḍhamāsarattidivapubbaṇhamajjhanhikasāyanhapaṭhamamajjhi-mapacchimayāmamuhuttādīsu kālappabhaidabhūtaisu samayaisu aikaṃ samayanti dīpaiti|

tattha kiñ‍cāpi aitaisu saṃvaccharādīsu samayaisu yaṃ yaṃ suttaṃ yasmiṃ yasmiṃ saṃvaccharai utumhi māsai pakkhai rattibhāgai vā divasabhāgai vā vuttaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ thairassa suviditaṃ suvavatthāpitaṃ pañ‍ñāya| yasmā pana – ‘‘aivaṃ mai sutaṃ’’ asukasaṃvaccharai asukautumhi asukamāsai asukapakkhai asukarattibhāgai asukadivasabhāgai vāti aivaṃ vuttai na sak‍kā sukhaina  dhāraituṃ vā uddisituṃ vā uddisāpaituṃ vā, bahu ca vattabbaṃ hauti, tasmā aikainaiva padaina tamatthaṃ samaudhānaitvā ‘‘aikaṃ samaya’’nti āha| yai vā imai gabbhauk‍kantisamayau, jātisamayau, saṃvaigasamayau, abhinikkhamanasamayau, duk‍karakārikasamayau, māravijayasamayau, abhisambaudhisamayau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārasamayau, daisanāsamayau, parinibbānasamayauti, aivamādayau bhagavatau daivamanussaisu ativiya pakāsā anaikakālappabhaidā aiva samayā| taisu samayaisu daisanāsamayasaṅkhātaṃ aikaṃ samayanti dīpaiti| yau cāyaṃ ñāṇakaruṇākic‍casamayaisu karuṇākic‍casamayau, attahitaparahitapaṭipattisamayaisu parahitapaṭipattisamayau, san‍nipatitānaṃ karaṇīyadvayasamayaisu dhammikathāsamayau daisanāpaṭipattisamayaisu  daisanāsamayau, taisupi samayaisu añ‍ñataraṃ samayaṃ sandhāya ‘‘aikaṃ samaya’’nti āha|

kasmā panaittha yathā abhidhammai ‘‘yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacara’’nti (dha॰ sa॰ 1) ca, itau añ‍ñaisu ca suttapadaisu – ‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihī’’ti ca bhummavacananiddaisau katau, vinayai ca – ‘‘taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā’’ti karaṇavacanaina, tathā akatvā ‘‘aikaṃ samaya’’nti upayaugavacananiddaisau katauti? tattha tathā idha ca añ‍ñathā atthasambhavatau| tattha hi abhidhammai itau añ‍ñaisu suttapadaisu ca adhikaraṇatthau  bhāvaina bhāvalakkhaṇatthau ca sambhavati| adhikaraṇañhi kālatthau, samūhatthau ca samayau, tattha tattha vuttānaṃ phassādidhammānaṃ khaṇasamavāyahaitusaṅkhātassa ca samayassa bhāvaina taisaṃ bhāvau lakkhīyati, tasmā tadatthajautanatthaṃ tattha bhummavacananiddaisau katau|

vinayai ca haituatthau karaṇatthau ca sambhavati| yau hi sau sikkhāpadapañ‍ñattisamayau sāriputtādīhipi dubbiñ‍ñaiyyau, taina samayaina haitubhūtaina karaṇabhūtaina ca sikkhāpadāni pañ‍ñāpayantau sikkhāpadapañ‍ñattihaituñ‍ca apaikkhamānau bhagavā tattha tattha vihāsi, tasmā tadatthajautanatthaṃ tattha karaṇavacanaina niddaisau katau|

idha pana añ‍ñasmiñ‍ca aivaṃ jātikai ac‍cantasaṃyaugatthau sambhavati| yañhi samayaṃ bhagavā imaṃ añ‍ñaṃ vā suttantaṃ daisaisi, ac‍cantamaiva taṃ samayaṃ karuṇāvihāraina vihāsi, tasmā tadatthajautanatthaṃ idha upayaugavacananiddaisau katauti|

tainaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘taṃ  taṃ atthamapaikkhitvā, bhummaina karaṇaina ca|

añ‍ñatra samayau vuttau, upayaugaina sau idhā’’ti||

paurāṇā pana vaṇṇayanti – ‘‘tasmiṃ samayai’’ti vā, ‘‘taina samayainā’’ti vā, ‘‘aikaṃ samaya’’nti vā, abhilāpamattabhaidau aisa, sabbattha bhummamaivatthauti| tasmā ‘‘aikaṃ samaya’’nti vuttaipi ‘‘aikasmiṃ samayai’’ti atthau vaiditabbau|

bhagavāti garu| garuñhi laukai bhagavāti vadanti| ayañ‍ca  sabbaguṇavisiṭṭhatāya sabbasattānaṃ garu, tasmā bhagavāti vaiditabbau| paurāṇaihipi vuttaṃ –

‘‘bhagavāti vacanaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, bhagavāti vacanamuttamaṃ|

garu gāravayuttau sau, bhagavā taina vuc‍catī’’ti||

api ca –

‘‘bhāgyavā bhaggavā yuttau, bhagaihi ca vibhattavā|

bhattavā vantagamanau, bhavaisu bhagavā tatau’’ti||

imissā  gāthāya vasainassa padassa vitthāraatthau vaiditabbau| sau ca visuddhimaggai buddhānussatiniddaisai vuttauyaiva|

aittāvatā caittha aivaṃ mai sutanti vacanaina yathāsutaṃ dhammaṃ dassaintau bhagavatau dhammakāyaṃ pac‍cakkhaṃ karauti| taina ‘‘nayidaṃ atik‍kantasatthukaṃ pāvacanaṃ, ayaṃ vau satthā’’ti satthu adassanaina uk‍kaṇṭhitaṃ janaṃ samassāsaiti|

aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavāti vacanaina tasmiṃ samayai bhagavatau avij‍jamānabhāvaṃ dassaintau rūpakāyaparinibbānaṃ sādhaiti| taina ‘‘aivaṃvidhassa nāma ariyadhammassa daisakau dasabaladharau vajirasaṅghāta samānakāyau saupi bhagavā parinibbutau, kaina añ‍ñaina jīvitai āsā janaitabbā’’ti jīvitamadamattaṃ janaṃ saṃvaijaiti, saddhammai cassa ussāhaṃ janaiti|

aivanti  ca bhaṇantau daisanāsampattiṃ niddisati| mai sutanti sāvakasampattiṃ| aikaṃ samayanti kālasampattiṃ| bhagavāti daisakasampattiṃ|

antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca nāḷandanti antarā-saddau kāraṇakhaṇacittavaimajjhavivarādīsu dissati| ‘‘tadantaraṃ kau jānaiyya añ‍ñatra tathāgatā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.44) ca, ‘‘janā saṅgamma mantainti mañ‍ca tañ‍ca kimantara’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.228) ca ādīsu hi kāraṇai antarā-saddau| ‘‘addasa maṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatarā itthī vij‍jantarikāya bhājanaṃ dhauvantī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.149) khaṇai| ‘‘yassantaratau na santi kaupā’’tiādīsu (udā॰ 20) cittai| ‘‘antarā vausānamāpādī’’tiādīsu  (cūḷava॰ 350) vaimajjhai| ‘‘api cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, tapaudā dvin‍naṃ mahānirayānaṃ antarikāya āgacchatī’’tiādīsu (pārā॰ 231) vivarai| svāyamidha vivarai vattati, tasmā rājagahassa ca nāḷandāya ca vivaraiti aivamaitthatthau vaiditabbau| antarā-saddaina pana yuttattā upayaugavacanaṃ kataṃ| īdisaisu ca ṭhānaisu akkharacintakā ‘‘antarā gāmañ‍ca nadiñ‍ca yātī’’ti aivaṃ aikamaiva antarāsaddaṃ payuj‍janti, sau dutiyapadainapi yaujaitabbau hauti, ayaujiyamānai upayaugavacanaṃ na pāpuṇāti| idha pana yaujaitvāyaiva vuttauti|

addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau  hautīti addhānasaṅkhātaṃ maggaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti, ‘‘dīghamagga’’nti atthau| addhānagamanasamayassa hi vibhaṅgai ‘‘aḍḍhayaujanaṃ gacchissāmīti bhuñ‍jitabba’’ntiādivacanatau (pāci॰ 218) aḍḍhayaujanampi addhānamaggau hauti| rājagahatau pana nāḷandā yaujanamaiva|

mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhinti ‘mahatā’ti guṇamahattainapi mahatā, saṅkhyāmahattainapi mahatā| sau hi bhikkhusaṅghau guṇaihipi mahā ahausi, appicchatādiguṇasaman‍nāgatattā| saṅkhyāyapi mahā, pañ‍casatasaṅkhyattā| bhikkhūnaṃ saṅghau ‘bhikkhusaṅghau’, taina bhikkhusaṅghaina| diṭṭhisīlasāmañ‍ñasaṅghātasaṅkhātaina samaṇagaṇaināti atthau| saddhinti aikatau|

pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihīti pañ‍camattā aitaisanti pañ‍camattāni| mattāti pamāṇaṃ vuc‍cati, tasmā yathā ‘‘bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū’’ti vuttai ‘‘bhaujanai mattaṃ jānāti, pamāṇaṃ jānātī’’ti atthau hauti, aivamidhāpi – ‘‘taisaṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ pañ‍camattā pañ‍capamāṇa’’nti aivamatthau daṭṭhabbau| bhikkhūnaṃ satāni bhikkhusatāni, taihi pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi|

suppiyaupi  khau paribbājakauti suppiyauti tassa nāmaṃ| pi-kārau maggappaṭipan‍nasabhāgatāya puggalasampiṇḍanatthau| khau-kārau padasandhikarau, byañ‍janasiliṭṭhatāvasaina vuttau| paribbājakauti sañ‍jayassa antaivāsī chan‍naparibbājakau| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – ‘‘yadā bhagavā taṃ addhānamaggaṃ  paṭipan‍nau, tadā suppiyaupi paribbājakau paṭipan‍nau ahausī’’ti| atītakālatthau haittha hauti-saddau|

saddhiṃ antaivāsinā brahmadattaina māṇavaināti – aittha antai vasatīti antaivāsī| samīpacārau santikāvacarau sissauti atthau| brahmadattauti tassa nāmaṃ| māṇavauti sattaupi cauraupi taruṇaupi vuc‍cati|

‘‘cauditā daivadūtaihi, yai pamaj‍janti māṇavā|

tai dīgharattaṃ saucanti, hīnakāyūpagā narā’’ti|| (ma॰ ni॰ 3.271) –

ādīsu  hi sattau māṇavauti vuttau| ‘‘māṇavaihipi samāgacchanti katakammaihipi akatakammaihipī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.149) caurau| ‘‘ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, aṅgakau māṇavau’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.316) taruṇau ‘māṇavau’ti vuttau| idhāpi ayamaivatthau| idañhi vuttaṃ hauti – brahmadattaina nāma taruṇantaivāsinā saddhinti|

tatrāti tasmiṃ addhānamaggai, taisu vā dvīsu janaisu| sudanti nipātamattaṃ| anaikapariyāyaināti pariyāya-saddau tāva vāradaisanākāraṇaisu vattati| ‘‘kassa nu khau, ānanda, aj‍ja pariyāyau bhikkhuniyau auvaditu’’ntiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 3.398) hi vārai pariyāyasaddau vattati| ‘‘madhupiṇḍikapariyāyautvaiva naṃ dhāraihī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.205) daisanāyaṃ| ‘‘imināpi khau, tai rājañ‍ña, pariyāyaina aivaṃ hautū’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.411) kāraṇai| svāyamidhāpi kāraṇai vattati, tasmā ayamaittha atthau – ‘‘anaikavidhaina kāraṇainā’’ti, ‘‘bahūhi kāraṇaihī’’ti vuttaṃ hauti|

buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsatīti avaṇṇavirahitassa aparimāṇavaṇṇasaman‍nāgatassāpi buddhassa bhagavatau – ‘‘yaṃ laukai jātivuḍḍhaisu kattabbaṃ abhivādanādisāmīcikammaṃ ‘sāmaggirasau’ti vuc‍cati, taṃ samaṇassa gautamassa natthi tasmā arasarūpau samaṇau gautamau, nibbhaugau, akiriyavādau, ucchaidavādau, jaigucchī, vainayikau, tapassī, apagabbhau| natthi samaṇassa gautamassa  uttarimanussadhammau alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau| tak‍kapariyāhataṃ samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaiti, vīmaṃsānucaritaṃ, sayaṃpaṭibhānaṃ| samaṇau gautamau na sabbañ‍ñū, na laukavidū, na anuttarau, na aggapuggalau’’ti| aivaṃ taṃ taṃ akāraṇamaiva kāraṇanti vatvā  tathā tathā avaṇṇaṃ dausaṃ nindaṃ bhāsati|

yathā ca buddhassa, aivaṃ dhammassāpi taṃ taṃ akāraṇamaiva kāraṇatau vatvā – ‘‘samaṇassa gautamassa dhammau durakkhātau, duppaṭivaiditau, aniyyānikau, anupasamasaṃvattanikau’’ti tathā tathā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati|

yathā  ca dhammassa, aivaṃ saṅghassāpi yaṃ vā taṃ vā akāraṇamaiva kāraṇatau vatvā – ‘‘micchāpaṭipan‍nau samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakasaṅghau, kuṭilapaṭipan‍nau, pac‍canīkapaṭipadaṃ ananulaumapaṭipadaṃ adhammānulaumapaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau’’ti tathā tathā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati|

antaivāsī panassa – ‘‘amhākaṃ ācariyau aparāmasitabbaṃ parāmasati, anak‍kamitabbaṃ ak‍kamati, svāyaṃ aggiṃ gilantau viya, hatthaina asidhāraṃ parāmasantau viya, muṭṭhinā sinairuṃ padālaitukāmau viya, kakacadantapantiyaṃ kīḷamānau viya, pabhin‍namadaṃ caṇḍahatthiṃ hatthaina gaṇhantau viya ca vaṇṇārahassaiva ratanattayassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsamānau anayabyasanaṃ pāpuṇissati| ācariyai khau pana gūthaṃ vā aggiṃ vā kaṇṭakaṃ vā kaṇhasappaṃ vā ak‍kamantai, sūlaṃ vā abhirūhantai, halāhalaṃ vā visaṃ khādantai, khāraudakaṃ vā pakkhalantai, narakapapātaṃ vā papatantai, na antaivāsinā taṃ sabbamanukātabbaṃ hauti| kammassakā hi sattā attanau kammānurūpamaiva gatiṃ gacchanti| naiva pitā puttassa kammaina gacchati, na puttau pitu kammaina, na mātā puttassa, na puttau mātuyā, na bhātā bhaginiyā, na bhaginī bhātu, na ācariyau antaivāsinau, na antaivāsī ācariyassa kammaina gacchati| mayhañ‍ca ācariyau tiṇṇaṃ ratanānaṃ avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, mahāsāvaj‍jau khau panāriyūpavādauti| aivaṃ yaunisau ummuj‍jitvā ācariyavādaṃ maddamānau sammākāraṇamaiva kāraṇatau apadisantau anaikapariyāyaina tiṇṇaṃ ratanānaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitumāraddhau, yathā taṃ paṇḍitajātikau kulaputtau’’| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘suppiyassa pana paribbājakassa antaivāsī brahmadattau māṇavau anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsatī’’ti|

tattha vaṇṇanti vaṇṇa-saddau saṇṭhāna-jāti-rūpāyatana-kāraṇa-pamāṇa-guṇa-pasaṃsādīsu dissati| tattha ‘‘mahantaṃ sapparājavaṇṇaṃ  abhinimminitvā’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.142) saṇṭhānaṃ vuc‍cati| ‘‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.402) jāti| ‘‘paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.303) rūpāyatanaṃ|

‘‘na  harāmi na bhañ‍jāmi, ārā siṅghāmi vārijaṃ|

atha kaina nu vaṇṇaina, gandhatthainauti vuc‍catī’’ti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.234) –

ādīsu kāraṇaṃ| ‘‘tayau pattassa vaṇṇā’’tiādīsu (pārā॰ 602) pamāṇaṃ| ‘‘kadā sañ‍ñūḷhā pana, tai gahapati, imai samaṇassa gautamassa vaṇṇā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.77) guṇau| ‘‘vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsatī’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 2.135) pasaṃsā| idha guṇaupi pasaṃsāpi| ayaṃ kira taṃ taṃ bhūtamaiva kāraṇaṃ apadisantau anaikapariyāyaina ratanattayassa guṇūpasañhitaṃ pasaṃsaṃ abhāsi| tattha – ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’tiādinā (pārā॰ 1) nayaina, ‘‘yai bhikkhavai, buddhai pasan‍nā aggai tai pasan‍nā’’tiādinā ‘‘aikapuggalau, bhikkhavai, laukai uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… asamau asamasamau’’tiādinā (a॰ ni॰ 1.174) ca nayaina buddhassa vaṇṇau vaiditabbau| ‘‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.159) ca ‘‘ālayasamugghātau vaṭṭupacchaidau’’ti (iti॰ 90, a॰ ni॰ 4.34) ca, ‘‘yai bhikkhavai, ariyai aṭṭhaṅgikai maggai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā’’ti ca aivamādīhi nayaihi dhammassa vaṇṇau vaiditabbau| ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.159) ca, ‘‘yai, bhikkhavai, saṅghai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.34) ca aivamādīhi pana nayaihi saṅghassa vaṇṇau vaiditabbau| pahauntaina pana dhammakathikaina pañ‍canikāyai navaṅgaṃ satthusāsanaṃ caturāsītidhammakkhandhasahassāni augāhitvā buddhādīnaṃ vaṇṇau pakāsaitabbau| imasmiñhi ṭhānai buddhādīnaṃ guṇai pakāsaintau atitthaina pakkhandau dhammakathikauti na sak‍kā vattuṃ| īdisaisu hi ṭhānaisu dhammakathikassa thāmau vaiditabbau| brahmadattau pana māṇavau anussavādimattasambandhitaina attanau thāmaina ratanattayassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati|

itiha tai ubhau ācariyantaivāsīti aivaṃ tai dvai ācariyantaivāsikā| añ‍ñamañ‍ñassāti añ‍ñau añ‍ñassa| ujuvipac‍canīkavādāti īsakampi apariharitvā ujumaiva vividhapac‍canīkavādā, anaikavāraṃ viruddhavādā aiva hutvāti  atthau| ācariyaina hi ratanattayassa  avaṇṇai bhāsitai antaivāsī vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, puna itarau avaṇṇaṃ, itarau vaṇṇanti aivaṃ ācariyau sāraphalakai visarukkhaāṇiṃ ākauṭayamānau viya  punappunaṃ ratanattayassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| antaivāsī pana suvaṇṇarajatamaṇimayāya āṇiyā taṃ āṇiṃ paṭibāhayamānau viya punappunaṃ ratanattayassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘ujuvipac‍canīkavādā’’ti|

bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhā haunti bhikkhusaṅghañ‍cāti bhagavantañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca pacchatau pacchatau dassanaṃ avijahantā iriyāpathānubandhanaina anubandhā haunti, sīsānulaukinau hutvā anugatā hauntīti atthau|

kasmā pana bhagavā taṃ addhānaṃ paṭipan‍nau? kasmā ca suppiyau anubandhau? kasmā ca sau ratanattayassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsatīti? bhagavā tāva tasmiṃ kālai rājagahaparivattakaisu aṭṭhārasasu mahāvihāraisu añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vasitvā pātauva sarīrappaṭijagganaṃ katvā bhikkhācāravailāyaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau rājagahai piṇḍāya carati| sau taṃ divasaṃ bhikkhusaṅghassa sulabhapiṇḍapātaṃ katvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pattacīvaraṃ gāhāpaitvā – ‘‘nāḷandaṃ gamissāmī’’ti, rājagahatau nikkhamitvā taṃ addhānaṃ paṭipan‍nau| suppiyaupi khau tasmiṃ kālai rājagahaparivattakai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ paribbājakārāmai vasitvā paribbājakaparivutau rājagahai bhikkhāya carati| saupi taṃ divasaṃ paribbājakaparisāya sulabhabhikkhaṃ katvā bhuttapātarāsau paribbājakai paribbājakaparikkhāraṃ gāhāpaitvā – nāḷandaṃ gamissāmic‍caiva bhagavatau taṃ maggaṃ paṭipan‍nabhāvaṃ ajānantauva anubandhau| sacai pana jānaiyya nānubandhaiyya| sau ajānitvāva gacchantau gīvaṃ ukkhipitvā aulaukayamānau bhagavantaṃ addasa buddhasiriyā saubhamānaṃ rattakambalaparikkhittamiva jaṅgamakanakagirisikharaṃ|

tasmiṃ kira samayai dasabalassa sarīratau nikkhamitvā chabbaṇṇarasmiyau samantā asītihatthappamāṇai padaisai ādhāvanti vidhāvanti ratanāvaiḷaratanadāmaratanacuṇṇavippakiṇṇaṃ  viya, pasāritaratanacittakañ‍canapaṭamiva, rattasuvaṇṇarasanisiñ‍camānamiva, uk‍kāsatanipātasamākulamiva, nirantaravippakiṇṇakaṇikārapupphamiva vāyuvaigakkhittacīnapiṭṭhacuṇṇamiva, indadhanuvij‍julatātārāgaṇappabhāvisaravipphuritaviccharitamiva ca taṃ vanantaraṃ hauti|

asīti  anubyañ‍janānurañ‍jitañ‍ca pana bhagavatau sarīraṃ vikasitakamaluppalamiva, saraṃ sabbapāliphul‍lamiva  pāricchattakaṃ, tārāmarīcivikasitamiva, gaganatalaṃ siriyā avahasantamiva, byāmappabhāparikkhaipavilāsinī cassa dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇamālā ganthaitvā ṭhapitadvattiṃsacandamālāya dvattiṃsasūriyamālāya paṭipāṭiyā ṭhapitadvattiṃsacak‍kavattidvattiṃsasak‍kadaivarājadvattiṃsamahābrahmānaṃ siriṃ siriyā abhibhavantimiva| tañ‍ca pana bhagavantaṃ parivāraitvā ṭhitā bhikkhū sabbaiva appicchā santuṭṭhā pavivittā asaṃsaṭṭhā caudakā pāpagarahinau vattārau vacanakkhamā sīlasampan‍nā samādhipañ‍ñāvimuttivimuttiñ‍ñāṇadassanasampan‍nā| taisaṃ majjhai bhagavā rattakambalapākāraparikkhittau viya kañ‍canathambhau, rattapadumasaṇḍamajjhagatā viya suvaṇṇanāvā, pavāḷavaidikāparikkhittau viya aggikkhandhau, tārāgaṇaparivāritau viya puṇṇacandau migapakkhīnampi cakkhūni pīṇayati, pagaiva daivamanussānaṃ| tasmiñ‍ca pana divasai yaibhuyyaina asītimahāthairā maighavaṇṇaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ aikaṃsaṃ karitvā kattaradaṇḍaṃ ādāya suvammavammitā viya gandhahatthinau vigatadausā vantadausā bhin‍nakilaisā vijaṭitajaṭā chin‍nabandhanā bhagavantaṃ parivārayiṃsu| sau sayaṃ vītarāgau vītarāgaihi, sayaṃ vītadausau vītadausaihi, sayaṃ vītamauhau vītamauhaihi, sayaṃ vītataṇhau vītataṇhaihi, sayaṃ nik‍kilaisau nik‍kilaisaihi, sayaṃ buddhau anubuddhaihi parivāritau; pattaparivāritaṃ viya kaisaraṃ, kaisaraparivāritā viya kaṇṇikā, aṭṭhanāgasahassaparivāritau viya chaddantau nāgarājā, navutihaṃsasahassaparivāritau viya dhataraṭṭhau haṃsarājā, sainaṅgaparivāritau viya cak‍kavattirājā, daivagaṇaparivāritau viya sak‍kau daivarājā, brahmagaṇaparivāritau viya hāritau mahābrahmā, aparimitakālasañ‍citapuñ‍ñabalanibbattāya acintaiyyāya anaupamāya buddhalīlāya  candau viya gaganatalaṃ taṃ maggaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti|

athaivaṃ bhagavantaṃ anaupamāya buddhalīlāya gacchantaṃ bhikkhū ca aukkhittacakkhū santindriyai santamānasai uparinabhai ṭhitaṃ puṇṇacandaṃ viya bhagavantaṃyaiva namassamānai disvāva paribbājakau attanau parisaṃ avalaukaisi| sā hauti kājadaṇḍakai aulambaitvā gahitauluggaviluggapiṭṭhakatidaṇḍamaurapiñchamattikāpattapasibbakakuṇḍikādianaikaparikkhārabhārabharitā| ‘‘asukassa hatthā saubhaṇā, asukassa pādā’’ti aivamādiniratthakavacanā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā adassanīyā apāsādikā| tassa taṃ disvā vippaṭisārau udapādi|

idāni taina bhagavatau vaṇṇau vattabbau bhavaiyya| yasmā panaisa lābhasak‍kārahāniyā caiva pakkhahāniyā ca nic‍campi bhagavantaṃ usūyati| añ‍ñatitthiyānañhi yāva buddhau laukai nuppaj‍jati, tāvadaiva lābhasak‍kārā nibbattanti, buddhuppādatau pana paṭṭhāya parihīnalābhasak‍kārā haunti, sūriyuggamanai khaj‍jaupanakā viya nissirīkataṃ āpaj‍janti| upatissakaulitānañ‍ca sañ‍jayassa santikai pabbajitakālaiyaiva paribbājakā mahāparisā ahaisuṃ, taisu pana pak‍kantaisu sāpi taisaṃ parisā bhin‍nā| iti imaihi dvīhi kāraṇaihi ayaṃ paribbājakau yasmā nic‍campi bhagavantaṃ usūyati, tasmā taṃ usūyavisuggāraṃ uggirantau ratanattayassa avaṇṇamaiva bhāsatīti vaiditabbau|

2. atha khau bhagavā ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ rājāgārakai aikarattivāsaṃ upagacchi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaināti bhagavā tāya buddhalīlāya gacchamānau anupubbaina ambalaṭṭhikādvāraṃ pāpuṇitvā sūriyaṃ aulaukaitvā – ‘‘akālau dāni gantuṃ, atthasamīpaṃ gatau sūriyau’’ti ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ rājāgārakai aikarattivāsaṃ upagacchi|

tattha ambalaṭṭhikāti rañ‍ñau uyyānaṃ| tassa kira dvārasamīpai taruṇaambarukkhau atthi, taṃ ‘‘ambalaṭṭhikā’’ti vadanti| tassa avidūrai bhavattā uyyānampi ambalaṭṭhikā tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gataṃ| taṃ chāyūdakasampan‍naṃ  pākāraparikkhittaṃ suyaujitadvāraṃ mañ‍jusā viya suguttaṃ| tattha rañ‍ñau kīḷanatthaṃ paṭibhānacittavicittaṃ agāraṃ akaṃsu| taṃ ‘‘rājāgāraka’’nti vuc‍cati|

suppiyaupi khauti suppiyaupi tasmiṃ ṭhānai sūriyaṃ aulaukaitvā – ‘‘akālau dāni gantuṃ, bahū khuddakamahal‍lakā paribbājakā, bahuparissayau ca ayaṃ maggau cauraihipi vāḷayakkhaihipi vāḷamigaihipi| ayaṃ khau pana samaṇau gautamau uyyānaṃ paviṭṭhau, samaṇassa ca gautamassa vasanaṭṭhānai  daivatā ārakkhaṃ gaṇhanti, handāhampi idha aikarattivāsaṃ upagantvā svaiva gamissāmī’’ti tadaivuyyānaṃ pāvisi| tatau bhikkhusaṅghau bhagavatau vattaṃ dassaitvā attanau attanau vasanaṭṭhānaṃ sal‍lakkhaisi| paribbājakaupi uyyānassa aikapassai paribbājakaparikkhārai autāraitvā vāsaṃ upagacchi saddhiṃ attanau parisāya| pāḷiyamārūḷhavasainaiva pana – ‘‘saddhiṃ attanau antaivāsinā brahmadattaina māṇavainā’’ti vuttaṃ|

aivaṃ vāsaṃ upagatau pana sau paribbājakau rattibhāgai dasabalaṃ aulaukaisi| tasmiñ‍ca samayai samantā vippakiṇṇatārakā viya padīpā jalanti, majjhai bhagavā nisin‍nau hauti, bhikkhusaṅghau ca bhagavantaṃ parivāraitvā| tattha aikabhikkhussapi hatthakuk‍kuc‍caṃ vā pādakuk‍kuc‍caṃ vā uk‍kāsitasaddau vā khipitasaddau vā natthi| sā hi parisā attanau ca sikkhitasikkhatāya satthari ca gāravaināti dvīhi kāraṇaihi nivātai padīpasikhā viya nic‍calā  san‍nisin‍nāva ahausi| paribbājakau taṃ vibhūtiṃ disvā attanau parisaṃ aulaukaisi| tattha kaici hatthaṃ khipanti, kaici pādaṃ, kaici vippalapanti, kaici nil‍lālitajivhā paggharitakhaiḷā, dantai khādantā kākacchamānā gharugharupassāsinau sayanti| sau ratanattayassa guṇavaṇṇai vattabbaipi issāvasaina puna avaṇṇamaiva ārabhi| brahmadattau pana vuttanayainaiva vaṇṇaṃ| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘tatrāpi sudaṃ suppiyau paribbājakau’’ti sabbaṃ vattabbaṃ| tattha tatrāpīti tasmimpi, ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ uyyānaiti atthau|

3. sambahulānanti bahukānaṃ| tattha vinayapariyāyaina tayau janā ‘‘sambahulā’’ti vuc‍canti| tatau paraṃ saṅghau| suttantapariyāyaina  pana tayau tayauva tatau paṭṭhāya sambahulā| idha suttantapariyāyaina ‘‘sambahulā’’ti vaiditabbā| maṇḍalamāḷaiti katthaci dvai kaṇṇikā gahaitvā haṃsavaṭṭakacchan‍naina katā kūṭāgārasālāpi ‘‘maṇḍalamāḷau’’ti vuc‍cati, katthaci aikaṃ kaṇṇikaṃ gahaitvā thambhapantiṃ parikkhipitvā katā upaṭṭhānasālāpi ‘‘maṇḍalamāḷau’’ti vuc‍cati| idha pana nisīdanasālā ‘‘maṇḍalamāḷau’’ti vaiditabbau| san‍nisin‍nānanti nisaj‍janavasaina| san‍nipatitānanti samaudhānavasaina| ayaṃ saṅkhiyadhammauti saṅkhiyā vuc‍cati kathā, kathādhammauti atthau| udapādīti uppan‍nau| katamau pana sauti? acchariyaṃ āvusauti aivamādi| tattha andhassa pabbatārauhaṇaṃ viya nic‍caṃ na hautīti acchariyaṃ| ayaṃ tāva saddanayau| ayaṃ pana aṭṭhakathānayau – accharāyaugganti acchariyaṃ| accharaṃ paharituṃ yuttanti atthau| abhūtapubbaṃ bhūtanti abbhutaṃ| ubhayaṃ paitaṃ vimhayassaivādhivacanaṃ| yāvañ‍cidanti yāva ca idaṃ taina suppaṭividitatāya appamaiyyattaṃ dassaiti|

taina bhagavatā jānatā…pai॰… suppaṭividitāti aitthāyaṃ saṅkhaipatthau| yau sau bhagavā samatiṃsa pāramiyau pūraitvā sabbakilaisai bhañ‍jitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau, taina bhagavatā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ āsayānusayaṃ jānatā, hatthatalai ṭhapitaṃ āmalakaṃ viya sabbañaiyyadhammaṃ passatā|

api ca pubbainivāsādīhi jānatā, dibbaina cakkhunā passatā| tīhi vij‍jāhi chahi vā pana abhiñ‍ñāhi jānatā, sabbattha appaṭihataina samantacakkhunā passatā|  sabbadhammajānanasamatthāya vā pañ‍ñāya jānatā, sabbasattānaṃ cakkhuvisayātītāni tiraukuṭṭādigatānipi rūpāni ativisuddhaina maṃsacakkhunā passatā| attahitasādhikāya vā samādhipadaṭṭhānāya  paṭivaidhapañ‍ñāya jānatā, parahitasādhikāya karuṇāpadaṭṭhānāya daisanāpañ‍ñāya passatā|

arīnaṃ hatattā pac‍cayādīnañ‍ca arahattā arahatā| sammā sāmañ‍ca sabbadhammānaṃ buddhattā sammāsambuddhaina antarāyikadhammai  vā jānatā, niyyānikadhammai passatā, kilaisārīnaṃ hatattā arahatā| sammā sāmañ‍ca sabbadhammānaṃ buddhattā sammāsambuddhaināti| aivaṃ catūvaisāraj‍javasaina catūhākāraihi thaumitaina sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikatā nānajjhāsayatā suppaṭividitā yāva ca suṭṭhu paṭividitā|
