idānissa suppaṭividitabhāvaṃ dassaituṃ ayañhītiādimāha| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti yā ca ayaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘dhātusau, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti, hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti| atītampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu, hīnādhimuttikā  hīnādhimuttikaihi…pai॰… kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu, anāgatampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ…pai॰… saṃsandissanti samaissanti, aitarahipi khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandanti samainti, hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi…pai॰… kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti aivaṃ sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikatā, nānajjhāsayatā, nānādiṭṭhikatā, nānākhantitā, nānārucitā, nāḷiyā minantaina viya tulāya tulayantaina viya ca nānādhimuttikatāñāṇaina sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina viditā, sā yāva suppaṭividitā| dvaipi nāma sattā aikajjhāsayā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ| aikasmiṃ gantukāmai aikau ṭhātukāmau hauti, aikasmiṃ pivitukāmai aikau bhuñ‍jitukāmau| imaisu cāpi dvīsu ācariyantaivāsīsu ayañhi ‘‘suppiyau paribbājakau…pai॰… bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhā haunti bhikkhusaṅghañ‍cā’’ti| tattha itihamaiti itiha imai, aivaṃ imaiti atthau| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

4. atha khau bhagavā taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ imaṃ saṅkhiyadhammaṃ viditvāti aittha viditvāti sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina jānitvā| bhagavā hi katthaci maṃsacakkhunā disvā jānāti – ‘‘addasā khau bhagavā mahantaṃ dārukkhandhaṃ gaṅgāya nadiyā sautaina vuyhamāna’’ntiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.241) viya| katthaci dibbacakkhunā disvā jānāti – ‘‘addasā khau bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā  visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina tā daivatāyau sahassassaiva pāṭaligāmai vatthūni parigaṇhantiyau’’tiādīsu  (dī॰ ni॰ 2.152) viya| katthaci pakatisautaina sutvā jānāti – ‘‘assausi khau bhagavā āyasmatau ānandassa subhaddaina paribbājakaina saddhiṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpa’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.213) viya| katthaci dibbasautaina sutvā jānāti – ‘‘assausi khau bhagavā dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya sandhānassa gahapatissa nigraudhaina paribbājakaina saddhiṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpa’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 3.54) viya| idha pana sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina sutvā añ‍ñāsi| kiṃ karauntau añ‍ñāsi? pacchimayāmakic‍caṃ, kic‍cañ‍ca nāmaitaṃ sātthakaṃ, niratthakanti duvidhaṃ hauti| tattha niratthakakic‍caṃ bhagavatā baudhipal‍laṅkaiyaiva arahattamaggaina samugghātaṃ kataṃ| sātthakaṃyaiva pana  bhagavatau kic‍caṃ hauti| taṃ pañ‍cavidhaṃ – puraibhattakic‍caṃ, pacchābhattakic‍caṃ, purimayāmakic‍caṃ, majjhimayāmakic‍caṃ, pacchimayāmakic‍canti|

tatridaṃ puraibhattakic‍caṃ –

bhagavā hi pātauva uṭṭhāya upaṭṭhākānuggahatthaṃ sarīraphāsukatthañ‍ca mukhadhauvanādisarīraparikammaṃ katvā yāva bhikkhācāravailā tāva vivittāsanai vītināmaitvā, bhikkhācāravailāyaṃ nivāsaitvā kāyabandhanaṃ bandhitvā cīvaraṃ pārupitvā pattamādāya kadāci aikakau, kadāci bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau, gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati; kadāci pakatiyā, kadāci anaikaihi pāṭihāriyaihi vattamānaihi| saiyyathidaṃ, piṇḍāya pavisatau laukanāthassa puratau puratau gantvā mudugatavātā pathaviṃ saudhainti, valāhakā udakaphusitāni muñ‍cantā maggai raiṇuṃ vūpasamaitvā upari vitānaṃ hutvā tiṭṭhanti, aparai vātā pupphāni upasaṃharitvā maggai aukiranti, un‍natā bhūmippadaisā aunamanti, aunatā un‍namanti, pādanikkhaipasamayai samāva bhūmi hauti, sukhasamphassāni padumapupphāni vā pādai sampaṭicchanti| indakhīlassa antau ṭhapitamattai dakkhiṇapādai sarīratau chabbaṇṇarasmiyau nikkhamitvā suvaṇṇarasapiñ‍jarāni viya citrapaṭaparikkhittāni viya ca pāsādakūṭāgārādīni alaṅkarauntiyau itau citau  ca dhāvanti, hatthiassavihaṅgādayau sakasakaṭṭhānaisu ṭhitāyaiva madhurainākāraina saddaṃ karaunti, tathā bhairivīṇādīni tūriyāni manussānañ‍ca kāyūpagāni ābharaṇāni| taina sañ‍ñāṇaina manussā jānanti – ‘‘aj‍ja bhagavā idha piṇḍāya paviṭṭhau’’ti| tai sunivatthā supārutā gandhapupphādīni ādāya gharā nikkhamitvā antaravīthiṃ paṭipaj‍jitvā bhagavantaṃ gandhapupphādīhi sak‍kac‍caṃ pūjaitvā vanditvā – ‘‘amhākaṃ, bhantai, dasa bhikkhū, amhākaṃ vīsati, pañ‍ñāsaṃ…pai॰… sataṃ daithā’’ti yācitvā bhagavataupi pattaṃ gahaitvā āsanaṃ  pañ‍ñapaitvā sak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaina paṭimānainti| bhagavā katabhattakic‍cau taisaṃ sattānaṃ cittasantānāni aulaukaitvā tathā dhammaṃ daisaiti, yathā kaici saraṇagamanaisu patiṭṭhahanti, kaici pañ‍casu sīlaisu, kaici sautāpattisakadāgāmianāgāmiphalānaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ; kaici pabbajitvā aggaphalai arahattaiti| aivaṃ mahājanaṃ anuggahaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ gacchati| tattha  gantvā maṇḍalamāḷai pañ‍ñattavarabuddhāsanai nisīdati, bhikkhūnaṃ bhattakic‍capariyausānaṃ āgamayamānau| tatau bhikkhūnaṃ bhattakic‍capariyausānai upaṭṭhākau bhagavatau nivaidaiti| atha bhagavā gandhakuṭiṃ pavisati| idaṃ tāva puraibhattakic‍caṃ|

atha bhagavā aivaṃ katapuraibhattakic‍cau gandhakuṭiyā upaṭṭhānai nisīditvā pādai pakkhālaitvā pādapīṭhai ṭhatvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ auvadati – ‘‘bhikkhavai, appamādaina sampādaitha, dul‍labhau buddhuppādau laukasmiṃ, dul‍labhau manussattapaṭilābhau, dul‍labhā sampatti, dul‍labhā pabbaj‍jā, dul‍labhaṃ saddhammassavana’’nti| tattha kaici bhagavantaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ pucchanti| bhagavāpi taisaṃ cariyānurūpaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ daiti| tatau sabbaipi bhagavantaṃ vanditvā attanau attanau rattiṭṭhānadivāṭṭhānāni gacchanti| kaici arañ‍ñaṃ, kaici rukkhamūlaṃ, kaici pabbatādīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ, kaici cātumahārājikabhavanaṃ…pai॰… kaici vasavattibhavananti| tatau bhagavā gandhakuṭiṃ pavisitvā sacai ākaṅkhati, dakkhiṇaina  passaina satau sampajānau muhuttaṃ sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti| atha samassāsitakāyau vuṭṭhahitvā dutiyabhāgai laukaṃ vaulaukaiti| tatiyabhāgai yaṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati tattha mahājanau puraibhattaṃ dānaṃ datvā pacchābhattaṃ sunivatthau supārutau gandhapupphādīni ādāya vihārai san‍nipatati| tatau bhagavā sampattaparisāya anurūpaina pāṭihāriyaina gantvā dhammasabhāyaṃ pañ‍ñattavarabuddhāsanai nisaj‍ja dhammaṃ daisaiti kālayuttaṃ samayayuttaṃ, atha kālaṃ viditvā parisaṃ uyyaujaiti, manussā bhagavantaṃ vanditvā pak‍kamanti| idaṃ pacchābhattakic‍caṃ|

sau aivaṃ niṭṭhitapacchābhattakic‍cau sacai gattāni ausiñ‍citukāmau hauti, buddhāsanā vuṭṭhāya nhānakauṭṭhakaṃ pavisitvā upaṭṭhākaina paṭiyāditaudakaina gattāni utuṃ gaṇhāpaiti| upaṭṭhākaupi buddhāsanaṃ ānaitvā gandhakuṭiparivaiṇai pañ‍ñapaiti| bhagavā surattadupaṭṭaṃ nivāsaitvā kāyabandhanaṃ bandhitvā uttarāsaṅgaṃ aikaṃsaṃ karitvā tattha gantvā nisīdati aikakauva muhuttaṃ paṭisal‍līnau, atha bhikkhū tatau tatau āgamma bhagavatau upaṭṭhānaṃ āgacchanti| tattha aikac‍cai pañhaṃ pucchanti, aikac‍cai kammaṭṭhānaṃ, aikac‍cai  dhammassavanaṃ yācanti| bhagavā taisaṃ adhippāyaṃ sampādaintau purimayāmaṃ vītināmaiti| idaṃ purimayāmakic‍caṃ|

purimayāmakic‍capariyausānai  pana bhikkhūsu bhagavantaṃ vanditvā pak‍kantaisu sakaladasasahassilaukadhātudaivatāyau aukāsaṃ labhamānā bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti, yathābhisaṅkhataṃ antamasau caturakkharampi| bhagavā tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ pañhaṃ vissaj‍jaintau majjhimayāmaṃ vītināmaiti| idaṃ majjhimayāmakic‍caṃ|

pacchimayāmaṃ pana tayau kauṭṭhāsai katvā puraibhattatau paṭṭhāya nisaj‍jāya pīḷitassa sarīrassa kilāsubhāvamaucanatthaṃ aikaṃ kauṭṭhāsaṃ caṅkamaina vītināmaiti| dutiyakauṭṭhāsai gandhakuṭiṃ pavisitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina satau sampajānau sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti| tatiyakauṭṭhāsai pac‍cuṭṭhāya nisīditvā purimabuddhānaṃ santikai dānasīlādivasaina katādhikārapuggaladassanatthaṃ  buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaiti| idaṃ pacchimayāmakic‍caṃ|

tasmiṃ pana divasai bhagavā puraibhattakic‍caṃ rājagahai pariyausāpaitvā pacchābhattai maggaṃ āgatau, purimayāmai bhikkhūnaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathaitvā, majjhimayāmai daivatānaṃ pañhaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā, pacchimayāmai caṅkamaṃ āruyha caṅkamamānau pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ imaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ ārabbha pavattaṃ kathaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇainaiva sutvā añ‍ñāsīti| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘pacchimayāmakic‍caṃ karauntau añ‍ñāsī’’ti|

ñatvā ca panassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imai bhikkhū mayhaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ ārabbha guṇaṃ kathainti, aitaisañ‍ca sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇakic‍caṃ na pākaṭaṃ, mayhamaiva pākaṭaṃ| mayi pana gatai aitai attanau kathaṃ nirantaraṃ āraucaissanti, tatau naisaṃ ahaṃ taṃ aṭṭhuppattiṃ katvā tividhaṃ sīlaṃ vibhajantau, dvāsaṭṭhiyā ṭhānaisu appaṭivattiyaṃ sīhanādaṃ nadantau, pac‍cayākāraṃ samaudhānaitvā buddhaguṇai pākaṭai katvā, sinairuṃ ukkhipaintau viya suvaṇṇakūṭaina nabhaṃ paharantau viya ca dasasahassilaukadhātukampanaṃ brahmajālasuttantaṃ arahattanikūṭaina niṭṭhāpaintau daisaissāmi, sā mai daisanā parinibbutassāpi pañ‍cavassasahassāni sattānaṃ amatamahānibbānaṃ sampāpikā bhavissatī’’ti| aivaṃ cintaitvā yaina maṇḍalamāḷau tainupasaṅkamīti| yaināti yaina disābhāgaina, sau upasaṅkamitabbau| bhummatthai vā aitaṃ karaṇavacanaṃ, yasmiṃ padaisai sau maṇḍalamāḷau, tattha gatauti ayamaittha atthau|

pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdīti buddhakālai kira yattha yattha aikaupi bhikkhu viharati sabbattha buddhāsanaṃ pañ‍ñattamaiva hauti| kasmā? bhagavā kira attanau santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā phāsukaṭṭhānai  viharantai manasi karauti – ‘‘asukau mayhaṃ santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā gatau, sakkhissati nu khau visaisaṃ nibbattaituṃ nau vā’’ti| atha naṃ passati kammaṭṭhānaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā akusalavitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kayamānaṃ, tatau ‘‘kathañhi nāma mādisassa satthu santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā  viharantaṃ imaṃ kulaputtaṃ akusalavitak‍kā abhibhavitvā anamataggai vaṭṭadukkhai saṃsāraissantī’’ti tassa anuggahatthaṃ tatthaiva attānaṃ dassaitvā taṃ kulaputtaṃ auvaditvā ākāsaṃ uppatitvā puna attanau vasanaṭṭhānamaiva gacchati| athaivaṃ auvadiyamānā tai bhikkhū cintayiṃsu – ‘‘satthā amhākaṃ manaṃ jānitvā āgantvā amhākaṃ samīpai ṭhitaṃyaiva attānaṃ dassaiti’’| tasmiṃ khaṇai – ‘‘bhantai, idha nisīdatha, idha nisīdathā’’ti āsanapariyaisanaṃ nāma bhārauti| tai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvāva viharanti| yassa pīṭhaṃ atthi, sau taṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| yassa natthi, sau mañ‍caṃ vā phalakaṃ vā kaṭṭhaṃ vā pāsāṇaṃ vā vālukapuñ‍jaṃ vā pañ‍ñapaiti| taṃ alabhamānā purāṇapaṇṇānipi saṅkaḍḍhitvā tattha paṃsukūlaṃ pattharitvā ṭhapainti| idha pana rañ‍ñau nisīdanāsanamaiva atthi, taṃ papphauṭaitvā pañ‍ñapaitvā parivāraitvā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau adhimuttikañāṇamārabbha guṇaṃ thaumayamānā nisīdiṃsu| taṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ – ‘‘pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdī’’ti|

aivaṃ nisin‍nau pana jānantauyaiva kathāsamuṭṭhāpanatthaṃ bhikkhū pucchi| tai cassa sabbaṃ kathayiṃsu| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā’’tiādi| tattha kāya nutthāti katamāya nu kathāya san‍nisin‍nā bhavathāti atthau| kāya naitthātipi pāḷi, tassā katamāya nu aitthāti atthau kāya nautthātipi pāḷi| tassāpi purimauyaiva atthau|

antarākathāti, kammaṭṭhānamanasikārauddaisaparipucchādīnaṃ antarā añ‍ñā aikā kathā| vippakatāti, mama āgamanapac‍cayā apariniṭṭhitā sikhaṃ appattā| taina kiṃ dassaiti? ‘‘nāhaṃ tumhākaṃ kathābhaṅgatthaṃ āgatau, ahaṃ pana sabbañ‍ñutāya tumhākaṃ kathaṃ niṭṭhāpaitvā matthakappattaṃ katvā dassāmīti āgatau’’ti nisaj‍jaiva sabbañ‍ñupavāraṇaṃ pavāraiti| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā vippakatā, atha bhagavā anuppattauti aitthāpi ayamadhippāyau| ayaṃ bhantai amhākaṃ bhagavatau sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ ārabbha guṇakathā vippakatā, na rājakathādikā tiracchānakathā, atha bhagavā anuppattau; taṃ nau idāni niṭṭhāpaitvā daisaithāti|

aittāvatā ca yaṃ āyasmatā ānandaina kamalakuvalayuj‍jalavimalasādhurasasalilāya paukkharaṇiyā sukhāvataraṇatthaṃ nimmalasilātalaracanavilāsasaubhitaratanasaupānaṃ, vippakiṇṇamuttātalasadisavālukākiṇṇapaṇḍarabhūmibhāgaṃ  titthaṃ viya suvibhattabhittivicitravaidikāparikkhittassa nakkhattapathaṃ phusitukāmatāya viya, vijambhitasamussayassa pāsādavarassa sukhārauhaṇatthaṃ dantamayasaṇhamuduphalakakañ‍canalatāvinaddhamaṇigaṇappabhāsamudayuj‍jalasaubhaṃ saupānaṃ viya, suvaṇṇavalayanūpurādisaṅghaṭṭanasaddasammissitakathitahasitamadhurassaragaihajanavicaritassa uḷārissarivibhavasaubhitassa mahāgharassa sukhappavaisanatthaṃ suvaṇṇarajatamaṇimuttapavāḷādijutivissaravij‍jautitasuppatiṭṭhitavisāladvārabāhaṃ mahādvāraṃ viya ca atthabyañ‍janasampan‍nassa buddhaguṇānubhāvasaṃsūcakassa imassa suttassa sukhāvagahaṇatthaṃ kāladaisadaisakavatthuparisāpadaisapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ nidānaṃ bhāsitaṃ, tassatthavaṇṇanā samattāti|

5. idāni – ‘‘mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyu’’ntiādinā nayaina bhagavatā nikkhittassa suttassa vaṇṇanāya aukāsau anuppattau| sā panaisā suttavaṇṇanā| yasmā suttanikkhaipaṃ vicāraitvā vuc‍camānā pākaṭā hauti, tasmā suttanikkhaipaṃ tāva vicārayissāma| cattārau hi suttanikkhaipā – attajjhāsayau, parajjhāsayau, pucchāvasikau, aṭṭhuppattikauti|

tattha  yāni suttāni bhagavā paraihi anajjhiṭṭhau kaivalaṃ attanau ajjhāsayainaiva kathaisi; saiyyathidaṃ, ākaṅkhaiyyasuttaṃ, vatthasuttaṃ, mahāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ, mahāsaḷāyatanavibhaṅgasuttaṃ, ariyavaṃsasuttaṃ, sammappadhānasuttantahārakau, iddhipādaindriyabalabaujjhaṅgamaggaṅgasuttantahārakauti aivamādīni; taisaṃ attajjhāsayau nikkhaipau|

yāni pana ‘‘paripak‍kā khau rāhulassa vimuttiparipācaniyā dhammā; yaṃnūnāhaṃ rāhulaṃ uttariṃ  āsavānaṃ khayai vinaiyya’’nti; (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.121) aivaṃ paraisaṃ ajjhāsayaṃ khantiṃ manaṃ abhinīhāraṃ bujjhanabhāvañ‍ca avaikkhitvā parajjhāsayavasaina kathitāni; saiyyathidaṃ, cūḷarāhulauvādasuttaṃ, mahārāhulauvādasuttaṃ, dhammacak‍kappavattanaṃ, dhātuvibhaṅgasuttanti aivamādīni; taisaṃ parajjhāsayau nikkhaipau|

bhagavantaṃ pana upasaṅkamitvā catassau parisā, cattārau vaṇṇā, nāgā, supaṇṇā, gandhabbā, asurā, yakkhā, mahārājānau, tāvatiṃsādayau daivā, mahābrahmāti aivamādayau – ‘‘baujjhaṅgā baujjhaṅgā’’ti, bhantai, vuc‍canti| ‘‘nīvaraṇā nīvaraṇā’’ti, bhantai, vuc‍canti; ‘‘imai nu khau, bhantai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā’’| ‘‘kiṃ sūdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭha’’ntiādinā nayaina pañhaṃ  pucchanti| aivaṃ puṭṭhaina bhagavatā yāni kathitāni baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttādīni, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi daivatāsaṃyutta-mārasaṃyutta-brahmasaṃyutta-sak‍kapañha-cūḷavaidal‍la-mahāvaidal‍la-sāmañ‍ñaphala-āḷavaka-sūcilauma-kharalaumasuttādīni; taisaṃ pucchāvasikau nikkhaipau|

yāni pana tāni uppan‍naṃ kāraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca kathitāni, saiyyathidaṃ – dhammadāyādaṃ, cūḷasīhanādaṃ, candūpamaṃ, puttamaṃsūpamaṃ, dārukkhandhūpamaṃ, aggikkhandhūpamaṃ, phaiṇapiṇḍūpamaṃ, pāricchattakūpamanti aivamādīni; taisaṃ aṭṭhuppattikau nikkhaipau|

aivamaitaisu catūsu nikkhaipaisu imassa suttassa aṭṭhuppattikau nikkhaipau| aṭṭhuppattiyā hi idaṃ bhagavatā nikkhittaṃ| katarāya aṭṭhuppattiyā? vaṇṇāvaṇṇai| ācariyau ratanattayassa avaṇṇaṃ abhāsi, antaivāsī vaṇṇaṃ| iti imaṃ vaṇṇāvaṇṇaṃ aṭṭhuppattiṃ katvā daisanākusalau bhagavā – ‘‘mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyu’’nti daisanaṃ ārabhi| tattha mamanti, sāmivacanaṃ, mamāti atthau| vāsaddau vikappanatthau| paraiti, paṭiviruddhā sattā| tatrāti yai avaṇṇaṃ vadanti taisu|

na āghātautiādīhi kiñ‍cāpi taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āghātauyaiva natthi, atha khau āyatiṃ kulaputtānaṃ īdisaisupi ṭhānaisu akusaluppattiṃ paṭisaidhaintau dhammanaittiṃ  ṭhapaiti| tattha āhanati cittanti ‘āghātau’; kaupassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| appatītā haunti taina atuṭṭhā asaumanassikāti appac‍cayau; daumanassassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| naiva attanau na paraisaṃ hitaṃ abhirādhayatīti anabhiraddhi; kaupassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| aivamaittha dvīhi padaihi saṅkhārakkhandhau, aikaina vaidanākkhandhauti dvai khandhā vuttā| taisaṃ vasaina saisānampi sampayuttadhammānaṃ kāraṇaṃ paṭikkhittamaiva|

aivaṃ paṭhamaina nayaina manaupadausaṃ nivāraitvā, dutiyaina nayaina tattha ādīnavaṃ dassaintau āha – ‘‘tatra cai tumhai assatha kupitā vā anattamanā vā, tumhaṃ yaivassa taina antarāyau’’ti| tattha ‘tatra cai tumhai assathā’ti taisu avaṇṇabhāsakaisu, tasmiṃ vā avaṇṇai tumhai bhavaiyyātha cai; yadi bhavaiyyāthāti atthau| ‘kupitā’ kaupaina, anattamanā daumanassaina| ‘tumhaṃ yaivassa taina antarāyau’ti tumhākaṃyaiva taina kaupaina, tāya ca anattamanatāya paṭhamajjhānādīnaṃ antarāyau bhavaiyya|

aivaṃ  dutiyaina nayaina ādīnavaṃ dassaitvā, tatiyaina nayaina vacanatthasal‍lakkhaṇamattaipi asamatthataṃ dassaintau – ‘‘api nu tumhai paraisa’’ntiādimāha| tattha paraisanti yaisaṃ kaisaṃ ci| kupitau hi naiva buddhapac‍caikabuddhaariyasāvakānaṃ, na mātāpitūnaṃ, na pac‍catthikānaṃ subhāsitadubbhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānāti| yathāha –

‘‘kuddhau atthaṃ na jānāti, kuddhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhaṃ tamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ kaudhau sahatai naraṃ||

anatthajananau kaudhau, kaudhau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhatī’’ti|| (a॰ ni॰ 7.64)|

aivaṃ  sabbathāpi avaṇṇai manaupadausaṃ nisaidhaitvā idāni paṭipaj‍jitabbākāraṃ dassaintau – ‘‘tatra tumhaihi abhūtaṃ abhūtatau’’tiādimāha|

tattha tatra tumhaihīti, tasmiṃ avaṇṇai tumhaihi| abhūtaṃ abhūtatau nibbaiṭhaitabbanti yaṃ abhūtaṃ, taṃ abhūtabhāvainaiva  apanaitabbaṃ| kathaṃ? itipaitaṃ abhūtantiādinā nayaina| tatrāyaṃ yaujanā – ‘‘tumhākaṃ satthā na sabbañ‍ñū, dhammau durakkhātau, saṅghau duppaṭipan‍nau’’tiādīni sutvā na tuṇhī bhavitabbaṃ| aivaṃ pana vattabbaṃ – ‘‘iti paitaṃ abhūtaṃ, yaṃ tumhaihi vuttaṃ, taṃ imināpi kāraṇaina abhūtaṃ, imināpi kāraṇaina atacchaṃ, ‘natthi caitaṃ amhaisu’, ‘na ca panaitaṃ amhaisu saṃvij‍jati’, sabbañ‍ñūyaiva amhākaṃ satthā, svākkhātau dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau saṅghau, tatra idañ‍cidañ‍ca kāraṇa’’nti| aittha ca dutiyaṃ padaṃ paṭhamassa, catutthañ‍ca tatiyassa vaivacananti vaiditabbaṃ| idañ‍ca avaṇṇaiyaiva nibbaiṭhanaṃ kātabbaṃ, na sabbattha| yadi hi ‘‘tvaṃ dussīlau, tavācariyau dussīlau, idañ‍cidañ‍ca tayā kataṃ, tavācariyaina kata’’nti vuttai tuṇhībhūtau adhivāsaiti, āsaṅkanīyau hauti| tasmā manaupadausaṃ akatvā avaṇṇau nibbaiṭhaitabbau| ‘‘auṭṭhausi, gauṇausī’’tiādinā pana nayaina dasahi ak‍kausavatthūhi ak‍kausantaṃ puggalaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā adhivāsanakhantiyaiva tattha kātabbā|

6. aivaṃ avaṇṇabhūmiyaṃ tādilakkhaṇaṃ dassaitvā idāni vaṇṇabhūmiyaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyu’’ntiādimāha| tattha paraiti yai kaici pasan‍nā daivamanussā| ānandanti aitaināti ānandau, pītiyā aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sumanassa bhāvau saumanassaṃ, caitasikasukhassaitaṃ  adhivacanaṃ| uppilāvinau bhāvau uppilāvitattaṃ| kassa uppilāvitattanti? caitasauti| uddhac‍cāvahāya uppilāpanapītiyā aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idhāpi dvīhi padaihi saṅkhārakkhandhau, aikaina vaidanākkhandhau vuttau|

aivaṃ paṭhamanayaina uppilāvitattaṃ nivāraitvā, dutiyaina tattha ādīnavaṃ dassaintau – ‘‘tatra cai tumhai assathā’’tiādimāha| idhāpi tumhaṃ yaivassa taina antarāyauti taina uppilāvitattaina tumhākaṃyaiva paṭhamajjhānādīnaṃ  antarāyau bhavaiyyāti atthau vaiditabbau| kasmā  panaitaṃ vuttaṃ? nanu bhagavatā –

‘‘buddhauti kittayantassa, kāyai bhavati yā pīti|

varamaiva hi sā pīti, kasiṇaināpi jambudīpassa||

dhammauti kittayantassa, kāyai bhavati yā pīti|

varamaiva hi sā pīti, kasiṇaināpi jambudīpassa||

saṅghauti kittayantassa, kāyai bhavati yā pīti|

varamaiva hi sā pīti, kasiṇaināpi jambudīpassā’’ti ca||

‘‘yai, bhikkhavai, buddhai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā’’ti ca aivamādīhi anaikasataihi suttaihi ratanattayai pītisaumanassamaiva vaṇṇitanti| sac‍caṃ vaṇṇitaṃ, taṃ pana naikkhammanissitaṃ| idha – ‘‘amhākaṃ buddhau, amhākaṃ dhammau’’tiādinā nayaina āyasmatau chan‍nassa uppan‍nasadisaṃ gaihassitaṃ pītisaumanassaṃ adhippaitaṃ| idañhi jhānādipaṭilābhāya antarāyakaraṃ hauti| tainaivāyasmā chan‍naupi yāva buddhau na parinibbāyi, tāva visaisaṃ nibbattaituṃ nāsakkhi, parinibbānakālai pañ‍ñattaina pana brahmadaṇḍaina taj‍jitau taṃ pītisaumanassaṃ pahāya visaisaṃ nibbattaisi| tasmā antarāyakaraṃyaiva sandhāya idaṃ vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ| ayañhi laubhasahagatā pīti| laubhau ca kaudhasadisauva| yathāha –

‘‘luddhau atthaṃ na jānāti, luddhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhaṃ tamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ laubhau sahatai naraṃ||

anatthajananau  laubhau, laubhau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhatī’’ti|| (itivu॰ 88)|

tatiyavārau pana idha anāgataupi atthatau āgatau yaivāti vaiditabbau| yathaiva hi kuddhau, aivaṃ luddhaupi atthaṃ na jānātīti|

paṭipaj‍jitabbākāradassanavārai panāyaṃ yaujanā – ‘‘tumhākaṃ satthā sabbañ‍ñū arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dhammau svākkhātau, saṅghau suppaṭipan‍nau’’tiādīni sutvā na tuṇhī bhavitabbaṃ| aivaṃ pana paṭijānitabbaṃ – ‘‘itipaitaṃ bhūtaṃ, yaṃ tumhaihi vuttaṃ, taṃ imināpi kāraṇaina bhūtaṃ, imināpi kāraṇaina tacchaṃ| sau hi bhagavā itipi arahaṃ, itipi sammāsambuddhau; dhammau itipi svākkhātau, itipi sandiṭṭhikau; saṅghau itipi suppaṭipan‍nau, itipi ujuppaṭipan‍nau’’ti| ‘‘tvaṃ sīlavā’’ti pucchitaināpi sacai sīlavā, ‘‘sīlavāhamasmī’’ti paṭijānitabbamaiva| ‘‘tvaṃ paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī…pai॰… arahā’’ti puṭṭhaināpi sabhāgānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃyaiva paṭijānitabbaṃ| aivañhi pāpicchatā caiva parivaj‍jitā hauti, sāsanassa ca amaughatā dīpitā hautīti| saisaṃ vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

cūḷasīlavaṇṇanā

7. appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavaiti kau anusandhi? idaṃ suttaṃ dvīhi padaihi ābaddhaṃ vaṇṇaina ca avaṇṇaina ca| tattha avaṇṇau – ‘‘iti paitaṃ abhūtaṃ iti paitaṃ ataccha’’nti, aitthaiva udakantaṃ patvā aggiviya nivattau| vaṇṇau pana bhūtaṃ bhūtatau paṭijānitabbaṃ – ‘‘iti paitaṃ bhūta’’nti aivaṃ anuvattatiyaiva| sau pana duvidhau brahmadattaina bhāsitavaṇṇau ca bhikkhusaṅghaina acchariyaṃ āvusautiādinā nayaina āraddhavaṇṇau ca| taisu bhikkhusaṅghaina vuttavaṇṇassa upari suñ‍ñatāpakāsanai anusandhiṃ dassaissati| idha pana brahmadattaina vuttavaṇṇassa anusandhiṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai’’ti daisanā āraddhā|

tattha appamattakanti parittassa nāmaṃ| auramattakanti tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| mattāti vuc‍cati pamāṇaṃ| appaṃ mattā aitassāti appamattakaṃ| auraṃ mattā aitassāti auramattakaṃ| sīlamaiva sīlamattakaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – ‘appamattakaṃ khau, panaitaṃ bhikkhavai, auramattakaṃ sīlamattakaṃ’ nāma  yaina ‘‘tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadāmī’’ti ussāhaṃ katvāpi vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau puthuj‍janau vadaiyyāti| tattha siyā – nanu idaṃ sīlaṃ nāma yauginau aggavibhūsanaṃ? yathāhu paurāṇā –

‘‘sīlaṃ yaugissa’laṅkārau, sīlaṃ yaugissa maṇḍanaṃ|

sīlaihi’laṅkatau yaugī, maṇḍanai aggataṃ gatau’’ti||

bhagavatāpi  ca anaikaisu suttasataisu sīlaṃ mahantamaiva katvā kathitaṃ| yathāha – ‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  ‘sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau cassaṃ manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’ti, sīlaisvaivassa paripūrakārī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.65) ca|

‘‘kikīva aṇḍaṃ, camarīva vāladhiṃ|

piyaṃva puttaṃ, nayanaṃva aikakaṃ||

tathaiva sīlaṃ, anurakkhamānā|

supaisalā hautha, sadā sagāravā’’ti ca||

‘‘na pupphagandhau paṭivātamaiti|

na candanaṃ taggaramal‍likā vā||

satañ‍ca gandhau paṭivātamaiti|

sabbā disā sappurisau pavāyati||

candanaṃ tagaraṃ vāpi, uppalaṃ atha vassikī|

aitaisaṃ gandhajātānaṃ, sīlagandhau anuttarau||

appamattau ayaṃ gandhau, yvāyaṃ tagaracandanaṃ|

yau ca sīlavataṃ gandhau, vāti daivaisu uttamau||

taisaṃ sampan‍nasīlānaṃ, appamādavihārinaṃ|

sammadañ‍ñā vimuttānaṃ, mārau maggaṃ na vindatī’’ti ca|| (dha॰ pa॰ 57)|

‘‘sīlai  patiṭṭhāya narau sapañ‍ñau, cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvayaṃ|

ātāpī nipakau bhikkhu, sau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭa’’nti ca|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.23)|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici bījagāmabhūtagāmā vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti, sabbai tai pathaviṃ nissāya, pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya; aivamaitai bījagāmabhūtagāmā vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya sattabaujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau sattabaujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisū’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.150) ca| aivaṃ añ‍ñānipi anaikāni suttāni daṭṭhabbāni| aivamanaikaisu suttasataisu sīlaṃ mahantamaiva katvā kathitaṃ| taṃ ‘‘kasmā  imasmiṃ ṭhānai appamattaka’’nti āhāti? upari  guṇai upanidhāya| sīlañhi samādhiṃ na pāpuṇāti, samādhi pañ‍ñaṃ na pāpuṇāti, tasmā uparimaṃ upanidhāya haiṭṭhimaṃ auramattakaṃ nāma hauti| kathaṃ sīlaṃ samādhiṃ na pāpuṇāti? bhagavā hi abhisambaudhitau sattamai saṃvaccharai sāvatthinagara – dvārai kaṇḍambarukkhamūlai dvādasayaujanai ratanamaṇḍapai yaujanappamāṇai ratanapal‍laṅkai nisīditvā tiyaujanikai dibbasaitacchattai dhāriyamānai dvādasayaujanāya parisāya attādānaparidīpanaṃ titthiyamaddanaṃ – ‘‘uparimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, haiṭṭhimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattati…pai॰… aikaikalaumakūpatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, aikaikalaumakūpatau udakadhārā pavattati, chan‍naṃ vaṇṇāna’’ntiādinayappavattaṃ yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ dassaiti| tassa suvaṇṇavaṇṇasarīratau suvaṇṇavaṇṇā rasmiyau uggantvā yāva bhavaggā gacchanti, sakaladasasahassacak‍kavāḷassa alaṅkaraṇakālau viya hauti, dutiyā dutiyā rasmiyau purimāya purimāya yamakayamakā viya aikakkhaṇai viya pavattanti|

dvin‍nañ‍ca cittānaṃ aikakkhaṇai pavatti nāma natthi| buddhānaṃ pana bhagavantānaṃ bhavaṅgaparivāsassa lahukatāya pañ‍cahākāraihi āciṇṇavasitāya ca, tā aikakkhaṇai viya pavattanti| tassā tassā pana rasmiyā āvaj‍janaparikammādhiṭṭhānāni visuṃ visuṃyaiva|

nīlarasmiatthāya hi bhagavā nīlakasiṇaṃ samāpaj‍jati, pītarasmiatthāya pītakasiṇaṃ, lauhitaaudātarasmiatthāya lauhitaaudātakasiṇaṃ, aggikkhandhatthāya taijaukasiṇaṃ, udakadhāratthāya āpaukasiṇaṃ samāpaj‍jati| satthā caṅkamati, nimmitau tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaitīti  sabbaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ| aittha aikampi sīlassa kic‍caṃ natthi, sabbaṃ samādhikic‍camaiva| aivaṃ sīlaṃ samādhiṃ na pāpuṇāti|

yaṃ pana bhagavā kappasatasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni pāramiyau pūraitvā, aikūnatiṃsavassakālai cak‍kavattisirīnivāsabhūtā bhavanā nikkhamma anaumānadītīrai  pabbajitvā, chabbassāni padhānayaugaṃ katvā, visākhapuṇṇamāyaṃ uruvailagāmai sujātāya din‍naṃ pakkhittadibbaujaṃ madhupāyāsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā, sāyanhasamayai dakkhiṇuttaraina baudhimaṇḍaṃ pavisitvā  assatthadumarājānaṃ tikkhattuṃ padakkhiṇaṃ katvā, pubbuttarabhāgai ṭhitau tiṇasanthāraṃ santharitvā, tisandhipal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā, caturaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ maittākammaṭṭhānaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ katvā, vīriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ adhiṭṭhāya, cuddasahatthapal‍laṅkavaragatau suvaṇṇapīṭhai ṭhapitaṃ rajatakkhandhaṃ viya pañ‍ñāsahatthaṃ baudhikkhandhaṃ piṭṭhitau katvā, upari maṇichattaina viya baudhisākhāya dhāriyamānau, suvaṇṇavaṇṇai cīvarai pavāḷasadisaisu baudhiaṅkuraisu patamānaisu, sūriyai atthaṃ upagacchantai mārabalaṃ vidhamitvā, paṭhamayāmai pubbainivāsaṃ anussaritvā, majjhimayāmai dibbacakkhuṃ visaudhaitvā, pac‍cūsakālai sabbabuddhānamāciṇṇai pac‍cayākārai ñāṇaṃ autāraitvā, ānāpānacatutthajjhānaṃ nibbattaitvā, tadaiva pādakaṃ katvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā, maggapaṭipāṭiyā adhigataina catutthamaggaina sabbakilaisai khaipaitvā sabbabuddhaguṇai paṭivijjhi, idamassa pañ‍ñākic‍caṃ| aivaṃ samādhi pañ‍ñaṃ na pāpuṇāti|

tattha yathā hatthai udakaṃ pātiyaṃ udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, pātiyaṃ udakaṃ ghaṭai udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, ghaṭai udakaṃ kaulambai udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, kaulambai udakaṃ cāṭiyaṃ udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, cāṭiyaṃ udakaṃ mahākumbhiyaṃ udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, mahākumbhiyaṃ udakaṃ kusaubbhai udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, kusaubbhai udakaṃ kandarai udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, kandarai udakaṃ kun‍nadiyaṃ udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, kun‍nadiyaṃ udakaṃ pañ‍camahānadiyaṃ udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, pañ‍camahānadiyaṃ udakaṃ cak‍kavāḷamahāsamuddai  udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti, cak‍kavāḷamahāsamuddai udakaṃ sinairupādakai mahāsamuddai udakaṃ na pāpuṇāti| pātiyaṃ udakaṃ upanidhāya hatthai udakaṃ parittaṃ…pai॰… sinairupādakamahāsamuddai udakaṃ upanidhāya cak‍kavāḷamahāsamuddai udakaṃ parittaṃ| iti uparūpari udakaṃ bahukaṃ upādāya haiṭṭhā haiṭṭhā udakaṃ parittaṃ hauti|

aivamaiva upari upari guṇai upādāya haiṭṭhā haiṭṭhā sīlaṃ appamattakaṃ auramattakanti vaiditabbaṃ| taināha – ‘‘appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, auramattakaṃ sīlamattaka’’nti|

yaina  puthuj‍janauti, aittha –

‘‘duvai puthuj‍janā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

andhau puthuj‍janau aikau, kalyāṇaikau puthuj‍janau’’ti||

tattha  yassa khandhadhātuāyatanādīsu uggahaparipucchāsavanadhāraṇapac‍cavaikkhaṇāni natthi, ayaṃ andhaputhuj‍janau| yassa tāni atthi, sau kalyāṇaputhuj‍janau| duvidhaupi panaisa –

‘‘puthūnaṃ jananādīhi, kāraṇaihi puthuj‍janau|

puthuj‍janantaugadhattā, puthuvāyaṃ janau iti’’||

sau hi puthūnaṃ nānappakārānaṃ kilaisādīnaṃ jananādīhi kāraṇaihi puthuj‍janau| yathāha –

‘‘puthu kilaisai janaintīti puthuj‍janā, puthu avihatasak‍kāyadiṭṭhikāti puthuj‍janā, puthu satthārānaṃ mukhul‍laukikāti puthuj‍janā, puthu sabbagatīhi avuṭṭhitāti puthuj‍janā, puthu nānābhisaṅkhārai abhisaṅkharauntīti puthuj‍janā, puthu nānāaughaihi vuyhanti, puthu santāpaihi santappanti, puthu pariḷāhaihi pariḍayhanti, puthu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu rattā giddhā gathitā mucchitā ajjhaupan‍nā laggā laggitā palibuddhāti puthuj‍janā, puthu pañ‍cahi nīvaraṇaihi āvutā nivutā auvutā pihitā paṭicchan‍nā paṭikuj‍jitāti puthuj‍janā’’ti| puthūnaṃ gaṇanapathamatītānaṃ ariyadhammaparammukhānaṃ nīcadhammasamācārānaṃ janānaṃ antaugadhattāpi puthuj‍janau, puthuvāyaṃ visuṃyaiva saṅkhyaṃ gatau visaṃsaṭṭhau sīlasutādiguṇayuttaihi ariyaihi janaihīti puthuj‍janauti|

tathāgatassāti aṭṭhahi kāraṇaihi bhagavā tathāgatau| tathā āgatauti tathāgatau, tathā gatauti tathāgatau, tathalakkhaṇaṃ  āgatauti tathāgatau, tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau, tathadassitāya tathāgatau, tathavāditāya tathāgatau, tathākāritāya tathāgatau, abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatauti|

kathaṃ bhagavā tathā āgatauti tathāgatau? yathā sabbalaukahitāya ussuk‍kamāpan‍nā purimakā sammāsambuddhā āgatā, yathā vipassī bhagavā āgatau, yathā sikhī bhagavā, yathā vaissabhū bhagavā, yathā kakusandhau bhagavā, yathā kauṇāgamanau bhagavā, yathā kassapau bhagavā āgatau| kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti? yaina abhinīhāraina aitai  bhagavantau āgatā, tainaiva amhākampi bhagavā āgatau| atha vā yathā vipassī bhagavā…pai॰… yathā kassapau bhagavā dānapāramiṃ pūraitvā, sīlanaikkhammapañ‍ñāvīriyakhantisac‍caadhiṭṭhānamaittāupaikkhāpāramiṃ pūraitvā, imā dasa pāramiyau, dasa upapāramiyau, dasa paramatthapāramiyauti samatiṃsapāramiyau pūraitvā aṅgaparic‍cāgaṃ, nayanadhanaraj‍japuttadāraparic‍cāganti imai pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgai paric‍cajitvā pubbayaugapubbacariyadhammakkhānañātatthacariyādayau pūraitvā buddhicariyāya kauṭiṃ patvā āgatau; tathā amhākampi bhagavā āgatau| atha vā yathā vipassī bhagavā…pai॰… kassapau bhagavā cattārau satipaṭṭhānai, cattārau sammappadhānai, cattārau iddhipādai, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgai, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaitvā brūhaitvā āgatau, tathā amhākampi bhagavā āgatau| aivaṃ tathā āgatauti tathāgatau|

‘‘yathaiva laukamhi vipassiādayau,

sabbañ‍ñubhāvaṃ munayau idhāgatā|

tathā ayaṃ sakyamunīpi āgatau,

tathāgatau vuc‍cati taina cakkhumā’’ti||

aivaṃ tathā āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathā gatauti tathāgatau? yathā sampatijātau vipassī bhagavā gatau…pai॰… kassapau bhagavā gatau|

kathañ‍ca sau bhagavā gatau? sau hi sampati jātauva samaihi pādaihi pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya uttarābhimukhau sattapadavītihāraina gatau| yathāha – ‘‘sampatijātau khau, ānanda, baudhisattau samaihi pādaihi patiṭṭhahitvā uttarābhimukhau sattapadavītihāraina gacchati, saitamhi chattai anudhāriyamānai sabbā ca disā anuvilaukaiti, āsabhiṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ‘aggauhamasmi laukassa, jaiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa, saiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa, ayamantimā jāti, natthidāni punabbhavau’ti’’ (dī॰ ni॰ 2.31)|

tañ‍cassa  gamanaṃ tathaṃ ahausi? avitathaṃ anaikaisaṃ visaisādhigamānaṃ pubbanimittabhāvaina| yañhi  sau sampatijātauva samaihi pādaihi patiṭṭhahi| idamassa caturiddhipādapaṭilābhassa pubbanimittaṃ|

uttarābhimukhabhāvau pana sabbalaukuttarabhāvassa pubbanimittaṃ|

sattapadavītihārau, sattabaujjhaṅgaratanapaṭilābhassa|

‘‘suvaṇṇadaṇḍā vītipatanti cāmarā’’ti, aittha vuttacāmarukkhaipau pana sabbatitthiyanimmaddanassa|

saitacchattadhāraṇaṃ, arahattavimuttivaravimalasaitacchattapaṭilābhassa|

sattamapadūpari ṭhatvā sabbadisānuvilaukanaṃ, sabbañ‍ñutānāvaraṇañāṇapaṭilābhassa|

āsabhivācābhāsanaṃ appaṭivattiyavaradhammacak‍kappavattanassa pubbanimittaṃ|

tathā ayaṃ bhagavāpi gatau, tañ‍cassa gamanaṃ tathaṃ ahausi, avitathaṃ, taisaṃyaiva visaisādhigamānaṃ pubbanimittabhāvaina|

taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘muhuttajātauva gavampatī yathā,

samaihi pādaihi phusī vasundharaṃ|

sau vik‍kamī satta padāni gautamau,

saitañ‍ca chattaṃ anudhārayuṃ marū||

gantvāna sau satta padāni gautamau,

disā vilaukaisi samā samantatau|

aṭṭhaṅgupaitaṃ giramabbhudīrayi,

sīhau yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau’’ti||

aivaṃ  tathā gatauti tathāgatau|

atha vā yathā vipassī bhagavā…pai॰… yathā kassapau bhagavā, ayampi bhagavā tathaiva naikkhammaina kāmacchandaṃ pahāya gatau, abyāpādaina byāpādaṃ, ālaukasañ‍ñāya thinamiddhaṃ, avikkhaipaina uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, dhammavavatthānaina vicikicchaṃ pahāya ñāṇaina avij‍jaṃ padālaitvā, pāmauj‍jaina  aratiṃ vinaudaitvā, paṭhamajjhānaina nīvaraṇakavāṭaṃ ugghāṭaitvā, dutiyajjhānaina vitak‍kavicāraṃ vūpasamaitvā, tatiyajjhānaina pītiṃ virājaitvā, catutthajjhānaina sukhadukkhaṃ pahāya, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā rūpasañ‍ñāpaṭighasañ‍ñānānattasañ‍ñāyau samatik‍kamitvā, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ samatik‍kamitvā gatau|

anic‍cānupassanāya nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ pahāya, dukkhānupassanāya sukhasañ‍ñaṃ, anattānupassanāya attasañ‍ñaṃ, nibbidānupassanāya nandiṃ, virāgānupassanāya rāgaṃ, niraudhānupassanāya samudayaṃ, paṭinissaggānupassanāya ādānaṃ, khayānupassanāya ghanasañ‍ñaṃ, vayānupassanāya āyūhanaṃ, vipariṇāmānupassanāya dhuvasañ‍ñaṃ, animittānupassanāya nimittaṃ, appaṇihitānupassanāya paṇidhiṃ, suñ‍ñatānupassanāya abhinivaisaṃ, adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya sārādānābhinivaisaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassanaina sammauhābhinivaisaṃ, ādīnavānupassanāya ālayābhinivaisaṃ, paṭisaṅkhānupassanāya appaṭisaṅkhaṃ, vivaṭṭānupassanāya saṃyaugābhinivaisaṃ, sautāpattimaggaina diṭṭhaikaṭṭhai kilaisai bhañ‍jitvā, sakadāgāmimaggaina auḷārikai kilaisai pahāya, anāgāmimaggaina aṇusahagatai kilaisai samugghāṭaitvā, arahattamaggaina sabbakilaisai samucchinditvā gatau| aivampi tathā gatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau?pathavīdhātuyā kakkhaḷattalakkhaṇaṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ| āpaudhātuyā paggharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ| taijaudhātuyā uṇhattalakkhaṇaṃ| vāyaudhātuyā vitthambhanalakkhaṇaṃ| ākāsadhātuyā asamphuṭṭhalakkhaṇaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā vijānanalakkhaṇaṃ|

rūpassa  ruppanalakkhaṇaṃ| vaidanāya vaidayitalakkhaṇaṃ| sañ‍ñāya sañ‍jānanalakkhaṇaṃ| saṅkhārānaṃ abhisaṅkharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇassa vijānanalakkhaṇaṃ|

vitak‍kassa  abhiniraupanalakkhaṇaṃ| vicārassa anumaj‍janalakkhaṇaṃ pītiyā pharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ| sukhassa sātalakkhaṇaṃ| cittaikaggatāya avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ| phassassa phusanalakkhaṇaṃ|

saddhindriyassa adhimaukkhalakkhaṇaṃ| vīriyindriyassa paggahalakkhaṇaṃ| satindriyassa upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ| samādhindriyassa avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ| pañ‍ñindriyassa pajānanalakkhaṇaṃ|

saddhābalassa assaddhiyai akampiyalakkhaṇaṃ| vīriyabalassa kausaj‍jai, satibalassa muṭṭhassac‍cai| samādhibalassa uddhac‍cai, pañ‍ñābalassa avij‍jāya akampiyalakkhaṇaṃ|

satisambaujjhaṅgassa upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ| dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa pavicayalakkhaṇaṃ| vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa paggahalakkhaṇaṃ| pītisambaujjhaṅgassa pharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ| passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa vūpasamalakkhaṇaṃ| samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ| upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa paṭisaṅkhānalakkhaṇaṃ|

sammādiṭṭhiyā dassanalakkhaṇaṃ| sammāsaṅkappassa abhiniraupanalakkhaṇaṃ| sammāvācāya pariggahalakkhaṇaṃ| sammākammantassa samuṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ| sammāājīvassa vaudānalakkhaṇaṃ| sammāvāyāmassa  paggahalakkhaṇaṃ| sammāsatiyā upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ| sammāsamādhissa avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ|

avij‍jāya añ‍ñāṇalakkhaṇaṃ| saṅkhārānaṃ caitanālakkhaṇaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇassa vijānanalakkhaṇaṃ| nāmassa namanalakkhaṇaṃ| rūpassa ruppanalakkhaṇaṃ| saḷāyatanassa āyatanalakkhaṇaṃ| phassassa phusanalakkhaṇaṃ| vaidanāya vaidayitalakkhaṇaṃ| taṇhāya haitulakkhaṇaṃ| upādānassa gahaṇalakkhaṇaṃ| bhavassa āyūhanalakkhaṇaṃ| jātiyā nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ| jarāya jīraṇalakkhaṇaṃ| maraṇassa cutilakkhaṇaṃ|

dhātūnaṃ suñ‍ñatālakkhaṇaṃ| āyatanānaṃ āyatanalakkhaṇaṃ| satipaṭṭhānānaṃ upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ| sammappadhānānaṃ padahanalakkhaṇaṃ| iddhipādānaṃ ijjhanalakkhaṇaṃ| indriyānaṃ adhipatilakkhaṇaṃ| balānaṃ akampiyalakkhaṇaṃ| baujjhaṅgānaṃ niyyānalakkhaṇaṃ| maggassa haitulakkhaṇaṃ|

sac‍cānaṃ  tathalakkhaṇaṃ| samathassa avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ| vipassanāya anupassanālakkhaṇaṃ| samathavipassanānaṃ aikarasalakkhaṇaṃ| yuganaddhānaṃ anativattanalakkhaṇaṃ|

sīlavisuddhiyā saṃvaralakkhaṇaṃ| cittavisuddhiyā avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ| diṭṭhivisuddhiyā dassanalakkhaṇaṃ|

khayai ñāṇassa samucchaidanalakkhaṇaṃ| anuppādai ñāṇassa passaddhilakkhaṇaṃ|

chandassa  mūlalakkhaṇaṃ| manasikārassa samuṭṭhāpanalakkhaṇaṃ| phassassa samaudhānalakkhaṇaṃ| vaidanāya samausaraṇalakkhaṇaṃ| samādhissa pamukhalakkhaṇaṃ| satiyā ādhipataiyyalakkhaṇaṃ| pañ‍ñāya tatuttariyalakkhaṇaṃ| vimuttiyā sāralakkhaṇaṃ… amataugadhassa nibbānassa pariyausānalakkhaṇaṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ| aivaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ ñāṇagatiyā āgatau avirajjhitvā pattau anuppattauti tathāgatau| aivaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau?  tathadhammā nāma cattāri ariyasac‍cāni| yathāha – ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| katamāni cattāri? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti bhikkhavai, tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaita’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1090) vitthārau| tāni ca bhagavā abhisambuddhau, tasmā tathānaṃ dhammānaṃ abhisambuddhattā tathāgatauti vuc‍cati| abhisambuddhatthau haittha gatasaddau|

api ca jarāmaraṇassa jātipac‍cayasambhūtasamudāgataṭṭhau tathau avitathau anañ‍ñathau…pai॰…, saṅkhārānaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayasambhūtasamudāgataṭṭhau tathau avitathau anañ‍ñathau…pai॰…, tathā avij‍jāya saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayaṭṭhau, saṅkhārānaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa pac‍cayaṭṭhau…pai॰…, jātiyā jarāmaraṇassa pac‍cayaṭṭhau tathau avitathau anañ‍ñathau| taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavā abhisambuddhau, tasmāpi tathānaṃ dhammānaṃ abhisambuddhattā tathāgatauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathadassitāya tathāgatau? bhagavā yaṃ sadaivakai laukai…pai॰…, sadaivamanussāya pajāya aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ  sattānaṃ cakkhudvārai āpāthamāgacchantaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ nāma atthi, taṃ sabbākāratau jānāti passati| aivaṃ jānatā passatā ca, taina taṃ  iṭṭhāniṭṭhādivasaina vā diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātaisu labbhamānakapadavasaina vā| ‘‘katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ  rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītaka’’ntiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 616) nayaina anaikaihi nāmaihi tairasahi vāraihi dvaipañ‍ñāsāya nayaihi vibhaj‍jamānaṃ tathamaiva hauti, vitathaṃ natthi| aisa nayau sautadvārādīsupi āpāthaṃ āgacchantaisu saddādīsu| vuttañ‍caitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘yaṃ bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya pajāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, tamahaṃ jānāmi| tamahaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, taṃ tathāgatassa viditaṃ, taṃ tathāgatau na upaṭṭhāsī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.24)| aivaṃ tathadassitāya tathāgatau| tattha tathadassī atthai tathāgatauti padasambhavau vaiditabbau|

kathaṃ  tathavāditāya tathāgatau? yaṃ rattiṃ bhagavā baudhimaṇḍai aparājitapal‍laṅkai nisin‍nau tiṇṇaṃ mārānaṃ matthakaṃ madditvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau, yañ‍ca rattiṃ yamakasālānamantarai anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyi, aitthantarai pañ‍cacattālīsavassaparimāṇai kālai paṭhamabaudhiyāpi majjhimabaudhiyāpi pacchimabaudhiyāpi yaṃ bhagavatā bhāsitaṃ – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ…pai॰… vaidal‍laṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ atthatau ca byañ‍janatau ca anupavaj‍jaṃ, anūnamanadhikaṃ, sabbākāraparipuṇṇaṃ, rāgamadanimmadanaṃ, dausamauhamadanimmadanaṃ| natthi tattha vālaggamattampi avakkhalitaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ aikamuddikāya lañchitaṃ viya, aikanāḷiyā mitaṃ viya, aikatulāya tulitaṃ viya ca, tathamaiva hauti avitathaṃ anañ‍ñathaṃ| taināha – ‘‘yañ‍ca, cunda, rattiṃ tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati, yaṃ aitasmiṃ antarai bhāsati lapati niddisati, sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.23)| gadatthau haittha gatasaddau| aivaṃ tathavāditāya tathāgatau|

api  ca āgadanaṃ āgadau, vacananti atthau| tayau aviparītau āgadau assāti, da-kārassa ta-kāraṃ katvā tathāgatauti aivamaitasmiṃ atthai padasiddhi vaiditabbā|

kathaṃ tathākāritāya tathāgatau? bhagavatau hi vācāya kāyau anulaumaiti, kāyassapi vācā, tasmā yathāvādī tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī ca hauti| aivaṃbhūtassa cassa yathāvācā, kāyaupi tathā gatau pavattauti atthau| yathā ca kāyau, vācāpi tathā gatā pavattāti tathāgatau| tainaivāha – ‘‘yathāvādī, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| iti yathāvādī tathākārī yathākārī tathāvādī| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.23)| aivaṃ tathākāritāya tathāgatau|

kathaṃ abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatau? upari bhavaggaṃ haiṭṭhā avīciṃ pariyantaṃ katvā tiriyaṃ aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu sabbasattai abhibhavati sīlainapi samādhināpi pañ‍ñāyapi vimuttiyāpi, vimuttiñāṇadassanainapi na tassa tulā vā pamāṇaṃ vā atthi; atulau appamaiyyau anuttarau rājātirājā daivadaivau sak‍kānaṃ atisak‍kau brahmānaṃ atibrahmā| taināha – ‘‘sadaivakai, bhikkhavai, laukai…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya pajāya tathāgatau abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī, tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti|

tatraivaṃ padasiddhi vaiditabbā| agadau viya agadau| kau panaisa? daisanāvilāsamayau caiva puñ‍ñussayau ca| taina haisa mahānubhāvau bhisak‍kau dibbāgadaina sappai viya sabbaparappavādinau sadaivakañ‍ca laukaṃ abhibhavati| iti sabbālaukābhibhavanai tathau aviparītau daisanāvilāsamayau caiva puñ‍ñussayau ca agadau assāti| da-kārassa ta-kāraṃ katvā tathāgatauti vaiditabbau| aivaṃ abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatau|

api ca tathāya gatautipi tathāgatau, tathaṃ gatautipi tathāgatau| gatauti avagatau, atītau pattau paṭipan‍nauti atthau|

tattha  sakalalaukaṃ tīraṇapariñ‍ñāya tathāya gatau avagatauti tathāgatau| laukasamudayaṃ pahānapariñ‍ñāya tathāya gatau atītauti tathāgatau| laukaniraudhaṃ sacchikiriyāya tathāya gatau pattauti tathāgatau| laukaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ tathaṃ gatau paṭipan‍nauti tathāgatau| taina  vuttaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘laukau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau, laukasmā tathāgatau visaṃyuttau| laukasamudayau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau, laukasamudayau tathāgatassa pahīnau| laukaniraudhau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau, laukaniraudhau tathāgatassa sacchikatau| laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā tathāgatassa bhāvitā| yaṃ bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa…pai॰… sabbaṃ taṃ tathāgataina abhisambuddhaṃ| tasmā, tathāgatauti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.23)|

tassapi  aivaṃ atthau vaiditabbau| idampi ca tathāgatassa tathāgatabhāvadīpanai mukhamattamaiva| sabbākāraina pana tathāgatauva tathāgatassa tathāgatabhāvaṃ vaṇṇaiyya|

katamañ‍ca taṃ bhikkhavaiti yaina appamattakaina auramattakaina sīlamattakaina puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya, taṃ katamanti pucchati? tattha pucchā nāma adiṭṭhajautanā pucchā, diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchā, vimaticchaidanā pucchā, anumatipucchā, kathaitukamyatā pucchāti pañ‍cavidhā hauti|

tattha katamā adiṭṭhajautanā pucchā? pakatiyā lakkhaṇaṃ añ‍ñātaṃ hauti, adiṭṭhaṃ atulitaṃ atīritaṃ avibhūtaṃ avibhāvitaṃ, tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya tulanāya tīraṇāya vibhāvanāya pañhaṃ pucchati, ayaṃ adiṭṭhajautanā pucchā|

katamā diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchā? pakatiyā lakkhaṇaṃ ñātaṃ hauti, diṭṭhaṃ tulitaṃ tīritaṃ vibhūtaṃ vibhāvitaṃ, tassa añ‍ñaihi paṇḍitaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati, ayaṃ diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchā|

katamā  vimaticchaidanā pucchā? pakatiyā saṃsayapakkhandau hauti, vimatipakkhandau, dvaiḷhakajātau, ‘‘aivaṃ nu khau, na nu khau, kin‍nu khau, kathaṃ nu khau’’ti| sau vimaticchaidanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati| ayaṃ vimaticchaidanā pucchā|

katamā anumatipucchā? bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ anumatiyā pañhaṃ pucchati – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti| anic‍caṃ, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vāti? dukkhaṃ bhantaiti (mahāva॰ 21) sabbaṃ vattabbaṃ, ayaṃ anumatipucchā|

katamā kathaitukamyatā pucchā? bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ kathaitukamyatāya pañhaṃ pucchati| cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau?…pai॰… aṭṭhimai bhikkhavai maggaṅgā| katamai aṭṭhāti, ayaṃ kathaitukamyatā pucchā|

iti imāsu pañ‍casu pucchāsu adiṭṭhassa tāva kassaci dhammassa abhāvatau tathāgatassa adiṭṭhajautanā  pucchā natthi| ‘‘idaṃ nāma añ‍ñaihi paṇḍitaihi samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi saddhiṃ saṃsanditvā daisaissāmī’’ti saman‍nāhārassaiva anuppaj‍janatau diṭṭhasaṃsandanā pucchāpi natthi| yasmā pana buddhānaṃ aikadhammaipi āsappanā parisappanā natthi, baudhimaṇḍaiyaiva sabbā kaṅkhā chin‍nā; tasmā vimaticchaidanā pucchāpi natthiyaiva| avasaisā pana dvai pucchā buddhānaṃ atthi, tāsu ayaṃ kathaitukamyatā pucchā nāma|

8. idāni taṃ kathaitukamyatāya pucchāya pucchitamatthaṃ kathaituṃ ‘‘pāṇātipātaṃ pahāyā’’tiādimāha|

tattha pāṇassa atipātau pāṇātipātau, pāṇavadhau, pāṇaghātauti vuttaṃ hauti| pāṇauti caittha vauhāratau sattau, paramatthatau jīvitindriyaṃ, tasmiṃ pana pāṇai pāṇasañ‍ñinau jīvitindriyupacchaidakaupak‍kamasamuṭṭhāpikā kāyavacīdvārānaṃ añ‍ñataradvārappavattā vadhakacaitanā pāṇātipātau| sau guṇavirahitaisu tiracchānagatādīsu pāṇaisu khuddakai pāṇai appasāvaj‍jau, mahāsarīrai mahāsāvaj‍jau, kasmā? payaugamahantatāya| payaugasamattaipi vatthumahantatāya| guṇavantaisu manussādīsu appaguṇai pāṇai appasāvaj‍jau, mahāguṇai mahāsāvaj‍jau| sarīraguṇānaṃ pana samabhāvai sati kilaisānaṃ upak‍kamānañ‍ca mudutāya appasāvaj‍jau, tibbatāya mahāsāvaj‍jauti vaiditabbau|

tassa  pañ‍ca sambhārā haunti – pāṇau, pāṇasañ‍ñitā, vadhakacittaṃ, upak‍kamau, taina maraṇanti| cha payaugā – sāhatthikau, āṇattikau, nissaggiyau, thāvarau, vij‍jāmayau, iddhimayauti| imasmiṃ panatthai vitthāriyamānai ativiya papañ‍cau hauti, tasmā taṃ na vitthārayāma, añ‍ñañ‍ca aivarūpaṃ| atthikaihi pana samantapāsādikaṃ vinayaṭṭhakathaṃ aulaukaitvā gahaitabbaṃ|

pahāyāti imaṃ pāṇātipātacaitanāsaṅkhātaṃ dussīlyaṃ pajahitvā| paṭiviratauti pahīnakālatau paṭṭhāya tatau dussīlyatau auratau viratauva| natthi tassa vītik‍kamissāmīti cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā pagaiva kāyikāti imināva nayaina añ‍ñaisupi aivarūpaisu padaisu atthau vaiditabbau|

samaṇauti bhagavā samitapāpatāya laddhavauhārau| gautamauti gauttavasaina| na kaivalañ‍ca bhagavāyaiva  pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau, bhikkhusaṅghaupi paṭiviratau, daisanā pana āditau paṭṭhāya aivaṃ āgatā, atthaṃ pana dīpaintaina bhikkhusaṅghavasaināpi dīpaituṃ vaṭṭati|

nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthauti parūpaghātatthāya daṇḍaṃ vā satthaṃ vā ādāya avattanatau nikkhittadaṇḍau caiva nikkhittasatthau cāti atthau| aittha ca ṭhapaitvā daṇḍaṃ sabbampi avasaisaṃ upakaraṇaṃ sattānaṃ vihaiṭhanabhāvatau satthanti vaiditabbaṃ| yaṃ pana bhikkhū kattaradaṇḍaṃ vā dantakaṭṭhaṃ vā vāsiṃ pipphalikaṃ vā gahaitvā vicaranti, na taṃ parūpaghātatthāya| tasmā nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

laj‍jīti pāpajigucchanalakkhaṇāya laj‍jāya saman‍nāgatau| dayāpan‍nauti dayaṃ maittacittataṃ āpan‍nau| sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampīti; sabbai pāṇabhūtai hitaina anukampakau| tāya dayāpan‍natāya sabbaisaṃ pāṇabhūtānaṃ hitacittakauti atthau| viharatīti iriyati yapaiti yāpaiti pālaiti| iti vā hi, bhikkhavaiti aivaṃ vā bhikkhavai| vā saddau upari ‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ  pahāyā’’tiādīni apaikkhitvā vikappatthau vuttau, aivaṃ sabbattha purimaṃ vā pacchimaṃ vā apaikkhitvā vikappabhāvau vaiditabbau|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipau – bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘samaṇau gautamau pāṇaṃ na hanati, na ghātaiti, na tattha samanuñ‍ñau hauti, viratau imasmā dussīlyā; ahau, vata rai buddhaguṇā mahantā’’ti, iti mahantaṃ ussāhaṃ katvā vaṇṇaṃ vattukāmaupi appamattakaṃ auramattakaṃ ācārasīlamattakamaiva vakkhati| upari asādhāraṇabhāvaṃ nissāya vaṇṇaṃ vattuṃ na sakkhissati| na kaivalañ‍ca puthuj‍janauva sautāpan‍nasakadāgāmianāgāmiarahantaupi pac‍caikabuddhāpi na sak‍kauntiyaiva; tathāgatauyaiva pana sak‍kauti, taṃ vau upari vakkhāmīti, ayamaittha sādhippāyā atthavaṇṇanā| itau paraṃ pana apubbapadamaiva vaṇṇayissāma|

adin‍nādānaṃ pahāyāti aittha adin‍nassa ādānaṃ adin‍nādānaṃ, parasaṃharaṇaṃ, thaiyyaṃ, caurikāti vuttaṃ hauti| tattha adin‍nanti parapariggahitaṃ, yattha parau yathākāmakāritaṃ āpaj‍jantau adaṇḍārahau anupavaj‍jau ca hauti| tasmiṃ parapariggahitai parapariggahitasañ‍ñinau, tadādāyakaupak‍kamasamuṭṭhāpikā thaiyyacaitanā adin‍nādānaṃ| taṃ hīnai parasantakai appasāvaj‍jaṃ, paṇītai mahāsāvaj‍jaṃ, kasmā? vatthupaṇītatāya| vatthusamattai sati guṇādhikānaṃ santakai vatthusmiṃ mahāsāvaj‍jaṃ| taṃ taṃ guṇādhikaṃ upādāya tatau tatau hīnaguṇassa santakai vatthusmiṃ appasāvaj‍jaṃ|

tassa pañ‍ca sambhārā haunti – parapariggahitaṃ, parapariggahitasañ‍ñitā, thaiyyacittaṃ, upak‍kamau, taina haraṇanti| cha payaugā – sāhatthikādayauva| tai ca khau yathānurūpaṃ thaiyyāvahārau, pasayhāvahārau, paṭicchan‍nāvahārau, parikappāvahārau, kusāvahārauti imaisaṃ avahārānaṃ vasaina pavattā, ayamaittha saṅkhaipau| vitthārau pana samantapāsādikāyaṃ vuttau|

din‍namaiva  ādiyatīti din‍nādāyī| cittainapi din‍namaiva paṭikaṅkhatīti din‍napāṭikaṅkhī| thainaitīti thainau| na thainaina athainaina| athainattāyaiva sucibhūtaina| attanāti attabhāvaina| athainaṃ sucibhūtaṃ attānaṃ katvā viharatīti vuttaṃ hauti| saisaṃ paṭhamasikkhāpadai vuttanayainaiva yaujaitabbaṃ| yathā ca idha, aivaṃ sabbattha|

abrahmacariyanti asaiṭṭhacariyaṃ| brahmaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ ācāraṃ caratīti brahmacārī| ārācārīti abrahmacariyatau dūracārī| maithunāti rāgapariyuṭṭhānavasaina sadisattā maithunakāti laddhavauhāraihi paṭisaivitabbatau maithunāti saṅkhyaṃ gatā asaddhammā| gāmadhammāti gāmavāsīnaṃ dhammā|

9. musāvādaṃ pahāyāti aittha musāti visaṃvādanapuraikkhārassa atthabhañ‍janakau vacīpayaugau kāyapayaugau, vā visaṃvādanādhippāyaina panassa paravisaṃvādakakāyavacīpayaugasamuṭṭhāpikā caitanā musāvādau|

aparau nayau, ‘musā’ti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ vatthu| ‘vādau’ti tassa bhūtatau tacchatau viñ‍ñāpanaṃ| lakkhaṇatau pana atathaṃ vatthuṃ tathatau paraṃ viñ‍ñāpaitukāmassa tathāviñ‍ñattisamuṭṭhāpikā caitanā musāvādau| sau yamatthaṃ bhañ‍jati, tassa appatāya appasāvaj‍jau, mahantatāya mahāsāvaj‍jau|

api ca gahaṭṭhānaṃ attanau santakaṃ adātukāmatāya natthītiādinayappavattau appasāvaj‍jau, sakkhinā hutvā atthabhañ‍janatthaṃ vuttau mahāsāvaj‍jau, pabbajitānaṃ appakampi tailaṃ vā sappiṃ vā labhitvā hasādhippāyaina – ‘‘aj‍ja gāmai tailaṃ nadī mañ‍ñai sandatī’’ti pūraṇakathānayaina  pavattau appasāvaj‍jau, adiṭṭhaṃyaiva pana diṭṭhantiādinā nayaina vadantānaṃ mahāsāvaj‍jau|

tassa cattārau sambhārā haunti – atathaṃ vatthu, visaṃvādanacittaṃ, taj‍jau vāyāmau, parassa tadatthavijānananti| aikau payaugau sāhatthikauva| sau kāyaina vā kāyapaṭibaddhaina vā vācāya  vā paravisaṃvādanakiriyākaraṇaina daṭṭhabbau| tāya cai kiriyāya parau tamatthaṃ jānāti, ayaṃ kiriyasamuṭṭhāpikacaitanākkhaṇaiyaiva musāvādakammunā bajjhati|

yasmā  pana yathā kāyakāyapaṭibaddhavācāhi paraṃ visaṃvādaiti, tathā ‘‘idamassa bhaṇāhī’’ti āṇāpaintaupi paṇṇaṃ likhitvā puratau nissaj‍jantaupi, ‘‘ayamatthau aivaṃ daṭṭhabbau’’ti kuḍḍādīsu likhitvā ṭhapaintaupi| tasmā aittha āṇattikanissaggiyathāvarāpi payaugā yuj‍janti, aṭṭhakathāsu pana anāgatattā vīmaṃsitvā gahaitabbā|

sac‍caṃ vadatīti sac‍cavādī| sac‍caina sac‍caṃ sandahati ghaṭaitīti sac‍casandhau| na antarantarā musā vadatīti atthau| yau hi purisau kadāci musā vadati, kadāci sac‍caṃ, tassa musāvādaina antaritattā sac‍caṃ sac‍caina na ghaṭīyati; tasmā sau na sac‍casandhau| ayaṃ pana na tādisau, jīvitahaitupi musā avatvā sac‍caina sac‍caṃ sandahati yaivāti sac‍casandhau|

thaitauti thirau thirakathauti atthau| aikau hi puggalau haliddirāgau viya, thusarāsimhi nikhātakhāṇu viya, assapiṭṭhai ṭhapitakumbhaṇḍamiva ca na thirakathau hauti, aikau pāsāṇalaikhā viya, indakhīlau viya ca thirakathau hauti, asinā sīsaṃ chindantaipi dvai kathā na kathaiti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati thaitau|

pac‍cayikauti pattiyāyitabbakau, saddhāyitabbakauti atthau| aikac‍cau hi puggalau na pac‍cayikau hauti, ‘‘idaṃ kaina vuttaṃ, asukainā’’ti vuttai ‘‘mā tassa vacanaṃ saddahathā’’ti vattabbataṃ āpaj‍jati| aikau pac‍cayikau hauti, ‘‘idaṃ kaina vuttaṃ, asukainā’’ti vuttai ‘‘yadi taina vuttaṃ, idamaiva pamāṇaṃ, idāni upaparikkhitabbaṃ natthi, aivamaiva ida’’nti vattabbataṃ āpaj‍jati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati pac‍cayikau| avisaṃvādakau laukassāti tāya sac‍cavāditāya laukaṃ na visaṃvādaitīti atthau|

pisuṇaṃ  vācaṃ pahāyātiādīsu yāya vācāya yassa taṃ vācaṃ  bhāsati, tassa hadayai attanau piyabhāvaṃ, parassa ca suñ‍ñabhāvaṃ karauti, sā pisuṇā vācā|

yāya pana attānampi parampi pharusaṃ karauti, yā vācā sayampi pharusā, naiva kaṇṇasukhā na hadayaṅgamā, ayaṃ pharusā vācā|

yaina  samphaṃ palapati niratthakaṃ, sau samphappalāpau|

taisaṃ mūlabhūtā caitanāpi pisuṇavācādināmaiva labhati, sā aiva ca idhādhippaitāti|

tattha saṃkiliṭṭhacittassa paraisaṃ vā bhaidāya attanau piyakamyatāya vā kāyavacīpayaugasamuṭṭhāpikā caitanā pisuṇavācā| sā yassa bhaidaṃ karauti, tassa appaguṇatāya appasāvaj‍jā, mahāguṇatāya mahāsāvaj‍jā|

tassā cattārau sambhārā – bhinditabbau parau, ‘‘iti imai nānā bhavissanti, vinā bhavissantī’’ti bhaidapuraikkhāratā vā, ‘‘iti ahaṃ piyau bhavissāmi vissāsikau’’ti piyakamyatā vā, taj‍jau vāyāmau, tassa tadatthavijānananti| imaisaṃ bhaidāyāti, yaisaṃ itauti vuttānaṃ santikai sutaṃ taisaṃ bhaidāya|

bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātāti dvin‍naṃ mittānaṃ vā samānupajjhāyakādīnaṃ vā kainacidaiva kāraṇaina bhin‍nānaṃ aikamaikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā ‘‘tumhākaṃ īdisai kulai jātānaṃ aivaṃ bahussutānaṃ idaṃ na yutta’’ntiādīni vatvā sandhānaṃ kattā anukattā| anuppadātāti sandhānānuppadātā| dvai janai samaggai disvā – ‘‘tumhākaṃ aivarūpai kulai jātānaṃ aivarūpaihi guṇaihi saman‍nāgatānaṃ anucchavikamaita’’ntiādīni vatvā daḷhīkammaṃ kattāti atthau| samaggau ārāmau assāti samaggārāmau| yattha samaggā natthi, tattha vasitumpi na icchatīti atthau| samaggarāmautipi pāḷi, ayamaivaittha atthau| samaggaratauti samaggaisu ratau, tai pahāya añ‍ñattha gantumpi na icchatīti atthau| samaggai disvāpi sutvāpi nandatīti samagganandī, samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitāti yā vācā sattai samaggaiyaiva karauti, taṃ sāmaggiguṇaparidīpikamaiva vācaṃ bhāsati, na itaranti|

parassa  mammacchaidakakāyavacīpayaugasamuṭṭhāpikā aikantapharusacaitanā pharusāvācā| tassā āvibhāvatthamidaṃ vatthu – aikau kira dārakau mātuvacanaṃ anādiyitvā arañ‍ñaṃ gacchati, taṃ mātā nivattaitumasak‍kauntī – ‘‘caṇḍā taṃ mahiṃsī anubandhatū’’ti ak‍kausi| athassa tathaiva arañ‍ñai mahiṃsī  uṭṭhāsi| dārakau ‘‘yaṃ mama mātā mukhaina kathaisi, taṃ mā hautu, yaṃ cittaina cintaisi taṃ hautū’’ti, sac‍cakiriyamakāsi| mahiṃsī tatthaiva baddhā viya aṭṭhāsi| aivaṃ mammacchaidakaupi payaugau cittasaṇhatāya na pharusā vācā hauti| mātāpitarau hi kadāci puttakai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘caurā vau khaṇḍākhaṇḍaṃ karauntū’’ti, uppalapattampi ca naisaṃ upari patantaṃ na icchanti| ācariyupajjhāyā ca kadāci nissitakai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘kiṃ imai ahirīkā anauttappinau caranti, niddhamatha nai’’ti, atha ca naisaṃ āgamādhigamasampattiṃ icchanti| yathā ca cittasaṇhatāya pharusā vācā na hauti, aivaṃ vacanasaṇhatāya apharusā vācā na hauti| na hi mārāpaitukāmassa – ‘‘imaṃ sukhaṃ sayāpaithā’’ti vacanaṃ apharusā vācā hauti, cittapharusatāya panaisā pharusā vācāva| sā yaṃ sandhāya pavattitā, tassa appaguṇatāya appasāvaj‍jā, mahāguṇatāya mahāsāvaj‍jā| tassā tayau sambhārā – ak‍kausitabbau parau, kupitacittaṃ, ak‍kausanāti|

nailāti ailaṃ vuc‍cati dausau, nāssā ailanti nailā, niddausāti atthau| ‘‘nailaṅgau saitapacchādau’’ti, (udā॰ 65) aittha vuttanailaṃ viya| kaṇṇasukhāti byañ‍janamadhuratāya kaṇṇānaṃ sukhā, sūcivijjhanaṃ viya kaṇṇasūlaṃ na janaiti| atthamadhuratāya sakalasarīrai kaupaṃ ajanaitvā paimaṃ janaitīti paimanīyā| hadayaṃ gacchati, appaṭihañ‍ñamānā sukhaina cittaṃ pavisatīti hadayaṅgamā|  guṇaparipuṇṇatāya purai bhavāti paurī purai saṃvaḍḍhanārī  viya sukumārātipi paurī| purassa aisātipi paurī| nagaravāsīnaṃ kathāti atthau| nagaravāsinau hi yuttakathā haunti| pitimattaṃ pitāti vadanti, bhātimattaṃ bhātāti vadanti, mātimattaṃ mātāti vadanti| aivarūpī kathā bahunau janassa kantā hautīti bahujanakantā| kantabhāvainaiva bahunau janassa manāpā cittavuḍḍhikarāti bahujanamanāpā|

anatthaviñ‍ñāpikā kāyavacīpayaugasamuṭṭhāpikā akusalacaitanā samphappalāpau| sau āsaivanamandatāya appasāvaj‍jau, āsaivanamahantatāya mahāsāvaj‍jau, tassa dvai sambhārā – bhāratayuddhasītāharaṇādiniratthakakathāpuraikkhāratā, tathārūpī kathā kathanañ‍ca|

kālaina  vadatīti kālavādī vattabbayuttakālaṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā vadatīti atthau| bhūtaṃ tathaṃ tacchaṃ  sabhāvamaiva vadatīti bhūtavādī|  diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikatthasan‍nissitamaiva katvā vadatīti atthavādī| navalaukuttaradhammasan‍nissitaṃ katvā vadatīti dhammavādī saṃvaravinayapahānavinayasan‍nissitaṃ katvā vadatīti vinayavādī|

nidhānaṃ vuc‍cati ṭhapanaukāsau, nidhānamassā atthīti nidhānavatī| hadayai nidhātabbayuttakaṃ vācaṃ bhāsitāti atthau| kālaināti aivarūpiṃ bhāsamānaupi ca – ‘‘ahaṃ nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsissāmī’’ti na akālaina bhāsati, yuttakālaṃ pana apaikkhitvāva bhāsatīti atthau| sāpadaisanti saupamaṃ, sakāraṇanti atthau| pariyantavatinti paricchaidaṃ dassaitvā yathāssā paricchaidau pañ‍ñāyati, aivaṃ bhāsatīti atthau| atthasaṃhitanti  anaikaihipi nayaihi vibhajantaina pariyādātuṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyatāya atthasampan‍naṃ bhāsati| yaṃ vā sau atthavādī atthaṃ vadati, taina atthaina sahitattā atthasaṃhitaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati, na añ‍ñaṃ nikkhipitvā añ‍ñaṃ bhāsatīti vuttaṃ hauti|

10. bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhāti mūlabījaṃ khandhabījaṃ phaḷubījaṃ aggabījaṃ bījabījanti pañ‍cavidhassa bījagāmassa caiva, yassa kassaci nīlatiṇarukkhādikassa bhūtagāmassa ca samārambhā, chaidanabhaidanapacanādibhāvaina vikaupanā paṭiviratauti atthau|

aikabhattikauti pātarāsabhattaṃ sāyamāsabhattanti dvai bhattāni, taisu pātarāsabhattaṃ antaumajjhanhikaina paricchin‍naṃ, itaraṃ majjhanhikatau uddhaṃ antau aruṇaina| tasmā antaumajjhanhikai dasakkhattuṃ bhuñ‍jamānaupi aikabhattikauva hauti| taṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘aikabhattikau’’ti|

rattiyā bhaujanaṃ ratti, tatau uparatauti rattūparatau| atik‍kantai majjhanhikai yāva sūriyatthaṅgamanā bhaujanaṃ vikālabhaujanaṃ nāma| tatau viratattā viratau vikālabhaujanā| kadā viratau? anaumānadītīrai pabbajitadivasatau paṭṭhāya|

sāsanassa ananulaumattā visūkaṃ paṭāṇībhūtaṃ dassananti visūkadassanaṃ| attanā nac‍cananac‍cāpanādivasaina nac‍cā ca gītā ca vāditā ca antamasau  mayūranac‍cādivasainapi pavattānaṃ nac‍cādīnaṃ visūkabhūtā dassanā cāti nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā| nac‍cādīni hi attanā  payaujaituṃ vā paraihi payaujāpaituṃ vā payuttāni passituṃ vā naiva bhikkhūnaṃ na bhikkhunīnañ‍ca vaṭṭanti|

mālādīsu mālāti yaṃ kiñ‍ci pupphaṃ| gandhanti yaṃ kiñ‍ci gandhajātaṃ| vilaipananti chavirāgakaraṇaṃ| tattha piḷandhantau dhāraiti nāma, ūnaṭṭhānaṃ pūraintau maṇḍaiti nāma, gandhavasaina chavirāgavasaina ca sādiyantau vibhūsaiti nāma| ṭhānaṃ vuc‍cati kāraṇaṃ| tasmā yāya  dussīlyacaitanāya tāni mālādhāraṇādīni mahājanau karauti, tatau paṭiviratauti atthau|

uc‍cāsayanaṃ vuc‍cati pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ| mahāsayananti akappiyapac‍cattharaṇaṃ| tatau viratauti atthau|

jātarūpanti suvaṇṇaṃ| rajatanti kahāpaṇau, lauhamāsakau, jatumāsakau, dārumāsakauti yai vauhāraṃ gacchanti| tassa ubhayassāpi paṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau, naiva naṃ uggaṇhāti, na uggaṇhāpaiti, na upanikkhittaṃ sādiyatīti atthau|

āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇāti, sālivīhiyavagaudhūmakaṅguvarakakudrūsakasaṅkhātassa sattavidhassāpi āmakadhañ‍ñassa paṭiggahaṇā| na kaivalañ‍ca aitaisaṃ paṭiggahaṇamaiva, āmasanampi bhikkhūnaṃ na vaṭṭatiyaiva| āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇāti aittha añ‍ñatra audissa anuñ‍ñātā āmakamaṃsamacchānaṃ paṭiggahaṇamaiva bhikkhūnaṃ na vaṭṭati, nau āmasanaṃ|

itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇāti aittha itthīti purisantaragatā, itarā kumārikā nāma, tāsaṃ paṭiggahaṇampi āmasanampi akappiyamaiva|

dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇāti aittha dāsidāsavasainaiva taisaṃ paṭiggahaṇaṃ na vaṭṭati| ‘‘kappiyakārakaṃ dammi, ārāmikaṃ dammī’’ti aivaṃ vuttai pana vaṭṭati|

ajaiḷakādīsu khaittavatthupariyausānaisu kappiyākappiyanayau vinayavasaina upaparikkhitabbau| tattha khaittaṃ nāma yasmiṃ pubbaṇṇaṃ ruhati| vatthu nāma yasmiṃ aparaṇṇaṃ ruhati| yattha vā ubhayampi ruhati, taṃ khaittaṃ| tadatthāya  akatabhūmibhāgau vatthu|  khaittavatthusīsaina caittha vāpitaḷākādīnipi saṅgahitānaiva|

dūtaiyyaṃ  vuc‍cati dūtakammaṃ, gihīnaṃ pahitaṃ paṇṇaṃ vā sāsanaṃ vā gahaitvā tattha tattha gamanaṃ| pahiṇagamanaṃ vuc‍cati gharā gharaṃ paisitassa khuddakagamanaṃ| anuyaugau nāma tadubhayakaraṇaṃ| tasmā dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānaṃ anuyaugāti| aivamaittha atthau vaiditabbau|

kayavik‍kayāti kayā ca vik‍kayā ca| tulākūṭādīsu  kūṭanti vañ‍canaṃ| tattha tulākūṭaṃ nāma rūpakūṭaṃ aṅgakūṭaṃ, gahaṇakūṭaṃ, paṭicchan‍nakūṭanti catubbidhaṃ hauti| tattha rūpakūṭaṃ nāma dvai tulā samarūpā katvā gaṇhantau mahatiyā gaṇhāti, dadantau khuddikāya daiti| aṅgakūṭaṃ nāma gaṇhantau pacchābhāgai hatthaina tulaṃ ak‍kamati, dadantau pubbabhāgai| gahaṇakūṭaṃ nāma gaṇhantau mūlai raj‍juṃ gaṇhāti, dadantau aggai| paṭicchan‍nakūṭaṃ nāma tulaṃ susiraṃ katvā antau ayacuṇṇaṃ pakkhipitvā gaṇhantau taṃ pacchābhāgai karauti, dadantau aggabhāgai|

kaṃsau vuc‍cati suvaṇṇapāti, tāya vañ‍canaṃ kaṃsakūṭaṃ| kathaṃ? aikaṃ suvaṇṇapātiṃ katvā añ‍ñā dvai tissau lauhapātiyau suvaṇṇavaṇṇai karauti, tatau janapadaṃ gantvā kiñ‍cidaiva aḍḍhaṃ kulaṃ pavisitvā – ‘‘suvaṇṇabhājanāni kiṇathā’’ti vatvā agghai pucchitai samagghataraṃ dātukāmā haunti| tatau taihi – ‘‘kathaṃ imaisaṃ suvaṇṇabhāvau jānitabbau’’ti vuttai, ‘‘vīmaṃsitvā gaṇhathā’’ti suvaṇṇapātiṃ pāsāṇai ghaṃsitvā sabbā pātiyau datvā gacchati|

mānakūṭaṃ nāma hadayabhaidasikhābhaidaraj‍jubhaidavasaina tividhaṃ hauti| tattha hadayabhaidau sappitailādiminanakālai labbhati| tāni hi gaṇhantau haiṭṭhāchiddaina mānaina – ‘‘saṇikaṃ āsiñ‍cā’’ti vatvā antaubhājanai bahuṃ paggharāpaitvā gaṇhāti, dadantau chiddaṃ pidhāya sīghaṃ pūraitvā daiti|

sikhābhaidau tilataṇḍulādiminanakālai labbhati| tāni hi gaṇhantau saṇikaṃ sikhaṃ ussāpaitvā gaṇhāti, dadantau vaigaina pūraitvā sikhaṃ chindantau daiti|

raj‍jubhaidau  khaittavatthuminanakālai labbhati| lañ‍jaṃ alabhantā hi khaittaṃ amahantampi mahantaṃ katvā minanti|

uk‍kauṭanādīsu uk‍kauṭananti assāmikai sāmikai kātuṃ lañ‍jaggahaṇaṃ| vañ‍cananti taihi taihi upāyaihi paraisaṃ vañ‍canaṃ| tatridamaikaṃ vatthu – aikau kira luddakau migañ‍ca migapautakañ‍ca gahaitvā  āgacchati, tamaikau dhuttau – ‘‘kiṃ bhau, migau agghati, kiṃ migapautakau’’ti āha| ‘‘migau dvai kahāpaṇai, migapautakau aika’’nti ca vuttai aikaṃ kahāpaṇaṃ datvā migapautakaṃ gahaitvā thaukaṃ gantvā nivattau – ‘‘na mai bhau, migapautakaina atthau, migaṃ mai daihī’’ti āha| taina hi – dvai kahāpaṇai daihīti| sau āha – ‘‘nanu tai bhau, mayā paṭhamaṃ aikau kahāpaṇau din‍nau’’ti? ‘‘āma, din‍nau’’ti| ‘‘idaṃ migapautakaṃ gaṇha, aivaṃ sau ca kahāpaṇau, ayañ‍ca kahāpaṇagghanakau migapautakauti dvai kahāpaṇā bhavissantī’’ti| sau ‘‘kāraṇaṃ vadatī’’ti sal‍lakkhaitvā migapautakaṃ gahaitvā migaṃ adāsīti| nikatīti yaugavasaina vā māyāvasaina vā apāmaṅgaṃ pāmaṅganti, amaṇiṃ maṇinti, asuvaṇṇaṃ suvaṇṇanti katvā patirūpakaina vañ‍canaṃ| sāciyaugauti kuṭilayaugau, aitaisaṃyaiva uk‍kauṭanādīnamaitaṃ nāmaṃ| tasmā – uk‍kauṭanasāciyaugau, vañ‍canasāciyaugau, nikatisāciyaugauti, aivamaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau| kaici añ‍ñaṃ dassaitvā añ‍ñassa parivattanaṃ sāciyaugauti vadanti| taṃ pana vañ‍canainaiva saṅgahitaṃ|

chaidanādīsu chaidananti hatthacchaidanādi| vadhauti māraṇaṃ| bandhauti raj‍jubandhanādīhi bandhanaṃ| viparāmausauti himaviparāmausau, gumbaviparāmausauti duvidhau| yaṃ himapātasamayai himaina paṭicchan‍nā hutvā maggappaṭipan‍naṃ janaṃ musanti, ayaṃ himaviparāmausau| yaṃ gumbādīhi paṭicchan‍nā musanti, ayaṃ gumbaviparāmausau| ālaupau vuc‍cati gāmanigamādīnaṃ vilaupakaraṇaṃ| sahasākārauti sāhasikakiriyā| gaihaṃ pavisitvā manussānaṃ urai satthaṃ ṭhapaitvā icchitabhaṇḍānaṃ gahaṇaṃ| aivamaitasmā chaidana…pai॰… sahasākārā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamauti| iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyyāti|

aittāvatā cūḷasīlaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ hauti|

majjhimasīlavaṇṇanā

11. idāni  majjhimasīlaṃ vitthāraintau ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau’’tiādimāha| tatrāyaṃ anuttānapadavaṇṇanā| saddhādaiyyānīti kammañ‍ca phalañ‍ca idhalaukañ‍ca paralaukañ‍ca saddahitvā din‍nāni| ‘ayaṃ mai ñātī’ti vā, ‘mittau’ti vā, idaṃ paṭikarissati, idaṃ vā taina katapubbanti vā, aivaṃ na din‍nānīti atthau| aivaṃ din‍nāni hi na saddhādaiyyāni nāma haunti| bhaujanānīti daisanāsīsamattamaitaṃ, atthatau pana saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā cīvarāni pārupitvā saināsanāni saivamānā gilānabhaisaj‍jaṃ paribhuñ‍jamānāti sabbamaitaṃ vuttamaiva hauti|

saiyyathidanti nipātau| tassatthau katamau sau bījagāmabhūtagāmau, yassa samārambhaṃ anuyuttā viharantīti| tatau taṃ dassaintau mūlabījantiādimāha| tattha mūlabījaṃ nāma haliddi, siṅgivairaṃ, vacā, vacattaṃ, ativisā, kaṭukarauhiṇī, usīraṃ, bhaddamuttakanti aivamādi| khandhabījaṃ nāma assatthau, nigraudhau, pilakkhau, udumbarau, kacchakau, kapitthanauti aivamādi| phaḷubījaṃ nāma ucchu, naḷau, vaiḷūti aivamādi| aggabījaṃ nāma aj‍jakaṃ, phaṇij‍jakaṃ, hirivairanti aivamādi| bījabījaṃ nāma pubbaṇṇaṃ aparaṇṇanti aivamādi| sabbañhaitaṃ rukkhatau viyaujitaṃ viruhanasamatthamaiva ‘‘bījagāmau’’ti vuc‍cati| rukkhatau pana aviyaujitaṃ asukkhaṃ ‘‘bhūtagāmau’’ti vuc‍cati| tattha bhūtagāmasamārambhau pācittiyavatthu, bījagāmasamārambhau duk‍kaṭavatthūti vaiditabbau|

12. san‍nidhikāraparibhauganti san‍nidhikatassa paribhaugaṃ| tattha duvidhā kathā, vinayavasaina ca sal‍laikhavasaina ca| vinayavasaina tāva yaṃ kiñ‍ci an‍naṃ aj‍ja paṭiggahitaṃ aparaj‍ju san‍nidhikārakaṃ hauti, tassa paribhaugai pācittiyaṃ| attanā laddhaṃ pana sāmaṇairānaṃ datvā, taihi laddhaṃ ṭhapāpaitvā dutiyadivasai bhuñ‍jituṃ vaṭṭati, sal‍laikhau pana na hauti|

pānasan‍nidhimhipi aisaiva nayau| tattha pānaṃ nāma ambapānādīni aṭṭha pānāni, yāni ca taisaṃ anulaumāni| taisaṃ vinicchayau samantapāsādikāyaṃ vuttau|

vatthasan‍nidhimhi  anadhiṭṭhitaṃ avikappitaṃ san‍nidhi ca hauti, sal‍laikhañ‍ca kaupaiti, ayaṃ pariyāyakathā| nippariyāyatau pana ticīvarasantuṭṭhaina bhavitabbaṃ, catutthaṃ labhitvā añ‍ñassa dātabbaṃ| sacai yassa kassaci dātuṃ na sak‍kauti, yassa pana dātukāmau hauti, sau uddaisatthāya vā paripucchatthāya vā gatau, āgatamattai dātabbaṃ, adātuṃ na vaṭṭati| cīvarai pana appahauntai satiyā pac‍cāsāya anuñ‍ñātakālaṃ ṭhapaituṃ vaṭṭati| sūcisuttacīvarakārakānaṃ alābhaina tatau parampi vinayakammaṃ katvā ṭhapaituṃ vaṭṭati| ‘‘imasmiṃ jiṇṇai puna īdisaṃ kutau labhissāmī’’ti pana ṭhapaituṃ na vaṭṭati, san‍nidhi ca hauti, sal‍laikhañ‍ca kaupaiti|

yānasan‍nidhimhi  yānaṃ nāma vayhaṃ, rathau, sakaṭaṃ, sandamānikā, sivikā, pāṭaṅkīti; naitaṃ pabbajitassa yānaṃ| upāhanā pana pabbajitassa yānaṃyaiva| aikabhikkhussa hi aikau arañ‍ñatthāya, aikau dhautapādakatthāyāti, uk‍kaṃsatau dvai upāhanasaṅghāṭā vaṭṭanti| tatiyaṃ labhitvā añ‍ñassa dātabbau| ‘‘imasmiṃ jiṇṇai añ‍ñaṃ kutau labhissāmī’’ti hi ṭhapaituṃ na vaṭṭati, san‍nidhi ca hauti, sal‍laikhañ‍ca kaupaiti|

sayanasan‍nidhimhi sayananti mañ‍cau| aikassa bhikkhunau aikau gabbhai, aikau divāṭhānaiti uk‍kaṃsatau dvai mañ‍cā vaṭṭanti| tatau uttari labhitvā añ‍ñassa bhikkhunau vā gaṇassa vā dātabbau; adātuṃ na vaṭṭati| san‍nidhi ca hauti, sal‍laikhañ‍ca kaupaiti|

gandhasan‍nidhimhi bhikkhunau kaṇḍukacchuchavidausādiābādhai  sati gandhā vaṭṭanti| tai gandhai āharāpaitvā tasmiṃ raugai vūpasantai añ‍ñaisaṃ vā ābādhikānaṃ dātabbā, dvārai pañ‍caṅguligharadhūpanādīsu vā upanaitabbā| ‘‘puna raugai sati bhavissantī’’ti pana ṭhapaituṃ na vaṭṭati, san‍nidhi ca hauti, sal‍laikhañ‍ca kaupaiti|

āmisanti vuttāvasaisaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| saiyyathidaṃ, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu – ‘‘tathārūpai kālai upakārāya bhavissatī’’ti tilataṇḍulamuggamāsanāḷikairalauṇamacchamaṃsaval‍lūrasappitailaguḷabhājanādīni āharāpaitvā ṭhapaiti| sau vassakālai kālassaiva sāmaṇairaihi yāguṃ pacāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jitvā ‘‘sāmaṇaira, udakakaddamai dukkhaṃ gāmaṃ pavisituṃ, gaccha asukaṃ kulaṃ  gantvā mayhaṃ vihārai nisin‍nabhāvaṃ āraucaihi; asukakulatau dadhiādīni āharā’’ti paisaiti| bhikkhūhi – ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, gāmaṃ pavisissathā’’ti vuttaipi, ‘‘duppavaisau, āvusau, idāni gāmau’’ti vadati| tai – ‘‘hautu, bhantai, acchatha tumhai, mayaṃ bhikkhaṃ pariyaisitvā āharissāmā’’ti gacchanti| atha sāmaṇairaupi dadhiādīni āharitvā bhattañ‍ca byañ‍janañ‍ca sampādaitvā upanaiti, taṃ bhuñ‍jantassaiva upaṭṭhākā bhattaṃ pahiṇanti, tataupi manāpaṃ manāpaṃ bhuñ‍jati| atha bhikkhū piṇḍapātaṃ gahaitvā āgacchanti, tataupi manāpaṃ manāpaṃ gīvāyāmakaṃ bhuñ‍jatiyaiva| aivaṃ catumāsampi vītināmaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati – ‘‘bhikkhu muṇḍakuṭumbikajīvikaṃ jīvati, na samaṇajīvika’’nti| aivarūpau āmisasan‍nidhi nāma hauti|

bhikkhunau pana vasanaṭṭhānai aikā taṇḍulanāḷi, aikau guḷapiṇḍau, catubhāgamattaṃ sappīti aittakaṃ nidhaituṃ vaṭṭati, akālai sampattacaurānaṃ atthāya| tai hi aittakampi āmisapaṭisanthāraṃ alabhantā  jīvitāpi vauraupaiyyuṃ, tasmā sacai aittakaṃ natthi, āharāpaitvāpi  ṭhapaituṃ vaṭṭati| aphāsukakālai ca yadaittha kappiyaṃ, taṃ attanāpi paribhuñ‍jituṃ vaṭṭati| kappiyakuṭiyaṃ pana bahuṃ ṭhapaintassāpi san‍nidhi nāma natthi| tathāgatassa pana taṇḍulanāḷiādīsu vā yaṃ kiñ‍ci caturatanamattaṃ vā pilautikakhaṇḍaṃ ‘‘idaṃ mai aj‍ja vā svai vā bhavissatī’’ti ṭhapitaṃ nāma natthi|

13. visūkadassanaisu nac‍caṃ nāma yaṃ kiñ‍ci nac‍caṃ, taṃ maggaṃ gacchantaināpi gīvaṃ pasāraitvā daṭṭhuṃ na vaṭṭati| vitthāravinicchayau panaittha samantapāsādikāyaṃ vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbau| yathā caittha, aivaṃ sabbaisu sikkhāpadapaṭisaṃyuttaisu suttapadaisu| itau parañhi aittakampi avatvā tattha tattha payaujanamattamaiva vaṇṇayissāmāti|

paikkhanti naṭasamaj‍jaṃ| akkhānanti bhāratayujjhanādikaṃ| yasmiṃ ṭhānai kathīyati, tattha gantumpi na vaṭṭati| pāṇissaranti kaṃsatāḷaṃ, pāṇitāḷantipi vadanti| vaitāḷanti ghanatāḷaṃ, mantaina matasarīruṭṭhāpanantipi aikai| kumbhathūṇanti caturassaambaṇakatāḷaṃ, kumbhasaddantipi aikai| saubhanakanti naṭānaṃ  abbhauk‍kiraṇaṃ, saubhanakaraṃ vā, paṭibhānacittanti vuttaṃ hauti| caṇḍālanti ayauguḷakīḷā, caṇḍālānaṃ sāṇadhauvanakīḷātipi vadanti| vaṃsanti vaiḷuṃ ussāpaitvā kīḷanaṃ|

dhauvananti aṭṭhidhauvanaṃ, aikac‍caisu kira janapadaisu kālaṅkatai ñātakai na jhāpainti, nikhaṇitvā ṭhapainti| atha naisaṃ pūtibhūtaṃ kāyaṃ ñatvā nīharitvā aṭṭhīni dhauvitvā gandhaihi makkhaitvā ṭhapainti| tai nakkhattakālai aikasmiṃ ṭhānai aṭṭhīni ṭhapaitvā aikasmiṃ ṭhānai surādīni ṭhapaitvā raudantā paridaivantā suraṃ pivanti| vuttampi caitaṃ – ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇaisu janapadaisu aṭṭhidhauvanaṃ nāma, tattha hauti an‍nampi pānampi khaj‍jampi bhauj‍jampi laiyyampi paiyyampi nac‍campi gītampi vāditampi| atthaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhauvanaṃ, naitaṃ natthīti vadāmī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 10.107)| aikac‍cai pana indajālaina aṭṭhidhauvanaṃ dhauvanantipi vadanti|

hatthiyuddhādīsu bhikkhunau naiva hatthiādīhi saddhiṃ yujjhituṃ, na tai yujjhāpaituṃ, na yujjhantai daṭṭhuṃ vaṭṭati| nibbuddhanti mal‍layuddhaṃ| uyyaudhikanti yattha sampahārau dissati| balagganti balagaṇanaṭṭhānaṃ| sainābyūhanti sainānivaisau, sakaṭabyūhādivasaina saināya nivaisanaṃ| anīkadassananti – ‘‘tayau hatthī pacchimaṃ hatthānīka’’ntiādinā (pāci॰ 324) nayaina vuttassa anīkassa dassanaṃ|

14. pamādau  aittha tiṭṭhatīti pamādaṭṭhānaṃ| jūtañ‍ca taṃ pamādaṭṭhānañ‍cāti jūtappamādaṭṭhānaṃ| aikaikāya pantiyā aṭṭha aṭṭha padāni assāti aṭṭhapadaṃ dasapadaipi aisaiva nayau| ākāsanti aṭṭhapadadasapadaisu viya ākāsaiyaiva kīḷanaṃ| parihārapathanti bhūmiyaṃ nānāpathamaṇḍalaṃ katvā tattha tattha pariharitabbaṃ, pathaṃ pariharantānaṃ kīḷanaṃ| santikanti santikakīḷanaṃ| aikajjhaṃ ṭhapitā sāriyau vā sakkharāyau vā acālaintā nakhainaiva apanainti ca upanainti ca, sacai tattha kāci calati, parājayau hauti, aivarūpāya kīḷāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| khalikanti jūtaphalakai pāsakakīḷanaṃ| ghaṭikā vuc‍cati dīghadaṇḍakaina rassadaṇḍakaṃ paharaṇakīḷanaṃ| salākahatthanti lākhāya vā mañ‍jiṭṭhikāya vā piṭṭhaudakaina vā salākahatthaṃ  taimaitvā – ‘‘kiṃ  hautū’’ti bhūmiyaṃ vā bhittiyaṃ vā taṃ paharitvā hatthiassādirūpadassanakīḷanaṃ| akkhanti guḷakīḷā| paṅgacīraṃ vuc‍cati paṇṇanāḷikaṃ, taṃ dhamantā kīḷanti| vaṅkakanti gāmadārakānaṃ kīḷanakaṃ khuddakanaṅgalaṃ| maukkhacikā vuc‍cati samparivattanakīḷā, ākāsai vā daṇḍakaṃ gahaitvā bhūmiyaṃ vā sīsaṃ ṭhapaitvā haiṭṭhupariyabhāvaina parivattanakīḷāti vuttaṃ hauti| ciṅgulikaṃ vuc‍cati tālapaṇṇādīhi kataṃ vātappahāraina paribbhamanacak‍kaṃ| pattāḷhakaṃ vuc‍cati paṇṇanāḷikā| tāya vālukādīni minantā kīḷanti| rathakanti khuddakarathaṃ| dhanukanti khuddakadhanumaiva| akkharikā vuc‍cati ākāsai vā piṭṭhiyaṃ vā akkharajānanakīḷā| manaisikā nāma manasā cintitajānanakīḷā| yathāvaj‍jaṃ nāma kāṇakuṇikhuj‍jādīnaṃ yaṃ yaṃ vaj‍jaṃ, taṃ taṃ payaujaitvā dassanakīḷā|

15. āsandinti pamāṇātik‍kantāsanaṃ| anuyuttā viharantīti idaṃ apaikkhitvā pana sabbapadaisu upayaugavacanaṃ kataṃ| pal‍laṅkauti pādaisu vāḷarūpāni ṭhapaitvā katau| gaunakauti dīghalaumakau mahākaujavau, caturaṅgulādhikāni kira tassa laumāni| cittakanti vānavicittaṃ uṇṇāmayattharaṇaṃ| paṭikāti uṇṇāmayau saitattharaṇau| paṭalikāti  ghanapupphakau uṇṇāmayattharaṇau| yau āmalakapattautipi vuc‍cati| tūlikāti tiṇṇaṃ tūlānaṃ añ‍ñatarapuṇṇā tūlikā| vikatikāti sīhabyagghādirūpavicitrau uṇṇāmayattharaṇau| uddalaumīti ubhayataudasaṃ uṇṇāmayattharaṇaṃ, kaici ‘‘aikatauuggatapuppha’’nti vadanti| aikantalaumīti aikataudasaṃ uṇṇāmayattharaṇaṃ| kaici ‘‘ubhatauuggatapuppha’’nti vadanti| kaṭṭissanti ratanaparisibbitaṃ kausaiyyakaṭṭissamayapac‍cattharaṇaṃ| kausaiyyanti ratanaparisibbitamaiva kausiyasuttamayapac‍cattharaṇaṃ| suddhakausaiyyaṃ pana vaṭṭatīti vinayai vuttaṃ| dīghanikāyaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘ṭhapaitvā tūlikaṃ sabbānaiva gaunakādīni ratanaparisibbitāni na vaṭṭantī’’ti vuttaṃ|

kuttakanti sauḷasan‍naṃ nāṭakitthīnaṃ ṭhatvā nac‍canayauggaṃ uṇṇāmayattharaṇaṃ| hatthattharaṃ assattharanti  hatthiassapiṭṭhīsu attharaṇaattharakāyaiva| rathattharaipi aisaiva nayau| ajinappavaiṇīti ajinacammaihi mañ‍cappamāṇaina sibbitvā katā  pavaiṇī| kadalīmigapavarapac‍cattharaṇanti kadalīmigacammaṃ nāma atthi, taina kataṃ pavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ; uttamapac‍cattharaṇanti atthau| taṃ kira saitavatthassa upari kadalīmigacammaṃ pattharitvā sibbaitvā karaunti| sauttaracchadanti saha uttaracchadaina, uparibaddhaina rattavitānaina saddhinti atthau| saitavitānampi haiṭṭhā akappiyapac‍cattharaṇai sati na vaṭṭati, asati pana vaṭṭati| ubhataulauhitakūpadhānanti sīsūpadhānañ‍ca pādūpadhānañ‍cāti  mañ‍cassa ubhataulauhitakaṃ upadhānaṃ, aitaṃ na kappati| yaṃ pana aikamaiva upadhānaṃ ubhausu passaisu rattaṃ vā hauti padumavaṇṇaṃ vā vicitraṃ vā, sacai pamāṇayuttaṃ, vaṭṭati| mahāupadhānaṃ pana paṭikkhittaṃ| alauhitakāni dvaipi vaṭṭantiyaiva| tatau uttari labhitvā añ‍ñaisaṃ dātabbāni| dātuṃ asak‍kauntau mañ‍cai tiriyaṃ attharitvā upari pac‍cattharaṇaṃ datvā nipaj‍jitumpi labhati| āsandīādīsu pana vuttanayainaiva paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āsandiyā pādai chinditvā paribhuñ‍jituṃ, pal‍laṅkassa vāḷai bhinditvā paribhuñ‍jituṃ, tūlikaṃ vijaṭaitvā bimbauhanaṃ kātuṃ, avasaisaṃ bhummattharaṇaṃ kātu’’nti (cūḷava॰ 297)|

16. ucchādanādīsu mātukucchitau nikkhantadārakānaṃ sarīragandhau dvādasavassapattakālai nassati, taisaṃ sarīraduggandhaharaṇatthāya gandhacuṇṇādīhi ucchādainti, aivarūpaṃ ucchādanaṃ na vaṭṭati| puñ‍ñavantai pana dārakai ūrūsu nipaj‍jāpaitvā tailaina makkhaitvā hatthapādaūrunābhiādīnaṃ saṇṭhānasampādanatthaṃ parimaddanti, aivarūpaṃ parimaddanaṃ na vaṭṭati|

nhāpananti taisaṃyaiva dārakānaṃ gandhādīhi nhāpanaṃ| sambāhananti mahāmal‍lānaṃ viya hatthapādai muggarādīhi paharitvā bāhuvaḍḍhanaṃ| ādāsanti yaṃ kiñ‍ci ādāsaṃ pariharituṃ na vaṭṭati| añ‍jananti alaṅkārañ‍janamaiva| mālāti baddhamālā vā abaddhamālā vā| vilaipananti yaṃ kiñ‍ci chavirāgakaraṇaṃ| mukhacuṇṇaṃ mukhalaipananti mukhai kāḷapīḷakādīnaṃ haraṇatthāya mattikakak‍kaṃ dainti, taina lauhitai calitai sāsapakak‍kaṃ dainti, taina dausai khāditai tilakak‍kaṃ dainti, taina lauhitai san‍nisin‍nai haliddikak‍kaṃ dainti, taina chavivaṇṇai ārūḷhai mukhacuṇṇakaina mukhaṃ cuṇṇainti, taṃ sabbaṃ na vaṭṭati|

hatthabandhādīsu  hatthai vicitrasaṅkhakapālādīni bandhitvā vicaranti, taṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā sabbampi hatthābharaṇaṃ na vaṭṭati, aparai sikhaṃ bandhitvā vicaranti| suvaṇṇacīrakamuttalatādīhi ca  taṃ parikkhipanti; taṃ sabbaṃ na vaṭṭati| aparai catuhatthadaṇḍaṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā pana alaṅkatadaṇḍakaṃ gahaitvā vicaranti, tathā itthipurisarūpādivicittaṃ bhaisaj‍janāḷikaṃ suparikkhittaṃ vāmapassai aulaggitaṃ; aparai kaṇṇikaratanaparikkhittakausaṃ atitikhiṇaṃ asiṃ, pañ‍cavaṇṇasuttasibbitaṃ makaradantakādivicittaṃ chattaṃ, suvaṇṇarajatādivicitrā maurapiñchādiparikkhittā upāhanā, kaici ratanamattāyāmaṃ caturaṅgulavitthataṃ kaisantaparicchaidaṃ dassaitvā maighamukhai vij‍julataṃ viya nalāṭai uṇhīsapaṭṭaṃ bandhanti, cūḷāmaṇiṃ dhārainti, cāmaravālabījaniṃ dhārainti, taṃ sabbaṃ na vaṭṭati|

17. aniyyānikattā saggamaukkhamaggānaṃ tiracchānabhūtā kathāti tiracchānakathā| tattha rājānaṃ ārabbha mahāsammatau mandhātā dhammāsaukau aivaṃ mahānubhāvautiādinā nayaina pavattā kathā rājakathā| aisa nayau caurakathādīsu| taisu asukau rājā abhirūpau dassanīyautiādinā nayaina gaihassitakathāva tiracchānakathā hauti| saupi nāma aivaṃ mahānubhāvau khayaṃ gatauti aivaṃ pavattā pana kammaṭṭhānabhāvai tiṭṭhati| cauraisu mūladaivau aivaṃ mahānubhāvau, maighamālau aivaṃ mahānubhāvauti taisaṃ kammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahau sūrāti gaihassitakathāva tiracchānakathā| yuddhaipi bhāratayuddhādīsu asukaina asukau aivaṃ māritau, aivaṃ viddhauti kāmassādavasainaiva kathā tiracchānakathā| taipi nāma khayaṃ gatāti aivaṃ pavattā pana sabbattha kammaṭṭhānamaiva hauti| api ca an‍nādīsu aivaṃ vaṇṇavantaṃ gandhavantaṃ rasavantaṃ phassasampan‍naṃ khādimha bhuñ‍jimhāti kāmassādavasaina kathaituṃ na vaṭṭati| sātthakaṃ  pana katvā pubbai aivaṃ vaṇṇādisampan‍naṃ an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ sayanaṃ mālaṃ gandhaṃ sīlavantānaṃ adamha, caitiyai pūjaṃ karimhāti kathaituṃ vaṭṭati| ñātikathādīsu pana ‘‘amhākaṃ ñātakā sūrā samatthā’’ti vā ‘‘pubbai mayaṃ aivaṃ vicitraihi yānaihi vicarimhā’’ti vā assādavasaina vattuṃ na vaṭṭati| sātthakaṃ pana katvā ‘‘taipi nau ñātakā khayaṃ gatā’’ti vā ‘‘pubbai mayaṃ aivarūpā upāhanā saṅghassa adamhā’’ti vā kathaituṃ vaṭṭati| gāmakathāpi suniviṭṭhadun‍niviṭṭhasubhikkhadubbhikkhādivasaina vā ‘‘asukagāmavāsinau sūrā samatthā’’ti  vā aivaṃ assādavasaina na vaṭṭati| sātthakaṃ pana katvā ‘‘saddhā pasan‍nā’’ti vā ‘‘khayavayaṃ gatā’’ti vā vattuṃ vaṭṭati| nigamanagarajanapadakathādīsupi aisaiva nayau|

itthikathāpi vaṇṇasaṇṭhānādīni paṭic‍ca assādavasaina na vaṭṭati, saddhā pasan‍nā khayavayaṃ gatāti aivamaiva vaṭṭati| sūrakathāpi ‘nandimittau nāma yaudhau sūrau ahausī’ti assādavasaina na vaṭṭati| saddhau ahausi khayaṃ gatauti aivamaiva vaṭṭati| visikhākathāpi ‘‘asukā  visikhā suniviṭṭhā dun‍niviṭṭhā sūrā samatthā’’ti assādavasaina na vaṭṭati| saddhā pasan‍nā khayavayaṃ gatāti aivamaiva vaṭṭati|

kumbhaṭṭhānakathāti udakaṭṭhānakathā, udakatitthakathātipi vuc‍cati, kumbhadāsikathā vā, sāpi ‘‘pāsādikā nac‍cituṃ gāyituṃ chaikā’’ti assādavasaina na vaṭṭati; saddhā pasan‍nātiādinā nayainaiva vaṭṭati| pubbapaitakathāti atītañātikathā| tattha vattamānañātikathāsadisau vinicchayau|

nānattakathāti purimapacchimakathāhi vimuttā avasaisā nānāsabhāvā niratthakakathā| laukakkhāyikāti ayaṃ laukau kaina nimmitau, asukaina nāma  nimmitau| kākau saitau, aṭṭhīnaṃ saitattā; balākā rattā| lauhitassa rattattāti aivamādikā laukāyatavitaṇḍasal‍lāpakathā|

samuddakkhāyikā nāma kasmā samuddau sāgarau? sāgaradaivaina khatau, tasmā sāgarau| khatau maiti hatthamuddāya sayaṃ nivaiditattā ‘‘samuddau’’ti aivamādikā niratthakā samuddakkhāyanakathā| bhavauti vuḍḍhi| abhavauti hāni| iti bhavau, iti abhavauti yaṃ vā taṃ vā niratthakakāraṇaṃ vatvā pavattitakathā itibhavābhavakathā|

18. viggāhikakathāti viggahakathā, sārambhakathā| tattha sahitaṃ maiti mayhaṃ vacanaṃ sahitaṃ siliṭṭhaṃ atthayuttaṃ kāraṇayuttanti atthau| asahitaṃ taiti tuyhaṃ vacanaṃ asahitaṃ asiliṭṭhaṃ| adhiciṇṇaṃ tai viparāvattanti yaṃ tuyhaṃ dīgharattāciṇṇavasaina suppaguṇaṃ, taṃ mayhaṃ aikavacanainaiva viparāvattaṃ parivattitvā ṭhitaṃ, na kiñ‍ci jānāsīti atthau|

āraupitau  tai vādauti mayā tava dausau āraupitau| cara vādappamaukkhāyāti dausamaucanatthaṃ cara, vicara; tattha tattha gantvā sikkhāti atthau| nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausīti atha sayaṃ pahausi, idānimaiva nibbaiṭhaihīti|

19. dūtaiyyakathāyaṃ idha gacchāti itau asukaṃ nāma ṭhānaṃ gaccha| amutrāgacchāti tatau asukaṃ nāma ṭhānaṃ āgaccha| idaṃ harāti itau idaṃ nāma hara| amutra idaṃ āharāti asukaṭṭhānatau  idaṃ nāma idha āhara| saṅkhaipatau pana idaṃ dūtaiyyaṃ nāma ṭhapaitvā pañ‍ca sahadhammikai ratanattayassa upakārapaṭisaṃyuttañ‍ca gihīsāsanaṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ na vaṭṭati|

20. kuhakātiādīsu tividhaina kuhanavatthunā laukaṃ kuhayanti, vimhāpayantīti kuhakā| lābhasak‍kāratthikā hutvā lapantīti lapakā| nimittaṃ sīlamaitaisanti naimittikā| nippaisau sīlamaitaisanti nippaisikā| lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīsanti  magganti pariyaisantīti lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīsitārau| kuhanā, lapanā, naimittikatā, nippaisikatā, lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīsanatāti aitāhi saman‍nāgatānaṃ puggalānaṃ aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau| vitthāraina panaitā kuhanādikā visuddhimaggai sīlaniddaisaiyaiva pāḷiñ‍ca aṭṭhakathañ‍ca āharitvā pakāsitāti|

aittāvatā majjhimasīlaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ hauti|

mahāsīlavaṇṇanā
